# II Chronicles

דברי הימים ב

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles)

חזרה על אירועים שסופרו בספרי הנביאים מימי שלמה ועד חורבן בית המקדש הראשון.

Recounts of events in the Prophets, from Solomon through the First Temple’s destruction.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 822, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 822, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 822, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### II Chronicles 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:1)


וַיִּתְחַזֵּ֛ק שְׁלֹמֹ֥ה בֶן־דָּוִ֖יד עַל־מַלְכוּת֑וֹ וַיהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהָיו֙ עִמּ֔וֹ וַֽיְגַדְּלֵ֖הוּ לְמָֽעְלָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo the son of David was strengthened in his kingdom, and the Lord his God was with him, and magnified him exceedingly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon the son of David strengthened himself over his kingdom, and the L rd his G d was with him, and He greatly exalted him.


###### II Chronicles 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:2)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה לְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֡ל לְשָׂרֵי֩ הָאֲלָפִ֨ים וְהַמֵּא֜וֹת וְלַשֹּׁפְטִ֗ים וּלְכֹ֛ל נָשִׂ֥יא לְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל רָאשֵׁ֥י הָאָבֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Shelomo spoke to all Yisra᾽el, to the captains of thousands and of hundreds, and to the judges, and to every governor in all Yisra᾽el, the heads of the fathers’ houses.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon said to all of Israel: to the officers of the thousands and the hundreds and to the judges and to every leader over all of Israel, the heads of the fathers' houses, [(to go with him to Giveon)].


###### II Chronicles 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:3)


וַיֵּלְכ֗וּ שְׁלֹמֹה֙ וְכׇל־הַקָּהָ֣ל עִמּ֔וֹ לַבָּמָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּגִבְע֑וֹן כִּֽי־שָׁ֣ם הָיָ֗ה אֹ֤הֶל מוֹעֵד֙ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֛ה מֹשֶׁ֥ה עֶבֶד־יְהֹוָ֖ה בַּמִּדְבָּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Shelomo, and all the congregation with him, went to the high place that was at Giv῾on; for there was the Tent of Meeting of God, which Moshe the servant of the Lord had made in the wilderness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon and the entire assembly went with him to the high place in Giveon. For there was the tent of meeting of G d that Moses the servant of the L rd had made in the desert.


###### II Chronicles 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:4)


אֲבָ֗ל אֲר֤וֹן הָאֱלֹהִים֙ הֶעֱלָ֤ה דָוִיד֙ מִקִּרְיַ֣ת יְעָרִ֔ים בַּהֵכִ֥ין ל֖וֹ דָּוִ֑יד כִּ֧י נָֽטָה־ל֛וֹ אֹ֖הֶל בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the ark of God David had brought up from Qiryat-ye῾arim to the place which David had prepared for it: for he had pitched a tent for it at Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the ark of G d, David had brought up from Kiryath-yearim to [the place that] David had prepared for it, having pitched a tent for it in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:5)


וּמִזְבַּ֣ח הַנְּחֹ֗שֶׁת אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשָׂה֙ בְּצַלְאֵל֙ בֶּן־אוּרִ֣י בֶן־ח֔וּר שָׁ֕ם לִפְנֵ֖י מִשְׁכַּ֣ן יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּדְרְשֵׁ֥הוּ שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה וְהַקָּהָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover the brazen altar, that Beżal᾽el the son of Uri, the son of Ḥur, had made, he put before the tabernacle of the Lord: and Shelomo and the congregation sought to it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the copper altar which Betzalel the son of Uri the son of Chur had made, he had placed before the tabernacle of the L rd, and Solomon and the assembly sought it out.


###### II Chronicles 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:6)


וַיַּ֩עַל֩ שְׁלֹמֹ֨ה שָׁ֜ם עַל־מִזְבַּ֤ח הַנְּחֹ֙שֶׁת֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר לְאֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וַיַּ֧עַל עָלָ֛יו עֹל֖וֹת אָֽלֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo went up there to the brazen altar before the Lord, which was at the Tent of Meeting, and offered a thousand burnt offerings upon it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon went up there on the copper altar before the L rd in the tent of meeting, and he offered up upon it a thousand burnt-offerings.


###### II Chronicles 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:7)


בַּלַּ֣יְלָה הַה֔וּא נִרְאָ֥ה אֱלֹהִ֖ים לִשְׁלֹמֹ֑ה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֔וֹ שְׁאַ֖ל מָ֥ה אֶתֶּן־לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In that night did God appear to Shelomo, and said to him, Ask what I shall give thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In that night G d appeared to Solomon and said to him: Ask what I shall give you.


###### II Chronicles 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:8)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר שְׁלֹמֹה֙ לֵאלֹהִ֔ים אַתָּ֗ה עָשִׂ֛יתָ עִם־דָּוִ֥יד אָבִ֖י חֶ֣סֶד גָּד֑וֹל וְהִמְלַכְתַּ֖נִי תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo said to God, Thou hast shown great steadfast love to David my father, and hast made me to reign in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon said to G d: You did great lovingkindness with David my father and made me king in his place.


###### II Chronicles 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:9)


עַתָּה֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהִ֔ים יֵֽאָמֵן֙ דְּבָ֣רְךָ֔ עִ֖ם דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֑י כִּ֤י אַתָּה֙ הִמְלַכְתַּ֔נִי עַל־עַ֕ם רַ֖ב כַּעֲפַ֥ר הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now, O Lord, let thy promise to David my father be fulfilled: for Thou hast made me king over a people like the dust of the earth in multitude.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, O L rd G d, let Your word be confirmed with David my father. For You have made me king over a great people, like the dust of the earth [in multitude].


###### II Chronicles 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:10)


עַתָּ֗ה חׇכְמָ֤ה וּמַדָּע֙ תֶּן־לִ֔י וְאֵ֥צְאָ֛ה לִפְנֵ֥י הָעָם־הַזֶּ֖ה וְאָב֑וֹאָה כִּֽי־מִ֣י יִשְׁפֹּ֔ט אֶת־עַמְּךָ֥ הַזֶּ֖ה הַגָּדֽוֹל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give me now wisdom and knowledge, that I may go out and come in before this people: for who can judge Thy people that is so great?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, give me wisdom and knowledge, so that I can go out before this people and come in. For who can judge this great people of Yours?


###### II Chronicles 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:11)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֱלֹהִ֣ים׀לִשְׁלֹמֹ֡ה יַ֣עַן אֲשֶׁר֩ הָיְתָ֨ה זֹ֜את עִם־לְבָבֶ֗ךָ וְלֹֽא־שָׁ֠אַ֠לְתָּ עֹ֣שֶׁר נְכָסִ֤ים וְכָבוֹד֙ וְאֵת֙ נֶ֣פֶשׁ שֹׂנְאֶ֔יךָ וְגַם־יָמִ֥ים רַבִּ֖ים לֹ֣א שָׁאָ֑לְתָּ וַתִּֽשְׁאַל־לְךָ֙ חׇכְמָ֣ה וּמַדָּ֔ע אֲשֶׁ֤ר תִּשְׁפּוֹט֙ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִמְלַכְתִּ֖יךָ עָלָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And God said to Shelomo, because this was in thy heart, and thou hast not asked riches, wealth, or honour, nor the life of thy enemies, nor even hast thou asked long life; but hast asked wisdom and knowledge for thyself, that thou mayst judge my people, over whom I have made thee king:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And G d said to Solomon: Because this was in your heart and you did not ask for wealth or possessions or honor or the lives of your enemies, and you did not ask either for many days, but you asked for wisdom and knowledge wherewith to judge My people over which I have made you king,


###### II Chronicles 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:12)


הַחׇכְמָ֥ה וְהַמַּדָּ֖ע נָת֣וּן לָ֑ךְ וְעֹ֨שֶׁר וּנְכָסִ֤ים וְכָבוֹד֙ אֶתֶּן־לָ֔ךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** לֹא־הָ֣יָה כֵ֗ן לַמְּלָכִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לְפָנֶ֔יךָ וְאַחֲרֶ֖יךָ לֹ֥א יִֽהְיֶה־כֵּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
wisdom and knowledge are granted to thee; and I will give thee riches, and wealth, and honour, such as none of the kings who have been before thee have had, nor shall any after thee have the like.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
wisdom and knowledge is given you; and wealth and possessions and honor I will give you, such as was not possessed by the kings before you and will not be by the kings after you.


###### II Chronicles 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:13)


וַיָּבֹ֨א שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה לַבָּמָ֤ה אֲשֶׁר־בְּגִבְעוֹן֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם מִלִּפְנֵ֖י אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֑ד וַיִּמְלֹ֖ךְ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Shelomo came to the high place that was at Giv῾on to Yerushalayim, from before the Tent of Meeting, and reigned over Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon came back from the high place in Giveon to Jerusalem, from before the tent of meeting, and he reigned over Israel.


###### II Chronicles 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:14)


וַיֶּאֱסֹ֣ף שְׁלֹמֹה֮ רֶ֣כֶב וּפָרָשִׁים֒ וַֽיְהִי־ל֗וֹ אֶ֤לֶף וְאַרְבַּע־מֵאוֹת֙ רֶ֔כֶב וּשְׁנֵים־עָשָׂ֥ר אֶ֖לֶף פָּרָשִׁ֑ים וַיַּנִּיחֵם֙ בְּעָרֵ֣י הָרֶ֔כֶב וְעִם־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo gathered chariots and horsemen: and he had one thousand four hundred chariots, and twelve thousand horsemen, whom he placed in the chariot cities, and with the king at Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon gathered chariots and riders. And he had fourteen hundred chariots and twelve thousand riders. And he quartered them in the chariot cities, and [some were] with the king [and] in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:15)


וַיִּתֵּ֨ן הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ אֶת־הַכֶּ֧סֶף וְאֶת־הַזָּהָ֛ב בִּירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם כָּאֲבָנִ֑ים וְאֵ֣ת הָאֲרָזִ֗ים נָתַ֛ן כַּשִּׁקְמִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־בַּשְּׁפֵלָ֖ה לָרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king made silver and gold to be in Yerushalayim as common as stones, and cedar trees he made as common as the sycamore trees that are in the lowland for abundance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king rendered silver and gold in Jerusalem as [common] as stones, and he rendered the cedars as [common] as the sycamores in the lowlands for abundance.


###### II Chronicles 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:16)


וּמוֹצָ֧א הַסּוּסִ֛ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִשְׁלֹמֹ֖ה מִמִּצְרָ֑יִם וּמִקְוֵ֕א סֹחֲרֵ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ מִקְוֵ֥א יִקְח֖וּ בִּמְחִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the horses that Shelomo had were exported from Miżrayim; and from Qeve the merchants of the king would collect them, from Qeve, at a set price.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the source of Solomon's horses was Egypt, and the assembly [(of Solomon's horse traders was there)]. The king's merchants would buy the assembly rights [and export privileges] for a price.


###### II Chronicles 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:17)


וַֽ֠יַּעֲל֠וּ וַיּוֹצִ֨יאוּ מִמִּצְרַ֤יִם מֶרְכָּבָה֙ בְּשֵׁ֣שׁ מֵא֣וֹת כֶּ֔סֶף וְס֖וּס בַּחֲמִשִּׁ֣ים וּמֵאָ֑ה וְ֠כֵ֠ן לְכׇל־מַלְכֵ֧י הַחִתִּ֛ים וּמַלְכֵ֥י אֲרָ֖ם בְּיָדָ֥ם יוֹצִֽיאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they fetched up, and brought forth out of Miżrayim a chariot for six hundred shekels of silver, and a horse for a hundred and fifty: and so they brought horses for all the kings of the Ḥittim, and for the kings of Aram, by their means.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They went up and took a chariot out of Egypt for six hundred [pieces of] silver, each horse [(of its four horses)] costing one hundred and fifty. And, likewise through them [(Solomon's agents)] was the [horse] traffic for all the kings of the Hittites and for the kings of Aram.


###### II Chronicles 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/1:18)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה לִבְנ֥וֹת בַּ֙יִת֙ לְשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה וּבַ֖יִת לְמַלְכוּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo determined to build a house for the name of the Lord, and a royal house for himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon resolved to build a house for the name of the L rd and a house for his kingdom.



## Chapter 2

###### II Chronicles 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:1)


וַיִּסְפֹּ֨ר שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה שִׁבְעִ֥ים אֶ֙לֶף֙ אִ֣ישׁ סַבָּ֔ל וּשְׁמוֹנִ֥ים אֶ֛לֶף אִ֖ישׁ חֹצֵ֣ב בָּהָ֑ר וּמְנַצְּחִ֣ים עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת אֲלָפִ֖ים וְשֵׁ֥שׁ מֵאֽוֹת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo counted seventy thousand men to carry burdens, and eighty thousand to hew in the mountain, and three thousand six hundred to oversee them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon counted seventy thousand porters [(to bring stones from the mountain to the city)] and eighty thousand hewers in the mountain [(all of them, "drag-along" proselytes [viz. 16])], and, overseeing them, six thousand and six hundred.


###### II Chronicles 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:2)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה אֶל־חוּרָ֥ם מֶלֶךְ־צֹ֖ר לֵאמֹ֑ר כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשִׂ֙יתָ֙ עִם־דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֔י וַתִּֽשְׁלַֽח־ל֣וֹ אֲרָזִ֔ים לִבְנֽוֹת־ל֥וֹ בַ֖יִת לָשֶׁ֥בֶת בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo sent to Ḥuram the king of Żor, saying, As thou didst deal with David my father, and didst send him cedars to build him a house to dwell in, even so deal with me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon sent to Hiram, king of Tyre, saying: As you did with David my father and sent him cedars to build for himself a house in which to dwell,


###### II Chronicles 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:3)


הִנֵּה֩ אֲנִ֨י בוֹנֶה־בַּ֜יִת לְשֵׁ֣ם**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֗י לְהַקְדִּ֣ישׁ ל֡וֹ לְהַקְטִ֣יר לְפָנָ֣יו קְטֹֽרֶת־סַמִּים֩ וּמַעֲרֶ֨כֶת תָּמִ֤יד וְעֹלוֹת֙ לַבֹּ֣קֶר וְלָעֶ֔רֶב לַשַּׁבָּתוֹת֙ וְלֶ֣חֳדָשִׁ֔ים וּֽלְמוֹעֲדֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ לְעוֹלָ֖ם זֹ֥את עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I build a house to the name of the Lord my God, to dedicate it to him, and to burn before him sweet incense, and for the continual showbread, and for the burnt offerings morning and evening, on the sabbaths, and on the new moons, and on the appointed seasons of the Lord our God. This is an ordinance for ever to Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, I am building a house for the name of the L rd my G d to devote to Him, to burn before Him spice incense and [to set before Him] perpetual showbread and [to sacrifice to Him] burnt-offerings morning and evening, for Sabbaths, for New Moons, and for the festivals of the L rd our G d, this forever [(binding)] upon Israel.


###### II Chronicles 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:4)


וְהַבַּ֛יִת אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י בוֹנֶ֖ה גָּד֑וֹל כִּֽי־גָד֥וֹל אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ מִכׇּל־הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the house which I build is great: for great is our God above all gods.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the house that I build will be great, for greater is our G d than all the gods.


###### II Chronicles 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:5)


וּמִ֤י יַֽעֲצׇר־כֹּ֙חַ֙ לִבְנֽוֹת־ל֣וֹ בַ֔יִת כִּ֧י הַשָּׁמַ֛יִם וּשְׁמֵ֥י הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם לֹ֣א יְכַלְכְּלֻ֑הוּ וּמִ֤י אֲנִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶבְנֶה־לּ֣וֹ בַ֔יִת כִּ֖י אִם־לְהַקְטִ֥יר לְפָנָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But who is able to build him a house, seeing the heaven and the heaven of heavens cannot contain him? who am I then, that I should build him a house, except as a place to burn incense before him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And who can summon up power to build Him a house? For the heavens and the heavens' heavens cannot sustain Him. And who am I to build Him a house, lest it be to offer incense before Him?


###### II Chronicles 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:6)


וְעַתָּ֡ה שְֽׁלַֽח־לִ֣י אִישׁ־חָכָ֡ם לַעֲשׂוֹת֩ בַּזָּהָ֨ב וּבַכֶּ֜סֶף וּבַנְּחֹ֣שֶׁת וּבַבַּרְזֶ֗ל וּבָֽאַרְגְּוָן֙ וְכַרְמִ֣יל וּתְכֵ֔לֶת וְיֹדֵ֖עַ לְפַתֵּ֣חַ פִּתּוּחִ֑ים עִם־הַחֲכָמִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר עִמִּי֙ בִּיהוּדָ֣ה וּבִירוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֵכִ֖ין דָּוִ֥יד אָבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Send me now therefore a man skilled for work in gold, and in silver, and in brass, and in iron, and in purple, and crimson, and blue, and who has skill to engrave with the skilful men that are with me in Yehuda and in Yerushalayim, whom David my father did provide.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, send me a wise man to work with gold, silver, copper, and iron, and with purple, crimson, and blue (yarn) and who knows how to make engravings [in stone, to work together] with the wise men who are with me in Judah and in Jerusalem, whom my father readied.


###### II Chronicles 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:7)


וּֽשְׁלַֽח־לִי֩ עֲצֵ֨י אֲרָזִ֜ים בְּרוֹשִׁ֣ים וְאַלְגּוּמִּים֮ מֵֽהַלְּבָנוֹן֒ כִּ֚י אֲנִ֣י יָדַ֔עְתִּי אֲשֶׁ֤ר עֲבָדֶ֙יךָ֙ יֽוֹדְעִ֔ים לִכְר֖וֹת עֲצֵ֣י לְבָנ֑וֹן וְהִנֵּ֥ה עֲבָדַ֖י עִם־עֲבָדֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Send me also cedar trees, cypress trees, and algum trees, out of the Levanon: for I know that thy servants know how to cut timber in the Levanon; and, behold, my servants shall be with thy servants,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And send me cedar wood, cypress wood and sandal wood from the Levanon, for I know that your servants know how to cut the trees of Levanon, and behold, my servants (are) with your servants.


###### II Chronicles 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:8)


וּלְהָכִ֥ין לִ֛י עֵצִ֖ים לָרֹ֑ב כִּ֥י הַבַּ֛יִת אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י בוֹנֶ֖ה גָּד֥וֹל וְהַפְלֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to prepare me timber in abundance: for the house which I am about to build shall be great and wonderful.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [command] to prepare for me wood in abundance, for the house that I am building (will be) great and wondrous.


###### II Chronicles 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:9)


וְהִנֵּ֣ה לַֽחֹטְבִ֣ים לְֽכֹרְתֵ֣י הָעֵצִ֡ים נָתַ֩תִּי֩ חִטִּ֨ים׀מַכּ֜וֹת לַעֲבָדֶ֗יךָ כֹּרִים֙ עֶשְׂרִ֣ים אֶ֔לֶף וּשְׂעֹרִ֕ים כֹּרִ֖ים עֶשְׂרִ֣ים אָ֑לֶף וְיַ֗יִן בַּתִּים֙ עֶשְׂרִ֣ים אֶ֔לֶף וְשֶׁ֕מֶן בַּתִּ֖ים עֶשְׂרִ֥ים אָֽלֶף׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And, behold, I will give to thy servants, the hewers who cut timber, twenty thousand measures of beaten wheat, and twenty thousand measures of barley, and twenty thousand bats of wine, and twenty thousand bats of oil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, for the chotvim, the wood cutters, I have given abundant wheat for your servants; twenty thousand kors, and barley, twenty thousand kors, and wine, twenty thousand baths, and oil, twenty thousand baths.


###### II Chronicles 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:10)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר חוּרָ֤ם מֶלֶךְ־צֹר֙ בִּכְתָ֔ב וַיִּשְׁלַ֖ח אֶל־שְׁלֹמֹ֑ה בְּאַהֲבַ֤ת יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־עַמּ֔וֹ נְתָנְךָ֥ עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מֶֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Ḥuram the king of Żor answered in a writing, which he sent to Shelomo, Seeing that the Lord has loved his people, he has made thee king over them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Hiram king of Tyre said in writing and sent (it) to Solomon: In the L rd's love for His people, He has made you king over them.


###### II Chronicles 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:11)


וַיֹּ֘אמֶר֮ חוּרָם֒ בָּר֤וּךְ יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וְאֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתַן֩ לְדָוִ֨יד הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ בֵּ֣ן חָכָ֗ם יוֹדֵ֙עַ֙ שֵׂ֣כֶל וּבִינָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִבְנֶה־בַּ֙יִת֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה וּבַ֖יִת לְמַלְכוּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Ḥuram said, Blessed is the Lord God of Yisra᾽el, who made heaven and earth, who has given to David the king a wise son, endued with prudence and understanding, who might build a house for the Lord, and a royal house for himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Hiram said: Blessed is the L rd, the G d of Israel, who made the heavens and the earth, who gave King David a wise son, knowing wisdom and understanding, who will build a house for the L rd and a house for his kingdom.


###### II Chronicles 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:12)


וְעַתָּ֗ה שָׁלַ֧חְתִּי אִישׁ־חָכָ֛ם יוֹדֵ֥עַ בִּינָ֖ה לְחוּרָ֥ם אָבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now I have sent a skilful man, endued with understanding, Ḥuram-avi,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, I have sent a wise man, knowing understanding, [(an artisan)] of Hiram, my father,


###### II Chronicles 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:13)


בֶּן־אִשָּׁ֞ה מִן־בְּנ֣וֹת דָּ֗ן וְאָבִ֣יו אִישׁ־צֹרִ֡י יוֹדֵ֡עַ לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת בַּזָּֽהָב־וּ֠בַכֶּ֠סֶף בַּנְּחֹ֨שֶׁת בַּבַּרְזֶ֜ל בָּאֲבָנִ֣ים וּבָעֵצִ֗ים בָּאַרְגָּמָ֤ן בַּתְּכֵ֙לֶת֙ וּבַבּ֣וּץ וּבַכַּרְמִ֔יל וּלְפַתֵּ֙חַ֙ כׇּל־פִּתּ֔וּחַ וְלַחְשֹׁ֖ב כׇּל־מַחֲשָׁ֑בֶת אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִנָּֽתֶן־לוֹ֙ עִם־חֲכָמֶ֔יךָ וְֽחַכְמֵ֔י אֲדֹנִ֖י דָּוִ֥יד אָבִֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the son of a woman of the daughters of Dan, and his father was a man of Żor, skilful to work in gold, and in silver, in brass, in iron, in stone, and in timber, in purple, in blue, and in fine linen, and in crimson; also to engrave any manner of engraving, and to work all kinds of artistic work; to do whatever shall be put to him, with thy skilful men, and with the skilful men of my lord David thy father.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
the son of a woman of the daughters of Dan, his father a man of Tyre, knowing how to work with gold, with silver, with copper, with stones and with wood, with purple (yarn), with blue, with fine linen and with crimson, and (knowing) how to do all (kinds of) engravings and (able) to work any work given him (together) with your wise men and the wise men of my lord, David your father.

וְ֠עַתָּ֠ה הַחִטִּ֨ים וְהַשְּׂעֹרִ֜ים הַשֶּׁ֤מֶן וְהַיַּ֙יִן֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָמַ֣ר אֲדֹנִ֔י יִשְׁלַ֖ח לַעֲבָדָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore the wheat, and the barley, the oil, and the wine, which my lord has spoken of, let him send to his servants:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, the wheat and the barley and the oil and the wine of which my lord spoke, let him send (it) to his servants.


###### II Chronicles 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:15)


וַ֠אֲנַ֠חְנוּ נִכְרֹ֨ת עֵצִ֤ים מִן־הַלְּבָנוֹן֙ כְּכׇל־צׇרְכֶּ֔ךָ וּנְבִיאֵ֥ם לְךָ֛ רַפְסֹד֖וֹת עַל־יָ֣ם יָפ֑וֹ וְאַתָּ֛ה תַּעֲלֶ֥ה אֹתָ֖ם יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and we will cut wood out of the Levanon, as much as thou shalt need: and we will bring it to thee on rafts by sea to Yafo; and thou shalt carry it up to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we will cut timbers from the Levanon, all that you need, and we will bring them to you as rafts on the sea of Yaffo, and you will bring them up to Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/2:16)


וַיִּסְפֹּ֣ר שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה כׇּל־הָאֲנָשִׁ֤ים הַגֵּירִים֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אַחֲרֵ֣י הַסְּפָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר סְפָרָ֖ם דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֑יו וַיִּמָּֽצְא֗וּ מֵאָ֤ה וַחֲמִשִּׁים֙ אֶ֔לֶף וּשְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת אֲלָפִ֖ים וְשֵׁ֥שׁ מֵאֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo numbered all the strangers who were in the land of Yisra᾽el, after the numbering wherewith David his father had numbered them; and they were found to be a hundred and fifty three thousand six hundred.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon counted all the men, the ["drag-along"] proselytes in Eretz Yisrael, after the census in which David his father had counted them, and they were found to be one hundred and fifty-three thousand and six hundred.


###### II Chronicles 2:17

וַיַּ֨עַשׂ מֵהֶ֜ם שִׁבְעִ֥ים אֶ֙לֶף֙ סַבָּ֔ל וּשְׁמֹנִ֥ים אֶ֖לֶף חֹצֵ֣ב בָּהָ֑ר וּשְׁלֹ֤שֶׁת אֲלָפִים֙ וְשֵׁ֣שׁ מֵא֔וֹת מְנַצְּחִ֖ים לְהַעֲבִ֥יד אֶת־הָעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he set seventy thousand of them to be bearers of burdens, and eighty thousand to be hewers in the mountain, and three thousand six hundred overseers to set the people to work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made of them seventy thousand porters and eighty thousand hewers in the mountain, and three thousand and six hundred overseers of the people's work.



## Chapter 3

###### II Chronicles 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:1)


וַיָּ֣חֶל שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה לִבְנ֤וֹת אֶת־בֵּית־יְהֹוָה֙ בִּיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם בְּהַר֙ הַמּ֣וֹרִיָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִרְאָ֖ה לְדָוִ֣יד אָבִ֑יהוּ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הֵכִין֙ בִּמְק֣וֹם דָּוִ֔יד בְּגֹ֖רֶן אׇרְנָ֥ן הַיְבוּסִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Shelomo began to build the house of the Lord at Yerushalayim in mount Moriyya, where the Lord appeared to David his father, in the place that David had prepared in the threshing floor of Ornan the Yevusite.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. I Kings 6, etc.] And Solomon began to build the house of the L rd in Jerusalem on Mount Moriah, where He had appeared to David, his father, viz. I Chronicles 21:26], which (house) he [(Solomon)] had prepared in David's place in the threshing floor of Arnon the Yevusite.


###### II Chronicles 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:2)


וַ֠יָּ֠חֶל לִבְנ֞וֹת בַּחֹ֤דֶשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי֙ בַּשֵּׁנִ֔י בִּשְׁנַ֥ת אַרְבַּ֖ע לְמַלְכוּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he began to build on the second day of the second month, in the fourth year of his reign.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he began to build in the second month [(Iyyar)] on the second [day] in the fourth year of his reign.


###### II Chronicles 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:3)


וְאֵ֙לֶּה֙ הוּסַ֣ד שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה לִבְנ֖וֹת אֶת־בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים הָאֹ֡רֶךְ אַמּ֞וֹת בַּמִּדָּ֤ה הָרִֽאשׁוֹנָה֙ אַמּ֣וֹת שִׁשִּׁ֔ים וְרֹ֖חַב אַמּ֥וֹת עֶשְׂרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now, these are the foundations which Shelomo laid for the building of the house of God. The length by cubits after the ancient measure was sixty cubits, and the breadth twenty cubits.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these are [(the dimensions)] which Solomon employed for the building of the house of G d: the length, in cubits, by the first measurement [(that for the mishkan, six handbreadths to the cubit)], sixty cubits; its breadth, twenty cubits.


###### II Chronicles 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:4)


וְהָאוּלָ֡ם אֲשֶׁר֩ עַל־פְּנֵ֨י הָאֹ֜רֶךְ עַל־פְּנֵ֤י רֹֽחַב־הַבַּ֙יִת֙ אַמּ֣וֹת עֶשְׂרִ֔ים וְהַגֹּ֖בַהּ מֵאָ֣ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֑ים וַיְצַפֵּ֥הוּ מִפְּנִ֖ימָה זָהָ֥ב טָהֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the porch that was in the front of the house, the length of it was according to the breadth of the house, twenty cubits, and the height was a hundred and twenty: and he overlaid it within with pure gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Ulam [(the entrance hall)], whose length ran along the breadth of the house, was twenty cubits [(from north to south)], and its height, one hundred and twenty; and he overlaid it with pure gold from within.


###### II Chronicles 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:5)


וְאֵ֣ת**׀** הַבַּ֣יִת הַגָּד֗וֹל חִפָּה֙ עֵ֣ץ בְּרוֹשִׁ֔ים וַיְחַפֵּ֖הוּ זָהָ֣ב ט֑וֹב וַיַּ֧עַל עָלָ֛יו תִּמֹרִ֖ים וְשַׁרְשְׁרֹֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the greater house he covered with cypress wood, which he overlaid with fine gold, and set on it shapes of palm trees and chains.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the great house [(the sanctuary)], he covered with cypress wood, which he covered with fine gold; and he wrought upon it (figures of) palm trees and chains.


###### II Chronicles 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:6)


וַיְצַ֧ף אֶת־הַבַּ֛יִת אֶ֥בֶן יְקָרָ֖ה לְתִפְאָ֑רֶת וְהַזָּהָ֖ב זְהַ֥ב פַּרְוָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he garnished the house with precious stones for beauty: and the gold was gold of Parvayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he overlaid the house with precious stones in splendor, and the gold was the gold of Parvayim.


###### II Chronicles 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:7)


וַיְחַ֨ף אֶת־הַבַּ֜יִת הַקֹּר֧וֹת הַסִּפִּ֛ים וְקִירוֹתָ֥יו וְדַלְתוֹתָ֖יו זָהָ֑ב וּפִתַּ֥ח כְּרוּבִ֖ים עַל־הַקִּירֽוֹת׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he overlaid the house, the beams, the posts, and its walls, and its doors, with gold; and engraved keruvim on the walls.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he covered the house — the beams, the sideposts, its walls, and its doors — with gold, and he engraved cherubim on the walls.


###### II Chronicles 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:8)


וַיַּ֙עַשׂ֙ אֶת־בֵּֽית־קֹ֣דֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁ֔ים אׇרְכּ֞וֹ עַל־פְּנֵ֤י רֹֽחַב־הַבַּ֙יִת֙ אַמּ֣וֹת עֶשְׂרִ֔ים וְרׇחְבּ֖וֹ אַמּ֣וֹת עֶשְׂרִ֑ים וַיְחַפֵּ֙הוּ֙ זָהָ֣ב ט֔וֹב לְכִכָּרִ֖ים שֵׁ֥שׁ מֵאֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made the most holy place, the length of which was according to the breadth of the house, twenty cubits, and its breadth twenty cubits: and he overlaid it with fine gold, amounting to six hundred talents.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made the house of the holy of holies, its length along the breadth of the house, twenty cubits, and its breadth, twenty cubits, and he covered it with fine gold, six hundred talents (in value).


###### II Chronicles 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:9)


וּמִשְׁקָ֛ל לְמִסְמְר֥וֹת לִשְׁקָלִ֖ים חֲמִשִּׁ֣ים זָהָ֑ב וְהָעֲלִיּ֖וֹת חִפָּ֥ה זָהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the weight of the nails was fifty shekels of gold. And he overlaid the upper chambers with gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the weight of the nails [(to clamp the gold to the wood)], in shekalim, fifty (shekels) of gold, and the upper stories he covered over with gold.


###### II Chronicles 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:10)


וַיַּ֜עַשׂ בְּבֵֽית־קֹ֤דֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים֙ כְּרוּבִ֣ים שְׁנַ֔יִם מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה צַעֲצֻעִ֑ים וַיְצַפּ֥וּ אֹתָ֖ם זָהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the most holy place he made two keruvim of figured work, and overlaid them with gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made in the holy of holies two cherubs in the form of children, and they overlaid them with gold.


###### II Chronicles 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:11)


וְכַנְפֵי֙ הַכְּרוּבִ֔ים אׇרְכָּ֖ם אַמּ֣וֹת עֶשְׂרִ֑ים כְּנַ֨ף הָאֶחָ֜ד לְאַמּ֣וֹת חָמֵ֗שׁ מַגַּ֙עַת֙ לְקִ֣יר הַבַּ֔יִת וְהַכָּנָ֤ף הָאַחֶ֙רֶת֙ אַמּ֣וֹת חָמֵ֔שׁ מַגִּ֕יעַ לִכְנַ֖ף הַכְּר֥וּב הָאַחֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the wings of the keruvim were twenty cubits long: the wing of the one was five cubits, reaching to the wall of the house: and the other wing was likewise five cubits, reaching to the wing of the other keruv.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the wings of the cherubs — their length was twenty cubits: one wing [(the outer)], five cubits (long), touching the [northern] wall of the house; and the other wing [(the inner)], five cubits (long), touching the [inner] wing of the other cherub.


###### II Chronicles 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:12)


וּכְנַ֨ף הַכְּר֤וּב הָאֶחָד֙ אַמּ֣וֹת חָמֵ֔שׁ מַגִּ֖יעַ לְקִ֣יר הַבָּ֑יִת וְהַכָּנָ֤ף הָאַחֶ֙רֶת֙ אַמּ֣וֹת חָמֵ֔שׁ דְּבֵקָ֕ה לִכְנַ֖ף הַכְּר֥וּב הָאַחֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the wing of the other keruv was five cubits, reaching to the wall of the house: and the other wing was five cubits also, joining to the wing of the other keruv.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the [outer] wing of the other cherub, five cubits (long), touching the [southern] wall of the house, and the other [(inner)] wing, five cubits (long), joined with the [inner] wing of the other cherub.


###### II Chronicles 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:13)


כַּנְפֵי֙ הַכְּרוּבִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה פֹּרְשִׂ֖ים אַמּ֣וֹת עֶשְׂרִ֑ים וְהֵ֛ם עֹמְדִ֥ים עַל־רַגְלֵיהֶ֖ם וּפְנֵיהֶ֥ם לַבָּֽיִת׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wings of these keruvim spread themselves out to twenty cubits: and they stood on their feet, and their faces were inward.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wings of these cherubs spread out for twenty cubits, and they stood on their feet, facing the house.


###### II Chronicles 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:14)


וַיַּ֙עַשׂ֙ אֶת־הַפָּרֹ֔כֶת תְּכֵ֥לֶת וְאַרְגָּמָ֖ן וְכַרְמִ֣יל וּב֑וּץ וַיַּ֥עַל עָלָ֖יו כְּרוּבִֽים׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made the veil of blue, and purple, and crimson, and fine linen, and worked keruvim on it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made the parocheth [(partition)], blue, purple, and crimson (yarn) and fine linen, and he wrought cherubs upon it.


###### II Chronicles 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:15)


וַיַּ֜עַשׂ לִפְנֵ֤י הַבַּ֙יִת֙ עַמּוּדִ֣ים שְׁנַ֔יִם אַמּ֕וֹת שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים וְחָמֵ֖שׁ אֹ֑רֶךְ וְהַצֶּ֥פֶת אֲשֶׁר־עַל־רֹאשׁ֖וֹ אַמּ֥וֹת חָמֵֽשׁ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also he made before the house two pillars of thirty five cubits high, and the capital that was on the top of each of them was five cubits.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made two pillars before the house, thirty-five cubits long. And the chapiter on the head of each, five cubits.


###### II Chronicles 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:16)


וַיַּ֤עַשׂ שַׁרְשְׁרוֹת֙ בַּדְּבִ֔יר וַיִּתֵּ֖ן עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הָעַמֻּדִ֑ים וַיַּ֤עַשׂ רִמּוֹנִים֙ מֵאָ֔ה וַיִּתֵּ֖ן בַּֽשַּׁרְשְׁרֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made chains in the inner sanctuary, and put them on the heads of the pillars; and made a hundred pomegranates, and put them on the chains.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made chain-work in the devir [(the holy of holies)], and he placed it on top of the pillars, and he made a hundred pomegranates and placed them in the chain-work.


###### II Chronicles 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/3:17)


וַיָּ֤קֶם אֶת־הָֽעַמּוּדִים֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַהֵיכָ֔ל אֶחָ֥ד מִיָּמִ֖ין וְאֶחָ֣ד מֵֽהַשְּׂמֹ֑אול וַיִּקְרָ֤א שֵׁם־[הַיְמָנִי֙] (הימיני) יָכִ֔ין וְשֵׁ֥ם הַשְּׂמָאלִ֖י בֹּֽעַז׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he set up the pillars before the temple, one on the right hand, and the other on the left; and called the name of that on the right hand Yakhin, and the name of that on the left Bo῾az.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he set up the pillars before the sanctuary, one on the right; the other, on the left. And he called the one on the right Yachin, and the one on the left, Boaz.



## Chapter 4

###### II Chronicles 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:1)


וַיַּ֙עַשׂ֙ מִזְבַּ֣ח נְחֹ֔שֶׁת עֶשְׂרִ֤ים אַמָּה֙ אׇרְכּ֔וֹ וְעֶשְׂרִ֥ים אַמָּ֖ה רׇחְבּ֑וֹ וְעֶ֥שֶׂר אַמּ֖וֹת קוֹמָתֽוֹ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made an altar of brass, twenty cubits its length, and twenty cubits its breadth, and ten cubits its height.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made a copper altar, twenty cubits long, twenty cubits broad, and ten cubits high.


###### II Chronicles 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ אֶת־הַיָּ֖ם מוּצָ֑ק עֶ֣שֶׂר בָּ֠אַמָּ֠ה מִשְּׂפָת֨וֹ אֶל־שְׂפָת֜וֹ עָג֣וֹל**׀** סָבִ֗יב וְחָמֵ֤שׁ בָּֽאַמָּה֙ קוֹמָת֔וֹ וְקָו֙ שְׁלֹשִׁ֣ים בָּאַמָּ֔ה יָסֹ֥ב אֹת֖וֹ סָבִֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also he made a molten sea of ten cubits from brim to brim, round in compass, and five cubits its height; and a line of thirty cubits did circle it round about.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made the molten Sea [(a tub for the ritual immersion of he priests)], ten cubits from brim to brim. It was round all about, and its height was five cubits. And a line of thirty cubits compassed it.


###### II Chronicles 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:3)


וּדְמ֣וּת בְּקָרִים֩ תַּ֨חַת ל֜וֹ סָבִ֤יב׀סָבִיב֙ סוֹבְבִ֣ים אֹת֔וֹ עֶ֚שֶׂר בָּֽאַמָּ֔ה מַקִּיפִ֥ים אֶת־הַיָּ֖ם סָבִ֑יב שְׁנַ֤יִם טוּרִים֙ הַבָּקָ֔ר יְצוּקִ֖ים בְּמֻצַקְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And under it was the likeness of oxen, which did circle it round about: ten in a cubit, compassing the sea round about. Two rows of oxen were cast with it, when it was cast.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the likeness of oxen was beneath it roundabout, compassing it; ten in a cubit, compassing the Sea roundabout. In two rows were the oxen cast when it [the Sea] was cast [(i.e., they were cast in one form, and not cast and superadded to it afterwards)].


###### II Chronicles 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:4)


עוֹמֵ֞ד עַל־שְׁנֵ֧ים עָשָׂ֣ר בָּקָ֗ר שְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה פֹנִ֣ים׀צָפ֡וֹנָה וּשְׁלוֹשָׁה֩ פֹנִ֨ים׀יָ֜מָּה וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה**׀** פֹּנִ֣ים נֶ֗גְבָּה וּשְׁלֹשָׁה֙ פֹּנִ֣ים מִזְרָ֔חָה וְהַיָּ֥ם עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מִלְמָ֑עְלָה וְכׇל־אֲחֹרֵיהֶ֖ם בָּֽיְתָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It stood upon twelve oxen, three looking towards the north, and three looking towards the west, and three looking towards the south, and three looking towards the east: and the sea was set above upon them, and all their hinder parts were inward.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It stood on twelve [(brazen)] oxen, three turned to the north, three turned to the west, three turned to the south, and three turned to the east. And the Sea was (set) upon them from above. And all their hind parts faced inwards [i.e., those in the north to those in the south, and those in the east to those in the west].


###### II Chronicles 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:5)


וְעׇבְי֣וֹ טֶ֔פַח וּשְׂפָתוֹ֙ כְּמַעֲשֵׂ֣ה שְׂפַת־כּ֔וֹס פֶּ֖רַח שׁוֹשַׁנָּ֑ה מַחֲזִ֣יק בַּתִּ֔ים שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת אֲלָפִ֖ים יָכִֽיל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the thickness of it was a handbreadth, and the brim of it like the work of the brim of a cup, like the petals of a lily; and it received and contained three thousand bats.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And its thickness was a handbreadth, but its rim [(was thinner)], wrought like the brim of a [drinking] cup [and decorated with] flower and lily. It contained three thousand bath [(a type of measure)].


###### II Chronicles 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:6)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ כִּיּוֹרִים֮ עֲשָׂרָה֒ וַ֠יִּתֵּ֠ן חֲמִשָּׁ֨ה מִיָּמִ֜ין וַחֲמִשָּׁ֤ה מִשְּׂמֹאול֙ לְרׇחְצָ֣ה בָהֶ֔ם אֶת־מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה הָעוֹלָ֖ה יָדִ֣יחוּ בָ֑ם וְהַיָּ֕ם לְרׇחְצָ֥ה לַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים בּֽוֹ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He made also ten lavers, and put five on the right hand, and five on the left, to wash in them: such things as belonged to the burnt offering they washed in them; but the sea was for the priests to wash in.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made ten lavers. And he placed five on the right and five on the left [for the priests] to wash their hands and feet with [(and)] in which to rinse the burnt-offering portions. And the Sea was for the priests to immerse in.


###### II Chronicles 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:7)


וַ֠יַּ֠עַשׂ אֶת־מְנֹר֧וֹת הַזָּהָ֛ב עֶ֖שֶׂר כְּמִשְׁפָּטָ֑ם וַיִּתֵּן֙ בַּֽהֵיכָ֔ל חָמֵ֥שׁ מִיָּמִ֖ין וְחָמֵ֥שׁ מִשְּׂמֹֽאול׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made ten candlesticks of gold according to their prescribed form, and set them in the temple, five on the right hand, and five on the left.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made the ten golden menoroth as ordained, and he placed (them) in the sanctuary, five to the right [(of the menorah of Moses)] and five to the left.


###### II Chronicles 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:8)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ שֻׁלְחָנוֹת֮ עֲשָׂרָה֒ וַיַּנַּח֙ בַּהֵיכָ֔ל חֲמִשָּׁ֥ה מִיָּמִ֖ין וַחֲמִשָּׁ֣ה מִשְּׂמֹ֑אול וַיַּ֛עַשׂ מִזְרְקֵ֥י זָהָ֖ב מֵאָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He made also ten tables, and placed them in the temple, five on the right side, and five on the left. And he made a hundred basins of gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made ten tables and he placed them in the sanctuary, five to the right and five to the left. And he made a hundred golden basins.


###### II Chronicles 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:9)


וַיַּ֙עַשׂ֙ חֲצַ֣ר הַכֹּהֲנִ֔ים וְהָעֲזָרָ֖ה הַגְּדוֹלָ֑ה וּדְלָת֧וֹת לָעֲזָרָ֛ה וְדַלְתוֹתֵיהֶ֖ם צִפָּ֥ה נְחֹֽשֶׁת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he made the court of the priests, and the great court, and doors for the court, and overlaid their doors with brass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he made the court of the priests and the great court [(of the Israelites)], and doors for the court. And he coated their doors with copper.


###### II Chronicles 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:10)


וְאֶת־הַיָּ֗ם נָתַ֞ן מִכֶּ֧תֶף הַיְמָנִ֛ית קֵ֖דְמָה מִמּ֥וּל נֶֽגְבָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he set the sea on the right side of the east end, over against the south.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Sea he set on the right side [of the house], on the east opposite the south [i.e., on the north-east corner].


###### II Chronicles 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:11)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ חוּרָ֔ם אֶ֨ת־הַסִּיר֔וֹת וְאֶת־הַיָּעִ֖ים וְאֶת־הַמִּזְרָק֑וֹת וַיְכַ֣ל (חירם) [חוּרָ֗ם] לַעֲשׂוֹת֙ אֶת־הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֛ה לַמֶּ֥לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה בְּבֵ֥ית הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃

And Ḥuram made the pans, and the shovels, and the basins. And Ḥuram finished the work that he was to do for king Shelomo for the house of God;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Hiram made the [copper] pots [(for removal of ashes from the altar)] and the shovels [(to rake the ashes into the pots)] and the [sprinkling] basins. And Hiram finished doing all the work which he did for King Solomon in the house of G d:


###### II Chronicles 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:12)


עַמּוּדִ֣ים שְׁנַ֔יִם וְהַגֻּלּ֧וֹת וְהַכֹּתָר֛וֹת עַל־רֹ֥אשׁ הָעַמּוּדִ֖ים שְׁתָּ֑יִם וְהַשְּׂבָכ֣וֹת שְׁתַּ֔יִם לְכַסּ֗וֹת אֶת־שְׁתֵּי֙ גֻּלּ֣וֹת הַכֹּתָר֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־רֹ֥אשׁ הָעַמּוּדִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the two pillars, and the two bowls, with the capitals that were on the top of the two pillars, and the two networks to cover the two bowls of the capitals which were on the top of the pillars;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The two pillars, and the two bowls of the chapiters on top of the pillars, and the two reticulations to cover the two bowls of the chapiters on top of the pillars.


###### II Chronicles 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:13)


וְאֶת־הָרִמּוֹנִ֛ים אַרְבַּ֥ע מֵא֖וֹת לִשְׁתֵּ֣י הַשְּׂבָכ֑וֹת שְׁנַ֨יִם טוּרִ֤ים רִמּוֹנִים֙ לַשְּׂבָכָ֣ה הָאֶחָ֔ת לְכַסּ֗וֹת אֶת־שְׁתֵּי֙ גֻּלּ֣וֹת הַכֹּתָר֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָעַמּוּדִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and four hundred pomegranates on the two wreathen chains; two rows of pomegranates on each wreath, to cover the two bowls of the capitals which were upon the pillars.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the four hundred pomegranates for the two reticulations, two rows of pomegranates for each reticulation to cover the two bowls of the chapiters on the pillars.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:14)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He made also bases, and he made lavers upon the bases;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

אֶת־הַיָּ֖ם אֶחָ֑ד וְאֶת־הַבָּקָ֥ר שְׁנֵים־עָשָׂ֖ר תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
one sea, and twelve oxen under it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(And he made) the one Sea and the twelve oxen under it.

וְאֶת־הַ֠סִּיר֠וֹת וְאֶת־הַיָּעִ֤ים וְאֶת־הַמִּזְלָגוֹת֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־כְּלֵיהֶ֔ם עָשָׂ֞ה חוּרָ֥ם אָבִ֛יו לַמֶּ֥לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה לְבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה נְחֹ֖שֶׁת מָרֽוּק׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the pots and the shovels and the forks. And all their vessels he made [as artfully as] Hiram his father for King Solomon (for) the house of the L rd, burnished copper.

In the plain of the Yarden did the king cast them, in the clay ground between Sukkot and Żeredata.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus Shelomo made all these vessels in great abundance: for the weight of the brass was not found out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon made all these vessels in great abundance. The weight of the copper was not plumbed.

וַיַּ֣עַשׂ שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַכֵּלִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְאֵת֙ מִזְבַּ֣ח הַזָּהָ֔ב וְאֶ֨ת־הַשֻּׁלְחָנ֔וֹת וַעֲלֵיהֶ֖ם לֶ֥חֶם הַפָּנִֽים׃

And Shelomo made all the vessels that belonged to the house of God, the golden altar also, and the tables upon which the showbread was set;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

and the candlesticks with their lamps, that they should burn after the prescribed form before the inner sanctuary of pure gold;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the menoroth and their lamps to kindle them as ordained, before the devir [(the Holy of Holies)], of pure gold.

וְהַפֶּ֧רַח וְהַנֵּר֛וֹת וְהַמֶּלְקַחַ֖יִם זָהָ֑ב ה֖וּא מִכְל֥וֹת זָהָֽב׃

and the flowers, and the lamps, and the tongs, of gold, and that perfect gold;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/4:22)


וְהַֽמְזַמְּר֧וֹת וְהַמִּזְרָק֛וֹת וְהַכַּפּ֥וֹת וְהַמַּחְתּ֖וֹת זָהָ֣ב סָג֑וּר וּפֶ֣תַח הַ֠בַּ֠יִת דַּלְתוֹתָ֨יו הַפְּנִימִיּ֜וֹת לְקֹ֣דֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁ֗ים וְדַלְתֵ֥י הַבַּ֛יִת לַֽהֵיכָ֖ל זָהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the snuffers, and the bowls, and the spoons, and the firepans, of pure gold: and the entry of the house, its inner doors for the most holy place, and the doors of the outer house, namely the temple, were of gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the musical instruments and the basins and the spoons [(for the frankincense)] and the fire-pans [(to remove coals from the outer to the inner altar to burn incense (he made) of pure gold. And the entrance of the house, its inner doors for the holy of holies and the doors of the house for the sanctuary, (he made) of gold.



## Chapter 5

###### II Chronicles 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:1)


וַתִּשְׁלַם֙ כׇּל־הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֥ה שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה לְבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה{ס}וַיָּבֵ֨א שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה אֶת־קׇדְשֵׁ֣י**׀** דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֗יו וְאֶת־הַכֶּ֤סֶף וְאֶת־הַזָּהָב֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־הַכֵּלִ֔ים נָתַ֕ן בְּאֹצְר֖וֹת בֵּ֥ית הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus all the work that Shelomo did for the house of the Lord was finished: and Shelomo brought in all the things that David his father had dedicated; and the silver, and the gold, and all the vessels he put in the treasures of the house of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the work that King Solomon did for the house of the L rd was finished. And Solomon brought the things that his father David had dedicated — the silver and the gold (and) the vessels — and he placed (them) in the treasuries of the house of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:2)


אָז֩ יַקְהֵ֨יל שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה אֶת־זִקְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וְאֶת־כׇּל־רָאשֵׁ֨י הַמַּטּ֜וֹת נְשִׂיאֵ֧י הָאָב֛וֹת לִבְנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אֶל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם לְֽהַעֲל֞וֹת אֶת־אֲר֧וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֛ה מֵעִ֥יר דָּוִ֖יד הִ֥יא צִיּֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Shelomo assembled the elders of Yisra᾽el, and all the heads of the tribes, the heads of the fathers’ houses of the children of Yisra᾽el, to Yerushalayim, to bring up the ark of the covenant of the Lord out of the city of David, which is Żiyyon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Solomon assembled the elders of Israel and all the heads of the tribes, the princes of the fathers [of the houses] of the children of Israel, to Jerusalem to bring up the ark of the covenant of the L rd from the city of David, which is Zion [(where David had placed it when he brought it up from the house of Oved-edom)].


###### II Chronicles 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:3)


וַיִּקָּהֲל֧וּ אֶל־הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ כׇּל־אִ֥ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בֶּחָ֑ג ה֖וּא הַחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִעִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So all the men of Yisra᾽el assembled themselves to the king on the feast, namely, that of the seventh month.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the men of Israel assembled before the king on the festival [(Succoth)] — which is the seventh month [(Tishrei, the first month being Nissan, viz. Exodus 12:2)].


###### II Chronicles 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:4)


וַיָּבֹ֕אוּ כֹּ֖ל זִקְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ הַלְוִיִּ֖ם אֶת־הָאָרֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the elders of Yisra᾽el came; and the Levites took up the ark.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the elders of Israel came and the Levites bore the ark.


###### II Chronicles 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:5)


וַיַּעֲל֤וּ אֶת־הָאָרוֹן֙ וְאֶת־אֹ֣הֶל מוֹעֵ֔ד וְאֶת־כׇּל־כְּלֵ֥י הַקֹּ֖דֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּאֹ֑הֶל הֶעֱל֣וּ אֹתָ֔ם הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים הַלְוִיִּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they brought up the ark, and the Tent of Meeting, and all the holy vessels that were in the tent, these did the priests and the Levites bring up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they brought up the ark and the tent of meeting [(of Moses, which Solomon secreted when the Temple was built)] and all the holy vessels in the tent; the priests and the Levites brought them up.


###### II Chronicles 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:6)


וְהַמֶּ֣לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה וְכׇל־עֲדַ֧ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל הַנּוֹעָדִ֥ים עָלָ֖יו לִפְנֵ֣י הָאָר֑וֹן מְזַבְּחִים֙ צֹ֣אן וּבָקָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹא־יִסָּפְר֛וּ וְלֹ֥א יִמָּנ֖וּ מֵרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king Shelomo, and all the congregation of Yisra᾽el who were assembled to him before the ark, sacrificed sheep and oxen, which could not be counted or numbered for multitude.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Solomon and all the congregation of Israel who had assembled before him (were) with him before the ark, sacrificing sheep and oxen, that could not be counted or numbered for multitude.


###### II Chronicles 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:7)


וַיָּבִ֣יאוּ הַ֠כֹּהֲנִ֠ים אֶת־אֲר֨וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֧ה אֶל־מְקוֹמ֛וֹ אֶל־דְּבִ֥יר הַבַּ֖יִת אֶל־קֹ֣דֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁ֑ים אֶל־תַּ֖חַת כַּנְפֵ֥י הַכְּרוּבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priests brought in the ark of the covenant of the Lord to its place, to the inner sanctuary of the house, to the most holy place, under the wings of the keruvim:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests brought the ark of the covenant of the L rd to its place, to the devir of the house, to the holy of holies, beneath the wings of the cherubs [(those made by Solomon, which stood on the ground [(not those on the cover of the ark)])].


###### II Chronicles 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:8)


וַיִּהְי֤וּ הַכְּרוּבִים֙ פֹּרְשִׂ֣ים כְּנָפַ֔יִם עַל־מְק֖וֹם הָאָר֑וֹן וַיְכַסּ֧וּ הַכְּרוּבִ֛ים עַל־הָאָר֥וֹן וְעַל־בַּדָּ֖יו מִלְמָֽעְלָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for the keruvim spread out their wings over the place of the ark, and the keruvim covered the ark and its poles from above.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the cherubs spread their wings in the place of the ark [(from wall to wall, viz. 6:27)], and the cherubs covered the ark and its staves [(with their wings)] from above.


###### II Chronicles 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:9)


וַֽיַּאֲרִ֘יכוּ֮ הַבַּדִּים֒ וַיֵּרָאוּ֩ רָאשֵׁ֨י הַבַּדִּ֤ים מִן־הָאָרוֹן֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַדְּבִ֔יר וְלֹ֥א יֵרָא֖וּ הַח֑וּצָה וַֽיְהִי־שָׁ֔ם עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they drew out the poles of the ark, so that the ends of the poles were visible from the ark before the sanctuary; but they were not seen outside. And there it is to this day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the staves extended, and the ends of the staves were seen from the ark before the devir [(pressing against the parocheth, the curtain before the holy of holies)], but they did not protrude [(from the parocheth)]. And they are there until this day.


###### II Chronicles 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:10)


אֵ֚ין בָּאָר֔וֹן רַ֚ק שְׁנֵ֣י הַלֻּח֔וֹת אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥ן מֹשֶׁ֖ה בְּחֹרֵ֑ב אֲשֶׁ֨ר כָּרַ֤ת יְהֹוָה֙ עִם־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּצֵאתָ֖ם מִמִּצְרָֽיִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There was nothing in the ark except the two tablets which Moshe put there at Ḥorev, when the Lord made a covenant with the children of Yisra᾽el, when they came out of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There was nothing in the ark except the two tablets which Moses had placed (there) in Chorev when the L rd made (a covenant) with the children of Israel when they went out of the land of Egypt.


###### II Chronicles 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:11)


וַיְהִ֕י בְּצֵ֥את הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים מִן־הַקֹּ֑דֶשׁ כִּ֠י כׇּל־הַכֹּהֲנִ֤ים הַֽנִּמְצְאִים֙ הִתְקַדָּ֔שׁוּ אֵ֖ין לִשְׁמ֥וֹר לְמַחְלְקֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when the priests were come out of the holy place: (for all the priests that were present had sanctified themselves, and did not then keep to their duty watches:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when the priests went out of the holy place [(after they had set the ark down there)], that all the priests found (there) sanctified themselves [(for the service)] irrespective of the divisions.


###### II Chronicles 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:12)


וְהַלְוִיִּ֣ם הַמְשֹׁרְרִ֣ים לְכֻלָּ֡ם לְאָסָ֡ף לְהֵימָ֣ן לִ֠ידֻת֠וּן וְלִבְנֵיהֶ֨ם וְלַאֲחֵיהֶ֜ם מְלֻבָּשִׁ֣ים בּ֗וּץ בִּמְצִלְתַּ֙יִם֙ וּבִנְבָלִ֣ים וְכִנֹּר֔וֹת עֹמְדִ֖ים מִזְרָ֣ח לַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וְעִמָּהֶ֤ם כֹּֽהֲנִים֙ לְמֵאָ֣ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֔ים (מחצררים) [מַחְצְרִ֖ים] בַּחֲצֹצְרֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
also the Levites who were the singers, all of them of Asaf, of Heman, of Yedutun, with their sons and their brethren, clothed in white linen, having cymbals and lutes and lyres, stood at the east end of the altar, and with them a hundred and twenty priests sounding the trumpets:)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites who sang — all of them, Asaph, Heiman, Yeduthun, and their sons and their brethren, attired in fine linen, with cymbals and with psalteries and with harps, standing east of the altar; and with them, priests, one hundred and twenty, sounding trumpets.


###### II Chronicles 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:13)


וַיְהִ֣י כְ֠אֶחָ֠ד (למחצצרים) [לַמְחַצְּרִ֨ים] וְלַמְשֹׁרְרִ֜ים לְהַשְׁמִ֣יעַ קוֹל־אֶחָ֗ד לְהַלֵּ֣ל וּלְהֹדוֹת֮ לַיהֹוָה֒ וּכְהָרִ֣ים ק֠וֹל בַּחֲצֹצְר֨וֹת וּבִמְצִלְתַּ֜יִם וּבִכְלֵ֣י הַשִּׁ֗יר וּבְהַלֵּ֤ל לַֽיהֹוָה֙ כִּ֣י ט֔וֹב כִּ֥י לְעוֹלָ֖ם חַסְדּ֑וֹ וְהַבַּ֛יִת מָלֵ֥א עָנָ֖ן בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and it came to pass when all at once the trumpeters and the singers sounded a note in unison, in praise and thanksgiving to the Lord; and when they lifted up their voice with the trumpets and cymbals and instruments of music, and praised the Lord, saying, For he is good; for his steadfast love endures for ever: that then the house, the house of the Lord, was filled with a cloud;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the trumpeters and the singers were as one, to make one sound, to praise and to thank the L rd. And when they resounded with trumpets and with cymbals and with the instruments of song, and with praise to the L rd — "for He is good; for His lovingkindness endures forever" — that the house was filled with the cloud [(indigenous to)] the house of G d.


###### II Chronicles 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/5:14)


וְלֹא־יָכְל֧וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֛ים לַעֲמ֥וֹד לְשָׁרֵ֖ת מִפְּנֵ֣י הֶעָנָ֑ן כִּֽי־מָלֵ֥א כְבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־בֵּ֥ית הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that the priests could not stand to minister by reason of the cloud: for the glory of the Lord had filled the house of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests could not stand to minister because of the cloud, for the glory of the L rd filled the house of G d.



## Chapter 6

###### II Chronicles 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:1)


אָ֖ז אָמַ֣ר שְׁלֹמֹ֑ה יְהֹוָ֣ה אָמַ֔ר לִשְׁכּ֖וֹן בָּעֲרָפֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then said Shelomo, The Lord has said that he would dwell in the thick darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then, [(when he saw the cloud)] Solomon said: [(Now I see that the Shechinah is in the house that I have built)], (for) the L rd said that He would dwell [there] in mist [(and cloud, viz. Leviticus 16:2)].


###### II Chronicles 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:2)


וַאֲנִ֛י בָּנִ֥יתִי בֵית־זְבֻ֖ל לָ֑ךְ וּמָכ֥וֹן לְשִׁבְתְּךָ֖ עוֹלָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But I have built a house of habitation for Thee, and a place for Thee to dwell in for ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I have built a house for You to inhabit and an abode for Your dwelling forever [(the mounds being banned with the Temple's initiation and the Shechinah dwelling in no other place)].


###### II Chronicles 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:3)


וַיַּסֵּ֤ב הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֶת־פָּנָ֔יו וַיְבָ֕רֶךְ אֵ֖ת כׇּל־קְהַ֣ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְכׇל־קְהַ֥ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עוֹמֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king turned his face, and blessed the whole congregation of Yisra᾽el: (and all the congregation of Yisra᾽el stood:)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king turned his face and blessed the entire congregation of the children of Israel. And the entire congregation of the children of Israel stood.


###### II Chronicles 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:4)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר בָּר֤וּךְ יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁר֙ דִּבֶּ֣ר בְּפִ֔יו אֵ֖ת דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֑י וּבְיָדָ֥יו מִלֵּ֖א לֵאמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he said, Blessed is the Lord God of Yisra᾽el, who has with his hands fulfilled that which he spoke with his mouth to my father David, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: Blessed is the L rd, the G d of Israel, who spoke with His mouth to David my father [(that He would give him a son who would build the Temple)], and fulfilled it with His [(gracious)] hand, saying [(to David)]:


###### II Chronicles 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:5)


מִן־הַיּ֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֨ר הוֹצֵ֣אתִי אֶת־עַמִּי֮ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֒יִם֒ לֹא־בָחַ֣רְתִּֽי בְעִ֗יר מִכֹּל֙ שִׁבְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לִבְנ֣וֹת בַּ֔יִת לִהְי֥וֹת שְׁמִ֖י שָׁ֑ם וְלֹא־בָחַ֣רְתִּֽי בְאִ֔ישׁ לִהְי֥וֹת נָגִ֖יד עַל־עַמִּ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Since the day that I brought my people out of the land of Miżrayim I chose no city among all the tribes of Yisra᾽el to build a house in, that my name might be there; nor did I choose any man to be a ruler over my people Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"From the day that I took out My people Israel from Egypt, I chose no city from all the tribes of Israel to build a house where My name would be, and I did not choose a man to be prince over My people Israel.


###### II Chronicles 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:6)


וָֽאֶבְחַר֙ בִּיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם לִהְי֥וֹת שְׁמִ֖י שָׁ֑ם וָאֶבְחַ֣ר בְּדָוִ֔יד לִהְי֖וֹת עַל־עַמִּ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but I have chosen Yerushalayim, that my name might be there; and have chosen David to be over my people Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But I chose Jerusalem for My name to be there, and I chose David to be over My people Israel."


###### II Chronicles 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:7)


וַיְהִ֕י עִם־לְבַ֖ב דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֑י לִבְנ֣וֹת בַּ֔יִת לְשֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now it was in the heart of David my father to build a house for the name of the Lord God of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was in the heart of David my father to build a house for the name of the L rd, the G d of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:8)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֔י יַ֗עַן אֲשֶׁ֤ר הָיָה֙ עִם־לְבָ֣בְךָ֔ לִבְנ֥וֹת בַּ֖יִת לִשְׁמִ֑י הֱֽטִיב֔וֹתָ כִּ֥י הָיָ֖ה עִם־לְבָבֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the Lord said to David my father, Since it was in thy heart to build a house for my name, thou didst well in that it was in thy heart:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to David my father: Since it was in your heart to build a house for My name, you did well that it was in your heart,


###### II Chronicles 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:9)


רַ֣ק אַתָּ֔ה לֹ֥א תִבְנֶ֖ה הַבָּ֑יִת כִּ֤י בִנְךָ֙ הַיּוֹצֵ֣א מֵחֲלָצֶ֔יךָ הוּא־יִבְנֶ֥ה הַבַּ֖יִת לִשְׁמִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet thou shalt not build the house; but thy son who shall come out of thy loins, he shall build the house for my name.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
but you shall not build the house. Rather, your son, who will issue from your loins, he will build the house for My name.


###### II Chronicles 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:10)


וַיָּ֣קֶם יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶת־דְּבָר֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֵּ֑ר וָאָק֡וּם תַּ֩חַת֩ דָּוִ֨יד אָבִ֜י וָאֵשֵׁ֣ב**׀** עַל־כִּסֵּ֣א יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כַּֽאֲשֶׁר֙ דִּבֶּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה וָאֶבְנֶ֣ה הַבַּ֔יִת לְשֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord has performed his word which he has spoken: for I am risen up in the place of David my father, and I sit on the throne of Yisra᾽el, as the Lord promised, and I have built the house for the name of the Lord God of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd fulfilled His word that He spoke, and I arose in the place of David my father, and I sat on the throne of Israel as the L rd spoke, and I built the house for the name of the L rd, the G d of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:11)


וָאָשִׂ֥ים שָׁם֙ אֶת־הָ֣אָר֔וֹן אֲשֶׁר־שָׁ֖ם בְּרִ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר כָּרַ֖ת עִם־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in it I have put the ark, in which is the covenant of the Lord, that he made with the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I made there [a place for] the ark wherein was the covenant of the L rd [i.e., the tablets of the covenant] which He had made with the children of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:12)


וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֗ד לִפְנֵי֙ מִזְבַּ֣ח יְהֹוָ֔ה נֶ֖גֶד כׇּל־קְהַ֣ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּפְרֹ֖שׂ כַּפָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he stood before the altar of the Lord in the presence of all the congregation of Yisra᾽el, and spread out his hands:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he stood before the altar of the L rd, facing all the congregation of Israel, and he spread out his hands.


###### II Chronicles 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:13)


כִּֽי־עָשָׂ֨ה שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה כִּיּ֣וֹר נְחֹ֗שֶׁת וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֘הוּ֮ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה֒ חָמֵ֨שׁ אַמּ֜וֹת אׇרְכּ֗וֹ וְחָמֵ֤שׁ אַמּוֹת֙ רׇחְבּ֔וֹ וְאַמּ֥וֹת שָׁל֖וֹשׁ קוֹמָת֑וֹ וַיַּעֲמֹ֣ד עָלָ֗יו וַיִּבְרַ֤ךְ עַל־בִּרְכָּיו֙ נֶ֚גֶד כׇּל־קְהַ֣ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וַיִּפְרֹ֥שׂ כַּפָּ֖יו הַשָּׁמָֽיְמָה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for Shelomo had made a bronze scaffold, five cubits long, and five cubits broad, and three cubits high, and had set it in the midst of the court: and upon it he stood, and kneeled down upon his knees before all the congregation of Yisra᾽el, and spread out his hands towards heaven,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(For Solomon had made a copper laver, which he had placed within the [Temple] court, five cubits long, five cubits wide, and three cubits high. And he stood upon it and knelt on his knees opposite all the congregation of Israel, and he spread out his hands to heaven.)


###### II Chronicles 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:14)


וַיֹּאמַ֗ר{פ}
יְהֹוָ֞ה אֱלֹהֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֵין־כָּמ֣וֹךָ אֱלֹהִ֔ים בַּשָּׁמַ֖יִם וּבָאָ֑רֶץ שֹׁמֵ֤ר הַבְּרִית֙ וְֽהַחֶ֔סֶד לַעֲבָדֶ֕יךָ הַהֹלְכִ֥ים לְפָנֶ֖יךָ בְּכׇל־לִבָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and said, O Lord God of Yisra᾽el, there is no God like Thee in the heaven, or on the earth; who keepest covenant and troth with Thy servants, who walk before Thee with all their hearts:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: L rd, G d of Israel, there is none like You, O G d, in the heavens and on the earth, keeper [(for fulfillment)] of the covenant and the lovingkindness [(promised)] to Your servants who walk before You with all their hearts,

Thou who hast kept with Thy servant David my father that which Thou hast promised him; and hast spoken with Thy mouth, and hast fulfilled it with Thy hand, as it is this day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who has kept for Your servant, David my father, what You spoke to him. You spoke it with Your mouth and fulfilled it with Your hand as of this day.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore, O Lord God of Yisra᾽el, keep with Thy servant David my father that which Thou didst promise him, saying, There shall never fail thee a man before me to sit upon the throne of Yisra᾽el; if only thy sons take heed to their way to walk in my Tora, as thou hast walked before me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, O L rd, G d of Israel, keep for Your servant David my father what You spoke to him, saying: There will not be cut off for you from before Me a man sitting on the throne of Israel — only if your sons watch their ways, to walk in My Torah as you walked before Me.

any prayer, any supplication which will be made by any man and by all of Your people Israel (each man knowing his plague and his pain), and he spreads out his hands to this house,
