###### II Chronicles 6:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:32)


וְגַ֣ם אֶל־הַנׇּכְרִ֗י אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹ֥א מֵעַמְּךָ֣ יִשְׂרָאֵל֮ הוּא֒ וּבָ֣א**׀** מֵאֶ֣רֶץ רְחוֹקָ֗ה לְמַ֨עַן שִׁמְךָ֤ הַגָּדוֹל֙ וְיָדְךָ֣ הַחֲזָקָ֔ה וּֽזְרוֹעֲךָ֖ הַנְּטוּיָ֑ה וּבָ֥אוּ וְהִֽתְפַּֽלְל֖וּ אֶל־הַבַּ֥יִת הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Likewise concerning the stranger who is not of Thy people Yisra᾽el, but is come from a far country for Thy great name’s sake, and Thy mighty hand, and Thy outstretched arm; if they come and pray in this house;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And also the stranger, who is not of Your people Israel, and who comes from a distant land for the sake of Your great name and strong hand and outstretched arm, who will come and pray in this house,


###### II Chronicles 6:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:33)


וְאַתָּ֞ה תִּשְׁמַ֤ע מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ מִמְּכ֣וֹן שִׁבְתֶּ֔ךָ וְעָשִׂ֕יתָ כְּכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יִקְרָ֥א אֵלֶ֖יךָ הַנׇּכְרִ֑י לְמַ֣עַן יֵדְעוּ֩ כׇל־עַמֵּ֨י הָאָ֜רֶץ אֶת־שְׁמֶ֗ךָ וּלְיִרְאָ֤ה אֹֽתְךָ֙ כְּעַמְּךָ֣ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְלָדַ֕עַת כִּֽי־שִׁמְךָ֣ נִקְרָ֔א עַל־הַבַּ֥יִת הַזֶּ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר בָּנִֽיתִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then hear from the heavens, from Thy dwelling place, and do according to all for which the stranger calls to Thee; that all peoples of the earth may know Thy name, and fear Thee, as do Thy people Yisra᾽el, and may know that this house which I have built is called by Thy name.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You will hear in heaven, the abode of Your dwelling, and You will do according to all that the stranger calls to You [(as opposed to verse 30: "and You will give to the man (an Israelite) according to all of his ways," a Jew being ready to attribute non-materialization of his prayers to his own deficiencies; a stranger, only to the non-existence of a Hearer)], so that all the peoples of the land know Your name, to fear You as (do) Your people Israel, and to know that Your name is called upon this house that I have built.


###### II Chronicles 6:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:34)


כִּֽי־יֵצֵ֨א עַמְּךָ֤ לַמִּלְחָמָה֙ עַל־אֹ֣יְבָ֔יו בַּדֶּ֖רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּשְׁלָחֵ֑ם וְהִתְפַּֽלְל֣וּ אֵלֶ֗יךָ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הָעִ֤יר הַזֹּאת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּחַ֣רְתָּ בָּ֔הּ וְהַבַּ֖יִת אֲשֶׁר־בָּנִ֥יתִי לִשְׁמֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If Thy people go out to war against their enemies by the way that Thou shalt lend them, and they pray to Thee towards this city which Thou hast chosen, and the house which I have built for Thy name;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If Your people go out to war against their enemy, whithersoever You send them, and they pray to You by way of [i.e., turning to] this city [(Jerusalem)] that You have chosen and (to) the Temple that I have built for Your name,


###### II Chronicles 6:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:35)


וְשָֽׁמַעְתָּ֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם אֶת־תְּפִלָּתָ֖ם וְאֶת־תְּחִנָּתָ֑ם וְעָשִׂ֖יתָ מִשְׁפָּטָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then hear from the heavens their prayer and their supplication, and maintain their cause.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then You will hear from heaven their prayers and their supplications, and You will do their judgment [(against their enemies)].


###### II Chronicles 6:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:36)


כִּ֣י יֶחֶטְאוּ־לָ֗ךְ כִּ֣י אֵ֤ין אָדָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־יֶחֱטָ֔א וְאָנַפְתָּ֣ בָ֔ם וּנְתַתָּ֖ם לִפְנֵ֣י אוֹיֵ֑ב וְשָׁב֧וּם שׁוֹבֵיהֶ֛ם אֶל־אֶ֥רֶץ רְחוֹקָ֖ה א֥וֹ קְרוֹבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If they sin against Thee, (for there is no man who does not sin) and Thou art angry with them, and deliver them over to the enemy, and they carry them away captives to a land far off or near;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If they sin against You (for there is no man who does not sin), and You be wroth with them and deliver them to the enemy, and their captors take them to a land far or near,


###### II Chronicles 6:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:37)


וְהֵשִׁ֙יבוּ֙ אֶל־לְבָבָ֔ם בָּאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁבּוּ־שָׁ֑ם וְשָׁ֣בוּ**׀** וְהִֽתְחַנְּנ֣וּ אֵלֶ֗יךָ בְּאֶ֤רֶץ שִׁבְיָם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר חָטָ֥אנוּ הֶעֱוִ֖ינוּ וְרָשָֽׁעְנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet if they take thought in the land where they are carried captive, and turn and pray to Thee in the land of their captivity, saying, We have sinned, we have done amiss, and have dealt wickedly;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and they return to their hearts in the land where they were taken captive, and they return and they make supplication to You in the land of their captivity, saying: "We have sinned, we have transgressed, and we have acted wickedly,"


###### II Chronicles 6:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:38)


וְשָׁ֣בוּ אֵלֶ֗יךָ בְּכׇל־לִבָּם֙ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשָׁ֔ם בְּאֶ֥רֶץ שִׁבְיָ֖ם אֲשֶׁר־שָׁב֣וּ אֹתָ֑ם וְהִֽתְפַּֽלְל֗וּ דֶּ֤רֶךְ אַרְצָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתַ֣תָּה לַאֲבוֹתָ֔ם וְהָעִיר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּחַ֔רְתָּ וְלַבַּ֖יִת אֲשֶׁר־בָּנִ֥יתִי לִשְׁמֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if they return to Thee with all their heart and with all their soul in the land of their captivity, where they have been carried away captives, and pray towards their land, which Thou didst give to their fathers, and towards the city which Thou hast chosen, and towards the house which I have built for Thy name:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and they return to You with all their heart and with all their soul in the land of their captivity, where they were taken captive, and they pray to You by way of Your land which You have given to their fathers, and the city which You have chosen, and towards the house which I have built for Your name,


###### II Chronicles 6:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:39)


וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֨ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֜יִם מִמְּכ֣וֹן שִׁבְתְּךָ֗ אֶת־תְּפִלָּתָם֙ וְאֶת־תְּחִנֹּ֣תֵיהֶ֔ם וְעָשִׂ֖יתָ מִשְׁפָּטָ֑ם וְסָלַחְתָּ֥ לְעַמְּךָ֖ אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטְאוּ־לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then hear from the heavens, from Thy dwelling place, their prayer and their supplications, and maintain their cause, and forgive Thy people who have sinned against Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then You will hear from heaven, from the abode of Your dwelling, their prayers and their supplications, and You will do their judgment. And You will forgive Your people who have sinned to You.


###### II Chronicles 6:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:40)


עַתָּ֣ה אֱלֹהַ֗י יִֽהְיוּ־נָ֤א עֵינֶ֙יךָ֙ פְּתֻח֔וֹת וְאׇזְנֶ֖יךָ קַשֻּׁב֑וֹת לִתְפִלַּ֖ת הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now, my God, let Thy eyes, I pray Thee, be open, and Thy ears attentive to the prayer that is made in this place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, my G d, may Your eyes be open and Your ears attentive to the prayer of this place.


###### II Chronicles 6:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:41)


וְעַתָּ֗ה קוּמָ֞ה יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהִים֙ לְֽנוּחֶ֔ךָ אַתָּ֖ה וַאֲר֣וֹן עֻזֶּ֑ךָ כֹּהֲנֶ֜יךָ יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהִים֙ יִלְבְּשׁ֣וּ תְשׁוּעָ֔ה וַחֲסִידֶ֖יךָ יִשְׂמְח֥וּ בַטּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore arise, O Lord God, into Thy resting place, Thou, and the ark of Thy strength: let Thy priests, O Lord God, be clothed with victory, and let thy pious ones rejoice in goodness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, arise, O L rd G d to [(repose Your Shechinah upon)] Your resting place, You and the ark of Your might. Your priests, O L rd G d, shall clothe themselves in salvation, and Your saints will rejoice in the good [(that flows from You)].


###### II Chronicles 6:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/6:42)


יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהִ֔ים אַל־תָּשֵׁ֖ב פְּנֵ֣י מְשִׁיחֶ֑ךָ זׇכְרָ֕ה לְחַֽסְדֵ֖י דָּוִ֥יד עַבְדֶּֽךָ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord God, do not turn away the face of Thy anointed: remember the faithful love of David Thy servant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L rd G d, do not turn back the face of Your anointed one. Remember the lovingkindness of David your servant.



## Chapter 7

###### II Chronicles 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:1)


וּכְכַלּ֤וֹת שְׁלֹמֹה֙ לְהִתְפַּלֵּ֔ל וְהָאֵ֗שׁ יָֽרְדָה֙ מֵֽהַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַתֹּ֥אכַל הָעֹלָ֖ה וְהַזְּבָחִ֑ים וּכְב֥וֹד יְהֹוָ֖ה מָלֵ֥א אֶת־הַבָּֽיִת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now when Shelomo had made an end of praying, the fire came down from heaven, and consumed the burnt offering and the sacrifices; and the glory of the Lord filled the house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when Solomon finished praying, fire descended from heaven and consumed the burnt-offerings and the peace-offerings, and the glory of G d filled the house.


###### II Chronicles 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:2)


וְלֹ֤א יָֽכְלוּ֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים לָב֖וֹא אֶל־בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּֽי־מָלֵ֥א כְבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priests could not enter into the house of the Lord, because the glory of the Lord had filled the Lord’s house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests could not enter the house of the L rd, for the glory of the L rd filled the house of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:3)


וְכֹ֣ל**׀** בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל רֹאִים֙ בְּרֶ֣דֶת הָאֵ֔שׁ וּכְב֥וֹד יְהֹוָ֖ה עַל־הַבָּ֑יִת וַיִּכְרְעוּ֩ אַפַּ֨יִם אַ֤רְצָה עַל־הָרִֽצְפָה֙ וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֔וּ וְהֹד֤וֹת לַֽיהֹוָה֙ כִּ֣י ט֔וֹב כִּ֥י לְעוֹלָ֖ם חַסְדּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when all the children of Yisra᾽el saw how the fire came down, and the glory of the Lord upon the house, they bowed themselves with their faces to the ground upon the pavement, and prostrated themselves, and praised the Lord, saying, For he is good; for his steadfast love endures for ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the children of Israel saw when the fire came down and the glory of the L rd was upon the house. And they knelt on the floor with their faces to the ground, and they prostrated themselves in thanks to the L rd — [saying:] "for He is good; for His lovingkindness endures forever."


###### II Chronicles 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:4)


וְהַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וְכׇל־הָעָ֑ם זֹבְחִ֥ים זֶ֖בַח לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king and all the people offered sacrifices before the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king and all the people slaughtered sacrifices before the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:5)


וַיִּזְבַּ֞ח הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה֮ אֶת־זֶ֣בַח הַבָּקָר֒ עֶשְׂרִ֤ים וּשְׁנַ֙יִם֙ אֶ֔לֶף וְצֹ֕אן מֵאָ֥ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֖ים אָ֑לֶף וַֽיַּחְנְכוּ֙ אֶת־בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וְכׇל־הָעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king Shelomo offered as a sacrifice twenty two thousand oxen, and a hundred and twenty thousand sheep: so the king and all the people consecrated the house of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Solomon slaughtered the sacrifice of cattle, twenty-two thousand, and sheep, one hundred and twenty thousand; and they inaugurated the house of G d, the king and all the people.


###### II Chronicles 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:6)


וְהַכֹּהֲנִ֞ים עַל־מִשְׁמְרוֹתָ֣ם עֹמְדִ֗ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֞ם בִּכְלֵי־שִׁ֤יר יְהֹוָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֜ה דָּוִ֣יד הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ לְהֹד֤וֹת לַֽיהֹוָה֙ כִּֽי־לְעוֹלָ֣ם חַסְדּ֔וֹ בְּהַלֵּ֥ל דָּוִ֖יד בְּיָדָ֑ם וְהַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ (מחצצרים) [מַחְצְרִ֣ים] נֶגְדָּ֔ם וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עֹמְדִֽים׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priests stood at their posts: the Levites also with the instruments of music of the Lord, which David the king had made to praise the Lord, because his steadfast love endures for ever; with the praises of David in their hands; and the priests sounded trumpets before them, and all Yisra᾽el stood.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests were standing on their watches, and the Levites with the instruments of song of the L rd, which King David had made to give thanks to the L rd, "for His lovingkindness endures forever," with the [(instruments of)] praise of the L rd in their hand, and the priests, sounding the trumpets opposite them, and all of Israel standing.


###### II Chronicles 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:7)


וַיְקַדֵּ֣שׁ שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה אֶת־תּ֤וֹךְ הֶחָצֵר֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ לִפְנֵ֣י בֵית־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּי־עָ֤שָׂה שָׁם֙ הָעֹל֔וֹת וְאֵ֖ת חֶלְבֵ֣י הַשְּׁלָמִ֑ים כִּֽי־מִזְבַּ֤ח הַנְּחֹ֙שֶׁת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֣ה שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה לֹ֣א יָכ֗וֹל לְהָכִ֛יל אֶת־הָעֹלָ֥ה וְאֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֖ה וְאֶת־הַחֲלָבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover Shelomo hallowed the middle of the court that was before the house of the Lord: for there he offered burnt offerings, and the fat of the peace offerings, because the brazen altar which Shelomo had made was not able to contain the burnt offerings, and the meal offerings, and the fat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon consecrated the midst of the court before the house of the L rd. For he offered there the burnt-offerings and the fats of the peace-offerings. For [(the stone altar made by Solomon in place of)] the copper altar [(made by Moses)], that was before the L rd, could not contain the burnt-offerings and the meal-offerings and the fats.


###### II Chronicles 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:8)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה אֶת־הֶ֠חָ֠ג בָּעֵ֨ת הַהִ֜יא שִׁבְעַ֤ת יָמִים֙ וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל עִמּ֔וֹ קָהָ֖ל גָּד֣וֹל מְאֹ֑ד מִלְּב֥וֹא חֲמָ֖ת עַד־נַ֥חַל מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also at that time Shelomo kept the feast for seven days, and all Yisra᾽el with him, a very great congregation, from the entrance of Ḥamat to the wadi of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon made the festival [(Succoth)] at that time, seven days, and all of Israel with him, a very great throng, from before Levo-chamath until Nachal Mitzrayim.


###### II Chronicles 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:9)


וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֛וּ בַּיּ֥וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֖י עֲצָ֑רֶת כִּ֣י**׀** חֲנֻכַּ֣ת הַמִּזְבֵּ֗חַ עָשׂוּ֙ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֔ים וְהֶחָ֖ג שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the eighth day they made a solemn assembly: for they kept the consecration of the altar for seven days, and the feast for seven days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the eighth day they made a convocation [(Shemini Atzereth)]. For the inauguration of the altar they made seven days, and the festival [(Succoth)] seven days [(whence it is seen that they ate and drank on Yom Kippur)].


###### II Chronicles 7:10

וּבְי֨וֹם עֶשְׂרִ֤ים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה֙ לַחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֔י שִׁלַּ֥ח אֶת־הָעָ֖ם לְאׇהֳלֵיהֶ֑ם שְׂמֵחִים֙ וְט֣וֹבֵי לֵ֔ב עַל־הַטּוֹבָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ לְדָוִ֣יד וְלִשְׁלֹמֹ֔ה וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עַמּֽוֹ׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(the next day)], the twenty-third day of the seventh month, he dismissed the people to their tents, rejoicing and glad of heart over the good that the L rd had wrought for David and for Solomon and for Israel, His people.

וַיְכַ֧ל שְׁלֹמֹ֛ה אֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָ֖ה וְאֶת־בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְאֵ֨ת כׇּל־הַבָּ֜א עַל־לֵ֣ב שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה לַעֲשׂ֧וֹת בְּבֵית־יְהֹוָ֛ה וּבְבֵית֖וֹ הִצְלִֽיחַ׃{פ}

Thus Shelomo finished the house of the Lord, and the king’s house: and all that came into Shelomo’s heart to make in the house of the Lord, and in his own house, he carried out successfully.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon finished (building) the house of the L rd and the house of the king. And (in) all that it had entered Solomon's heart to do for the house of the L rd and for his house, he prospered.

וַיֵּרָ֧א יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה בַּלָּ֑יְלָה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֗וֹ שָׁמַ֙עְתִּי֙ אֶת־תְּפִלָּתֶ֔ךָ וּבָחַ֜רְתִּי בַּמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֛ה לִ֖י לְבֵ֥ית זָֽבַח׃

And the Lord appeared to Shelomo by night, and said to him, I have heard thy prayer, and have chosen this place to myself for a house of sacrifice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

If I shut up the heaven so that there is no rain, or if I command the grasshopper to devour the land, or if I send pestilence among my people;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I hold back the heavens and there be no rain, or if I command the locust to consume the land, or if I send pestilence upon My people —


###### II Chronicles 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:14)


וְיִכָּנְע֨וּ עַמִּ֜י אֲשֶׁ֧ר נִֽקְרָא־שְׁמִ֣י עֲלֵיהֶ֗ם וְיִֽתְפַּֽלְלוּ֙ וִיבַקְשׁ֣וּ פָנַ֔י וְיָשֻׁ֖בוּ מִדַּרְכֵיהֶ֣ם הָרָעִ֑ים וַֽאֲנִי֙ אֶשְׁמַ֣ע מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְאֶסְלַח֙ לְחַטָּאתָ֔ם וְאֶרְפָּ֖א אֶת־אַרְצָֽם׃

if my people, who are called by my name, shall humble themselves, and pray, and seek my face, and turn from their wicked ways; then I will hear from heaven, and I will forgive their sin, and I will heal their land.

If My people, who are called by My name, humble themselves and pray and seek My face and turn back from their evil ways — then I shall hear from the heavens, and I will forgive their sins, and I will heal their land.

Now my eyes shall be open and my ears be attentive to the prayer that is made in this place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, My eyes will be open, and My ears attentive, to the prayer of this place.

For now I have chosen and sanctified this house, that my name may be there for ever: and my eyes and my heart shall be there perpetually.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, if you walk before Me as David your father did, to do according to all that I have commanded you, keeping My statutes and My judgments,

וַהֲקִ֣ימוֹתִ֔י אֵ֖ת כִּסֵּ֣א מַלְכוּתֶ֑ךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר כָּרַ֗תִּי לְדָוִ֤יד אָבִ֙יךָ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹֽא־יִכָּרֵ֤ת לְךָ֙ אִ֔ישׁ מוֹשֵׁ֖ל בְּיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃

then I will establish the throne of thy kingdom, as I have covenanted with David thy father, saying, There shall not fail thee a man to be ruler in Yisra᾽el.

then I will establish the throne of your kingdom as I covenanted with David your father, saying: No man will be cut off from you, ruling over Israel.

וְאִם־תְּשׁוּב֣וּן אַתֶּ֔ם וַֽעֲזַבְתֶּם֙ חֻקּוֹתַ֣י וּמִצְוֺתַ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לִפְנֵיכֶ֑ם וַהֲלַכְתֶּ֗ם וַֽעֲבַדְתֶּם֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוִיתֶ֖ם לָהֶֽם׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But if you turn away and forsake My statutes and My mitzvoth that I set before you, and you go and serve other gods and bow down to them,


###### II Chronicles 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:20)


וּנְתַשְׁתִּ֗ים מֵעַ֤ל אַדְמָתִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתַ֣תִּי לָהֶ֔ם וְאֶת־הַבַּ֤יִת הַזֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִקְדַּ֣שְׁתִּי לִשְׁמִ֔י אַשְׁלִ֖יךְ מֵעַ֣ל פָּנָ֑י וְאֶתְּנֶ֛נּוּ לְמָשָׁ֥ל וְלִשְׁנִינָ֖ה בְּכׇל־הָעַמִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then I will pluck them up from my land which I have given them; and this house, which I have sanctified for my name, I will cast out of my sight, and will make it to be a proverb and a byword among all nations.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then I will uproot them from My land that I have given them, and I will cast away from My face the house which I consecrated to My name [viz. Leviticus 26:31], and I will make it a proverb and a byword [(for derision)] among all the peoples.


###### II Chronicles 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:21)


וְהַבַּ֤יִת הַזֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה עֶלְי֔וֹן לְכׇל־עֹבֵ֥ר עָלָ֖יו יִשֹּׁ֑ם וְאָמַ֗ר בַּמֶּ֨ה עָשָׂ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ כָּ֔כָה לָאָ֥רֶץ הַזֹּ֖את וְלַבַּ֥יִת הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this house, which is high, shall be an astonishment to every one who passes by it; so that he shall say, Why has the Lord done thus to this land, and to this house?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this house, which had been exalted [(even among the nations)], all who pass before it will wonder [(in amazement)] and will say: Why did the L rd do this to this land and to this house?


###### II Chronicles 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/7:22)


וְאָמְר֗וּ עַל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָזְב֜וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה**׀** אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֹתֵיהֶ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר הוֹצִיאָם֮ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֒יִם֒ וַֽיַּחֲזִ֙יקוּ֙ בֵּאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲו֥וּ לָהֶ֖ם וַיַּעַבְד֑וּם עַל־כֵּן֙ הֵבִ֣יא עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הָרָעָ֖ה הַזֹּֽאת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then they will say, Because they forsook the Lord God of their fathers, who brought them out of the land of Miżrayim, and laid hold of other gods, and worshipped them, and served them: therefore he brought all this evil upon them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they will say: It is because they forsook the L rd, the G d of their fathers, who took them out of the land of Egypt, and they embraced other gods and bowed down to them and served them. It is for this that the L rd brought down upon them all of this evil.



## Chapter 8

###### II Chronicles 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:1)


וַיְהִ֞י מִקֵּ֣ץ**׀** עֶשְׂרִ֣ים שָׁנָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר בָּנָ֧ה שְׁלֹמֹ֛ה אֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָ֖ה וְאֶת־בֵּיתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass at the end of twenty years, during which Shelomo had built the house of the Lord, and his own house,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, at the end of twenty years of Solomon's building the house of the L rd [(seven years)] and his house [(thirteen years)],


###### II Chronicles 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:2)


וְהֶעָרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֤ן חוּרָם֙ לִשְׁלֹמֹ֔ה בָּנָ֥ה שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה אֹתָ֑ם וַיּ֥וֹשֶׁב שָׁ֖ם אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that the cities which Ḥuram had given Shelomo, Shelomo built them, and caused the children of Yisra᾽el to dwell there.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that Solomon built the cities that Hiram had given to Solomon, and he settled the children of Israel there.


###### II Chronicles 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:3)


וַיֵּ֤לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה֙ חֲמָ֣ת צוֹבָ֔ה וַיֶּחֱזַ֖ק עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo went to Ḥamat-żova, and prevailed against it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon went to Chamath-tzovah and he subdued it.


###### II Chronicles 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:4)


וַיִּ֥בֶן אֶת־תַּדְמֹ֖ר בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־עָרֵ֣י הַֽמִּסְכְּנ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר בָּנָ֖ה בַּחֲמָֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he built Tadmor in the wilderness, and all the store cities, which he built in Ḥamat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he built Tadmor in the desert and all the treasure cities that he had built in Chamath.


###### II Chronicles 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:5)


וַיִּ֜בֶן אֶת־בֵּ֤ית חוֹרוֹן֙ הָעֶלְי֔וֹן וְאֶת־בֵּ֥ית חוֹר֖וֹן הַתַּחְתּ֑וֹן עָרֵ֣י מָצ֔וֹר חוֹמ֖וֹת דְּלָתַ֥יִם וּבְרִֽיחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he built the upper Bet-ĥoron, and the lower Bet-ĥoron, fortified cities, with walls, gates, and bars;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he built upper Beth-choron and lower Beth-choron, fortified cities, with walls, doors and bars.


###### II Chronicles 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:6)


וְאֶֽת־בַּעֲלָ֗ת וְאֵ֨ת כׇּל־עָרֵ֤י הַֽמִּסְכְּנוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָי֣וּ לִשְׁלֹמֹ֔ה וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־עָרֵ֣י הָרֶ֔כֶב וְאֵ֖ת עָרֵ֣י הַפָּרָשִׁ֑ים וְאֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־חֵ֣שֶׁק שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר חָשַׁק֙ לִבְנ֤וֹת בִּירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ וּבַלְּבָנ֔וֹן וּבְכֹ֖ל אֶ֥רֶץ מֶמְשַׁלְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Ba῾alat, and all the store cities that Shelomo had, and all the chariot cities, and the cities of the horsemen, and all that Shelomo desired to build in Yerushalayim, and in the Levanon, and throughout all the land of his dominion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And (he built) Ba'alath and all the treasure cities that Solomon had, and all the cities of the chariots and the cities of the riders, and (in fulfillment of) all the desire of Solomon, what he desired to build in Jerusalem and in the Levanon and in all the land of his kingdom.


###### II Chronicles 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:7)


כׇּל־הָ֠עָ֠ם הַנּוֹתָ֨ר מִן־הַחִתִּ֜י וְהָאֱמֹרִ֤י וְהַפְּרִזִּי֙ וְהַחִוִּ֣י וְהַיְבוּסִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹ֥א מִיִּשְׂרָאֵ֖ל הֵֽמָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for all the people who were left of the Ḥitti, and the Emori, and the Perizzi, and the Ḥivvi, and the Yevusi, who were not of Yisra᾽el;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(the "labor levy" of Solomon for the building of the Temple:)] All the people remaining of the Chitti, the Emori, the Perizi, the Chivi, and the Yevussi, who were not of Israel,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:8)


מִן־בְּנֵיהֶ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר נוֹתְר֤וּ אַֽחֲרֵיהֶם֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־כִלּ֖וּם בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיַּעֲלֵ֤ם שְׁלֹמֹה֙ לְמַ֔ס עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
of their children, who were left after them in the land, whom the children of Yisra᾽el did not wipe out, Shelomo raised a levy of them until this day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
of their children who remained after them in the land, whom the children of Israel did not destroy. And Solomon raised them as a labor tribute until this day.


###### II Chronicles 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:9)


וּמִן־בְּנֵי֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹא־נָתַ֧ן שְׁלֹמֹ֛ה לַעֲבָדִ֖ים לִמְלַאכְתּ֑וֹ כִּי־הֵ֜מָּה אַנְשֵׁ֤י מִלְחָמָה֙ וְשָׂרֵ֣י שָׁלִישָׁ֔יו וְשָׂרֵ֥י רִכְבּ֖וֹ וּפָרָשָֽׁיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But of the children of Yisra᾽el of whom Shelomo did not make servants for his work, (for they were men of war, and chiefs of his captains, and captains of his chariots and horsemen,)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from the children of Israel Solomon did not raise a labor (tribute), but they were the men of war and his commanding officers and the officers of his chariots and his riders.


###### II Chronicles 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:10)


וְאֵ֨לֶּה שָׂרֵ֧י (הנציבים) [הַנִּצָּבִ֛ים] אֲשֶׁר־לַמֶּ֥לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה חֲמִשִּׁ֣ים וּמָאתָ֑יִם הָרֹדִ֖ים בָּעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
even of these were the chiefs of king Shelomo’s officers, two hundred and fifty, who bore rule over the people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these [i.e., among the above] were King Solomon's supervisors, two hundred and fifty who oversaw the people.


###### II Chronicles 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:11)


וְאֶת־בַּת־פַּרְעֹ֗ה הֶעֱלָ֤ה שְׁלֹמֹה֙ מֵעִ֣יר דָּוִ֔יד לַבַּ֖יִת אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּנָה־לָ֑הּ כִּ֣י אָמַ֗ר לֹא־תֵשֵׁ֨ב אִשָּׁ֥ה לִי֙ בְּבֵית֙ דָּוִ֣יד מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּֽי־קֹ֣דֶשׁ הֵ֔מָּה אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֥אָֽה־אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם אֲר֥וֹן יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo brought up the daughter of Par῾o out of the city of David to the house that he had built for her: for he said, My wife shall not dwell in the house of David, king of Yisra᾽el, because the places to which the ark of the Lord has come are holy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon brought up the daughter of Pharaoh from the city of David to the house that he had built for her, saying: A wife of mine shall not dwell in the house of David king of Israel, for they [(i.e., that complex of houses)] are holy, the ark of the L rd having come to them [i.e., to a house in their midst.]


###### II Chronicles 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:12)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Shelomo offered burnt offerings to the Lord, on the altar of the Lord, which he had built before the porch,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Solomon sacrificed burnt-offerings to the L rd on the altar of the L rd that he had built before the Ulam.


###### II Chronicles 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:13)


וּבִדְבַר־י֣וֹם בְּי֗וֹם לְהַֽעֲלוֹת֙ כְּמִצְוַ֣ת מֹשֶׁ֔ה לַשַּׁבָּתוֹת֙ וְלֶ֣חֳדָשִׁ֔ים וְלַמּ֣וֹעֲד֔וֹת שָׁל֥וֹשׁ פְּעָמִ֖ים בַּשָּׁנָ֑ה בְּחַ֧ג הַמַּצּ֛וֹת וּבְחַ֥ג הַשָּׁבֻע֖וֹת וּבְחַ֥ג הַסֻּכּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
after a certain rate every day, offering according to the commandment of Moshe, on the sabbaths, and on the new moons, and on the appointed seasons, three times in the year, on the feast of unleavened bread, and on the feast of weeks, and on the feast of booths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(sacrificed there was)] the thing [i.e., the offering] of the day in its day, to sacrifice (it), as ordained by Moses, on Sabbaths and on New Moons and on festivals, three times a year: on the festival of matzoth, on the festival of Shavuoth, and on the festival of Succoth.

וַיַּעֲמֵ֣ד כְּמִשְׁפַּ֣ט דָּֽוִיד־אָ֠בִ֠יו אֶת־מַחְלְק֨וֹת הַכֹּהֲנִ֜ים עַל־עֲבֹדָתָ֗ם וְהַלְוִיִּ֣ם עַל־מִ֠שְׁמְרוֹתָ֠ם לְהַלֵּ֨ל וּלְשָׁרֵ֜ת נֶ֤גֶד הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ לִדְבַר־י֣וֹם בְּיוֹמ֔וֹ וְהַשֹּׁעֲרִ֥ים בְּמַחְלְקוֹתָ֖ם לְשַׁ֣עַר וָשָׁ֑עַר כִּ֣י כֵ֔ן מִצְוַ֖ת דָּוִ֥יד אִישׁ־הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃

And according to the form prescribed by David his father, he set the divisions of the priests to their service, and the Levites to their charges, to praise and minister before the priests, as the duty of every day required: the gatekeepers also by their divisions at every gate: for so had David the man of God commanded.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he set up, according to the ordinance of David his father the divisions of the priests in their service and the Levites in their watches to praise (the L rd) and to minister alongside the priests, the (service of the) day in its day, and the gatekeepers according to their divisions for each gate. For thus was it ordained by David, the man of G d.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they did not depart from the commandment of the king to the priests and Levites concerning any matter, or concerning the treasures.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they did not deviate from the charge of the king to the priests and to the Levites, in any matter (of service) and for (overseeing) the treasuries.

So all the work of Shelomo was accomplished from the day of the foundation of the house of the Lord, until it was finished. So the house of the Lord was completed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the work of Solomon was perfectly executed, from the founding of the house of the L rd until its completion — the house of the L rd was perfect.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/8:17)


אָז֩ הָלַ֨ךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה לְעֶצְיֽוֹן־גֶּ֧בֶר וְאֶל־אֵיל֛וֹת עַל־שְׂפַ֥ת הַיָּ֖ם בְּאֶ֥רֶץ אֱדֽוֹם׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיִּֽשְׁלַֽח־לוֹ֩ חוּרָ֨ם בְּיַד־עֲבָדָ֜יו (אוניות) [אֳנִיּ֗וֹת] וַעֲבָדִים֮ י֣וֹדְעֵי יָם֒ וַיָּבֹ֜אוּ עִם־עַבְדֵ֤י שְׁלֹמֹה֙ אוֹפִ֔ירָה וַיִּקְח֣וּ מִשָּׁ֔ם אַרְבַּע־מֵא֥וֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁ֖ים כִּכַּ֣ר זָהָ֑ב וַיָּבִ֖יאוּ אֶל־הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Ḥuram sent him by the hands of his servants ships, and servants that had knowledge of the sea; and they went with the servants of Shelomo to Ofir, and took from there four hundred and fifty talents of gold, and brought them to king Shelomo.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Hiram sent him by his servants, ships, and servants knowing the sea. And they came with the servants of Solomon to Ophir. And they took from there four hundred and fifty talents of gold, and they brought (it) to King Solomon.



## Chapter 9

###### II Chronicles 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:1)


וּמַֽלְכַּת־שְׁבָ֗א שָׁמְעָה֮ אֶת־שֵׁ֣מַע שְׁלֹמֹה֒ וַתָּב֣וֹא לְנַסּוֹת֩ אֶת־שְׁלֹמֹ֨ה בְחִיד֜וֹת בִּירוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם בְּחַ֣יִל כָּבֵ֣ד מְאֹ֡ד וּ֠גְמַלִּ֠ים נֹשְׂאִ֨ים בְּשָׂמִ֧ים וְזָהָ֛ב לָרֹ֖ב וְאֶ֣בֶן יְקָרָ֑ה וַתָּבוֹא֙ אֶל־שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה וַתְּדַבֵּ֣ר עִמּ֔וֹ אֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָיָ֖ה עִם־לְבָבָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when the queen of Sheva heard of the fame of Shelomo, she came to prove Shelomo with hard questions at Yerushalayim, with a very great retinue, and camels that carried spices, and gold in abundance, and precious stones: and when she was come to Shelomo, she spoke to him of all that was in her heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the queen of Sheva heard of the renown of Solomon for the name of the L rd, and she came to test Solomon with riddles, in Jerusalem with a very great train and camels bearing spices and very much gold and precious stones. And she came to Solomon and she spoke to him of all that was in her heart.


###### II Chronicles 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:2)


וַיַּגֶּד־לָ֥הּ שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה אֶת־כׇּל־דְּבָרֶ֑יהָ וְלֹֽא־נֶעְלַ֤ם דָּבָר֙ מִשְּׁלֹמֹ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹ֦א הִגִּ֖יד לָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo answered her all her questions: and there was nothing hidden from Shelomo which he did not tell her.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon told her all of her things [(which she wished to know)]. There was nothing concealed from Solomon that he did not tell her.


###### II Chronicles 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:3)


וַתֵּ֙רֶא֙ מַֽלְכַּת־שְׁבָ֔א אֵ֖ת חׇכְמַ֣ת שְׁלֹמֹ֑ה וְהַבַּ֖יִת אֲשֶׁ֥ר בָּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when the queen of Sheva had seen the wisdom of Shelomo, and the house that he had built,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the queen of Sheva saw Solomon's wisdom and the house that he had built.


###### II Chronicles 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:4)


וּמַאֲכַ֣ל שֻׁלְחָנ֡וֹ וּמוֹשַׁ֣ב עֲבָדָיו֩ וּמַעֲמַ֨ד מְשָׁרְתָ֜יו וּמַלְבּוּשֵׁיהֶ֗ם וּמַשְׁקָיו֙ וּמַלְבּ֣וּשֵׁיהֶ֔ם וַֽעֲלִיָּת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַעֲלֶ֖ה בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹא־הָ֥יָה ע֛וֹד בָּ֖הּ רֽוּחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the food at his table, and the sitting of his servants, and the attendance of his ministers, and their apparel; his cupbearers also, and their apparel; and his ascent by which he went up into the house of the Lord; there was no more spirit in her.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the food of his table and the sitting of his servants and the standing of his ministers and their attire, and his cupbearers and their attire, and his ascent [(a passageway from his house)] by which he would go up to the house of the L rd, and she could no longer contain herself.


###### II Chronicles 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:5)


וַתֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אֱמֶת֙ הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁמַ֖עְתִּי בְּאַרְצִ֑י עַל־דְּבָרֶ֖יךָ וְעַל־חׇכְמָתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And she said to the king, It was a true report which I heard in my own land of thy acts, and of thy wisdom:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And she said to the king: True was the thing that I heard in my land about your deeds and about your wisdom.


###### II Chronicles 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:6)


וְלֹא־הֶאֱמַ֣נְתִּי לְדִבְרֵיהֶ֗ם עַ֤ד אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֙אתִי֙ וַתִּרְאֶ֣ינָה עֵינַ֔י וְהִנֵּה֙ לֹ֣א הֻגַּד־לִ֔י חֲצִ֖י מַרְבִּ֣ית חׇכְמָתֶ֑ךָ יָסַ֕פְתָּ עַל־הַשְּׁמוּעָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁמָֽעְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but I did not believe their words, until I came, and my eyes had seen it: and, behold, the one half of the greatness of thy wisdom was not told me: for thou dost exceed the fame that I heard.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I did not believe the things until I came and my eyes saw. And, behold, the half the abundance of your wisdom was not told me. You have added to the report that I heard.


###### II Chronicles 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:7)


אַשְׁרֵ֣י אֲנָשֶׁ֔יךָ וְאַשְׁרֵ֖י עֲבָדֶ֣יךָ אֵ֑לֶּה הָעֹמְדִ֤ים לְפָנֶ֙יךָ֙ תָּמִ֔יד וְשֹׁמְעִ֖ים אֶת־חׇכְמָתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Happy are thy men, and happy are these thy servants, who stand continually before thee, and hear thy wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fortunate are your men and fortunate are these servants of yours who stand before you always and hear your wisdom.


###### II Chronicles 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:8)


יְהִ֨י יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ בָּר֔וּךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** חָפֵ֣ץ בְּךָ֗ לְתִתְּךָ֤ עַל־כִּסְאוֹ֙ לְמֶ֔לֶךְ לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ בְּאַהֲבַ֨ת אֱלֹהֶ֤יךָ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לְהַעֲמִיד֣וֹ לְעוֹלָ֔ם וַיִּתֶּנְךָ֤ עֲלֵיהֶם֙ לְמֶ֔לֶךְ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וּצְדָקָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessed be the Lord thy God, who delighted in thee to set thee on his throne, to be king for the Lord thy God: because thy God loved Yisra᾽el, to establish them for ever, therefore he made thee king over them, to do judgment and justice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed be the L rd your G d who desired you, to place you on His throne as king for the L rd your G d in the love of your G d for Israel to establish them forever. And He made you a king over them to do justice and righteousness.


###### II Chronicles 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:9)


וַתִּתֵּ֨ן לַמֶּ֜לֶךְ מֵאָ֥ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֣ים**׀** כִּכַּ֣ר זָהָ֗ב וּבְשָׂמִ֛ים לָרֹ֥ב מְאֹ֖ד וְאֶ֣בֶן יְקָרָ֑ה וְלֹ֤א הָיָה֙ כַּבֹּ֣שֶׂם הַה֔וּא אֲשֶׁר־נָתְנָ֥ה מַֽלְכַּת־שְׁבָ֖א לַמֶּ֥לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And she gave the king a hundred and twenty talents of gold, and of spices great abundance, and precious stones: nor was there any such spice as the queen of Sheva gave king Shelomo.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And she gave the king one hundred and twenty talents of gold, and very much spices and precious stones. There was not the like of those spices that the queen of Sheva gave to King Solomon.


###### II Chronicles 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:10)


וְגַם־עַבְדֵ֤י (חירם) [חוּרָם֙] וְעַבְדֵ֣י שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הֵבִ֥יאוּ זָהָ֖ב מֵאוֹפִ֑יר הֵבִ֛יאוּ עֲצֵ֥י אַלְגּוּמִּ֖ים וְאֶ֥בֶן יְקָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the servants also of Ḥuram, and the servants of Shelomo, who brought gold from Ofir, brought algum trees and precious stones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And also the servants of Hiram and the servants of Solomon, who brought gold from Ophir, brought coral-wood and precious stones.


###### II Chronicles 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:11)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ הַ֠מֶּ֠לֶךְ אֶת־עֲצֵ֨י הָאַלְגּוּמִּ֜ים מְסִלּ֤וֹת לְבֵית־יְהֹוָה֙ וּלְבֵ֣ית הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְכִנֹּר֥וֹת וּנְבָלִ֖ים לַשָּׁרִ֑ים וְלֹא־נִרְא֥וּ כָהֵ֛ם לְפָנִ֖ים בְּאֶ֥רֶץ יְהוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of the algum trees the king made steps for the house of the Lord, and for the king’s palace, and lyres and lutes for singers: and there were none such seen before in the land of Yehuda.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king made of the coral-wood a paving for the house of the L rd and for the house of the king, and harps and psalteries for the singers [(the Levites)]. The like of them had not been seen before in the land of Judah.


###### II Chronicles 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:12)


וְהַמֶּ֨לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֜ה נָתַ֣ן לְמַֽלְכַּת־שְׁבָ֗א אֶת־כׇּל־חֶפְצָהּ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁאָ֔לָה מִלְּבַ֖ד אֲשֶׁר־הֵבִ֣יאָה אֶל־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַֽתַּהֲפֹ֛ךְ וַתֵּ֥לֶךְ לְאַרְצָ֖הּ הִ֥יא וַעֲבָדֶֽיהָ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king Shelomo gave to the queen of Sheva all that she desired, whatever she asked, besides that which she had brought to the king. So she turned, and went away to her own land, she and her servants.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Solomon gave the queen of Sheva all of her desire that she asked [i.e., he taught her wisdom], aside from [what he had given her for] what she had brought to the king. And she turned and went to her land, she and her servants.


###### II Chronicles 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:13)


וַֽיְהִי֙ מִשְׁקַ֣ל הַזָּהָ֔ב אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֥א לִשְׁלֹמֹ֖ה בְּשָׁנָ֣ה אֶחָ֑ת שֵׁ֥שׁ מֵא֛וֹת וְשִׁשִּׁ֥ים וָשֵׁ֖שׁ כִּכְּרֵ֥י זָהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the weight of gold that came to Shelomo in one year was six hundred and sixty talents of gold,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the weight of the gold which came to Solomon in one year was six hundred and sixty-six talents of gold.


###### II Chronicles 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:14)


לְבַ֨ד מֵאַנְשֵׁ֧י הַתָּרִ֛ים וְהַסֹּחֲרִ֖ים מְבִיאִ֑ים וְכׇל־מַלְכֵ֤י עֲרַב֙ וּפַח֣וֹת הָאָ֔רֶץ מְבִיאִ֛ים זָהָ֥ב וָכֶ֖סֶף לִשְׁלֹמֹֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
besides that which the traders and merchants brought. And all the kings of ῾Arav and the governors of the country brought gold and silver to Shelomo.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Aside from what was brought by traders and by (spice) merchants. And all the kings of Arabia and the regents of the land brought gold and silver to Solomon.


###### II Chronicles 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:15)


וַיַּ֨עַשׂ הַמֶּ֧לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֛ה מָאתַ֥יִם צִנָּ֖ה זָהָ֣ב שָׁח֑וּט שֵׁ֤שׁ מֵאוֹת֙ זָהָ֣ב שָׁח֔וּט יַעֲלֶ֖ה עַל־הַצִּנָּ֥ה הָאֶחָֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king Shelomo made two hundred targets of beaten gold: six hundred shekels of beaten gold went to one target.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Solomon made two hundred cuirasses of beaten gold. Six hundred [dinars]of beaten gold would go into one cuirass.


###### II Chronicles 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:16)


וּשְׁלֹשׁ־מֵא֤וֹת מָֽגִנִּים֙ זָהָ֣ב שָׁח֔וּט שְׁלֹ֤שׁ מֵאוֹת֙ זָהָ֔ב יַעֲלֶ֖ה עַל־הַמָּגֵ֣ן הָאֶחָ֑ת וַיִּתְּנֵ֣ם הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ בְּבֵ֖ית יַ֥עַר הַלְּבָנֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He made three hundred shields of beaten gold also: three hundred shekels of gold went to one shield. And the king put them in the house of the forest of the Levanon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And three hundred shields of beaten gold. Three hundred [dinars] of gold would go into one shield. And the king placed them in the house of the forest of the Levanon.


###### II Chronicles 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:17)


וַיַּ֧עַשׂ הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ כִּסֵּא־שֵׁ֖ן גָּד֑וֹל וַיְצַפֵּ֖הוּ זָהָ֥ב טָהֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king made a great throne of ivory, and overlaid it with pure gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king made a great ivory throne and he overlaid it with glittering gold.


###### II Chronicles 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:18)


וְשֵׁ֣שׁ מַעֲל֣וֹת לַ֠כִּסֵּ֠א וְכֶ֨בֶשׁ בַּזָּהָ֤ב לַכִּסֵּא֙ מׇאֳחָזִ֔ים וְיָד֛וֹת מִזֶּ֥ה וּמִזֶּ֖ה עַל־מְק֣וֹם הַשָּׁ֑בֶת וּשְׁנַ֣יִם אֲרָי֔וֹת עֹמְדִ֖ים אֵ֥צֶל הַיָּדֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there were six steps to the throne, with a footstool of gold, which were fastened to the throne, and arms on each side of the place of the seat, and two lions standing by the arms:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There were six steps to the throne ([ascended] by a golden ramp) attached to the throne. And there were arms on either side of the seat, and two [golden] lions standing by the arms.


###### II Chronicles 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:19)


וּשְׁנֵ֧ים עָשָׂ֣ר אֲרָי֗וֹת עֹמְדִ֥ים שָׁ֛ם עַל־שֵׁ֥שׁ הַֽמַּעֲל֖וֹת מִזֶּ֣ה וּמִזֶּ֑ה לֹא־נַעֲשָׂ֥ה כֵ֖ן לְכׇל־מַמְלָכָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and twelve lions stood there on the one side and on the other upon the six steps. There was not the like made in any kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And twelve lions stood there on the six steps on either side. Its like was made for no other kingdom.


###### II Chronicles 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:20)


וְ֠כֹ֠ל כְּלֵ֞י מַשְׁקֵ֨ה הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה֙ זָהָ֔ב וְכֹ֗ל כְּלֵ֛י בֵּֽית־יַ֥עַר הַלְּבָנ֖וֹן זָהָ֣ב סָג֑וּר אֵ֣ין כֶּ֗סֶף נֶחְשָׁ֛ב בִּימֵ֥י שְׁלֹמֹ֖ה לִמְאֽוּמָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the drinking vessels of king Shelomo were of gold, and all the vessels of the house of the forest of the Levanon were of pure gold: none were of silver; it was not valued in the least in the days of Shelomo.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of King Solomon's drinking vessels were gold, and all the vessels of the house of the forest of the Levanon were pure gold. Silver was of no account in Solomon's days.


###### II Chronicles 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:21)


כִּֽי־אֳנִיּ֤וֹת לַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הֹלְכ֣וֹת תַּרְשִׁ֔ישׁ עִ֖ם עַבְדֵ֣י חוּרָ֑ם אַחַת֩ לְשָׁל֨וֹשׁ שָׁנִ֜ים תָּב֣וֹאנָה**׀** אֳנִיּ֣וֹת תַּרְשִׁ֗ישׁ נֹֽשְׂאוֹת֙ זָהָ֣ב וָכֶ֔סֶף שֶׁנְהַבִּ֥ים וְקוֹפִ֖ים וְתוּכִּיִּֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the king’s ships went to Tarshish with the servants of Ḥuram: once every three years came the ships of Tarshish bringing gold, and silver, ivory, and apes, and peacocks.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the king had Tarshish [(Africa)] ships at sea with Hiram's servants. Once in three years the Tarshish ships would come, bearing gold and silver and ivory and monkeys and parrots.


###### II Chronicles 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:22)


וַיִּגְדַּל֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה מִכֹּ֖ל מַלְכֵ֣י הָאָ֑רֶץ לְעֹ֖שֶׁר וְחׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king Shelomo surpassed all the kings of the earth in riches and wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Solomon surpassed all the kings of the earth in wealth and in wisdom.


###### II Chronicles 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:23)


וְכֹל֙ מַלְכֵ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ מְבַקְשִׁ֖ים אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י שְׁלֹמֹ֑ה לִשְׁמֹ֙עַ֙ אֶת־חׇכְמָת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥ן הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים בְּלִבּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the kings of the earth sought the presence of Shelomo, to hear his wisdom, that God had put in his heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the kings of the earth sought out Solomon to hear his wisdom which the L rd had placed in his heart.


###### II Chronicles 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:24)


וְהֵ֣ם מְבִיאִ֣ים אִ֣ישׁ מִנְחָת֡וֹ כְּלֵ֣י כֶ֩סֶף֩ וּכְלֵ֨י זָהָ֤ב וּשְׂלָמוֹת֙ נֵ֣שֶׁק וּבְשָׂמִ֔ים סוּסִ֖ים וּפְרָדִ֑ים דְּבַר־שָׁנָ֖ה בְּשָׁנָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they brought every man his present, vessels of silver, and vessels of gold, and garments, harness, and spices, horses, and mules, a rate year by year.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they would bring, each his gift: vessels of silver and vessels of gold, and garments, and weapons, and spices, horses and mules, the thing of the year in its year.


###### II Chronicles 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:25)


וַיְהִ֨י לִשְׁלֹמֹ֜ה אַרְבַּ֩עַת֩ אֲלָפִ֨ים אֻֽרְי֤וֹת סוּסִים֙ וּמַרְכָּב֔וֹת וּשְׁנֵים־עָשָׂ֥ר אֶ֖לֶף פָּרָשִׁ֑ים וַיַּנִּיחֵם֙ בְּעָרֵ֣י הָרֶ֔כֶב וְעִם־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo had four thousand stalls for horses and chariots, and twelve thousand horsemen; whom he bestowed in the chariot cities, and with the king at Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon had four thousand horse stables and chariots and twelve thousand riders. And he quartered them in the chariot cities, and [some were] with the king [and] in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:26)


וַיְהִ֥י מוֹשֵׁ֖ל בְּכׇל־הַמְּלָכִ֑ים מִן־הַנָּהָר֙ וְעַד־אֶ֣רֶץ פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים וְעַ֖ד גְּב֥וּל מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he reigned over all the kings from the river as far as the land of the Pelishtim, and to the border of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he ruled over all the kings, from the river until the land of the Philistines and until the border of Egypt.


###### II Chronicles 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:27)


וַיִּתֵּ֨ן הַמֶּ֧לֶךְ אֶת־הַכֶּ֛סֶף בִּירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם כָּאֲבָנִ֑ים וְאֵ֣ת הָאֲרָזִ֗ים נָתַ֛ן כַּשִּׁקְמִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־בַּשְּׁפֵלָ֖ה לָרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king made silver in Yerushalayim as common as stones, and cedar trees he made as common as the sycamore trees that are in the lowland for abundance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king rendered the silver in Jerusalem as [common] as stones, and he rendered the cedars as [common] as the sycamores in the lowlands for abundance.


###### II Chronicles 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:28)


וּמוֹצִיאִ֨ים סוּסִ֧ים מִמִּצְרַ֛יִם לִשְׁלֹמֹ֖ה וּמִכׇּל־הָאֲרָצֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they brought horses to Shelomo out of Miżrayim and out of all lands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they brought horses from Egypt for Solomon and from all the lands.


###### II Chronicles 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:29)


וּשְׁאָר֙ דִּבְרֵ֣י שְׁלֹמֹ֔ה הָרִאשֹׁנִ֖ים וְהָאַחֲרוֹנִ֑ים הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־דִּבְרֵי֙ נָתָ֣ן הַנָּבִ֔יא וְעַל־נְבוּאַ֞ת אֲחִיָּ֣ה הַשִּׁילוֹנִ֗י וּבַֽחֲזוֹת֙ (יעדי) [יֶעְדּ֣וֹ] הַחֹזֶ֔ה עַל־יָרׇבְעָ֖ם בֶּן־נְבָֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the rest of the acts of Shelomo, first and last, are they not written in the book of Natan the prophet, and in the prophecy of Aĥiyya the Shilonite, and in the visions of Ye῾do the seer against Yorov῾am the son of Nevat?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the events of (the reign of) Solomon, the earlier and the later, are they not recorded in the words of Nathan the prophet, and in the prophecy of Achiyah the Shilonite, and in the visions of Yeddo the seer concerning Yeravam the son of Nevat.


###### II Chronicles 9:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:30)


וַיִּמְלֹ֨ךְ שְׁלֹמֹ֧ה בִירוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם עַל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אַרְבָּעִ֥ים שָׁנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo reigned in Yerushalayim over all Yisra᾽el for forty years.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon reigned in Jerusalem over all of Israel, forty years.


###### II Chronicles 9:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/9:31)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֤ב שְׁלֹמֹה֙ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֔יו וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֔הוּ בְּעִ֖יר דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֑יו וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ רְחַבְעָ֥ם בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shelomo slept with his fathers, and he was buried in the city of David his father: and Reĥav῾am his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Solomon rested with his fathers, and they buried him in the city of David his father, and Rechavam his son reigned in his place.



## Chapter 10

###### II Chronicles 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:1)


וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ רְחַבְעָ֖ם שְׁכֶ֑מָה כִּ֥י שְׁכֶ֛ם בָּ֥אוּ כׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְהַמְלִ֥יךְ אֹתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Reĥav῾am went to Shekhem: for all Yisra᾽el came to Shekhem to make him king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Rechavam went to Shechem, for to Shechem came all of Israel to make him king.


###### II Chronicles 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:2)


וַיְהִ֞י כִּשְׁמֹ֨עַ יָרׇבְעָ֤ם בֶּן־נְבָט֙ וְה֣וּא בְמִצְרַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּרַ֔ח מִפְּנֵ֖י שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיָּ֥שׇׁב יָרׇבְעָ֖ם מִמִּצְרָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when Yorov῾am the son of Nevat, who was in Miżrayim, where he had fled from the presence of Shelomo the king, heard it, that Yorov῾am returned out of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when Yeravam the son of Nevat heard (of this), he was still in Egypt, having fled (there) from King Solomon, and Yeravam returned from Egypt.


###### II Chronicles 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:3)


וַֽיִּשְׁלְחוּ֙ וַיִּקְרְאוּ־ל֔וֹ וַיָּבֹ֥א יָרׇבְעָ֖ם וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַֽיְדַבְּר֔וּ אֶל־רְחַבְעָ֖ם לֵאמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they sent and called him. So Yorov῾am and all Yisra᾽el came and spoke to Reĥav῾am saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:4)


אָבִ֖יךָ הִקְשָׁ֣ה אֶת־עֻלֵּ֑נוּ וְעַתָּ֡ה הָקֵל֩ מֵעֲבוֹדַ֨ת אָבִ֜יךָ הַקָּשָׁ֗ה וּמֵעֻלּ֧וֹ הַכָּבֵ֛ד אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥ן עָלֵ֖ינוּ וְנַעַבְדֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy father made our yoke hard: now therefore ease thou somewhat the grievous servitude of thy father, and his heavy yoke that he put upon us, and we will serve thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your father hardened our yoke, and now, lighten your father's hard labor and his heavy yoke that he placed upon us and we will serve you.


###### II Chronicles 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:5)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם ע֛וֹד שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת יָמִ֖ים וְשׁ֣וּבוּ אֵלָ֑י וַיֵּ֖לֶךְ הָעָֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said to them, Come back to me after three days. And the people departed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:6)


וַיִּוָּעַ֞ץ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ רְחַבְעָ֗ם אֶת־הַזְּקֵנִים֙ אֲשֶׁר־הָי֣וּ עֹמְדִ֗ים לִפְנֵי֙ שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה אָבִ֔יו בִּהְיֹת֥וֹ חַ֖י לֵאמֹ֑ר אֵ֚יךְ אַתֶּ֣ם נֽוֹעָצִ֔ים לְהָשִׁ֥יב לָעָם־הַזֶּ֖ה דָּבָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king Reĥav῾am took counsel with the old men who had stood before Shelomo his father while he still lived, saying, What counsel do you give me to return answer to this people?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Rechavam took counsel with the elders who had stood before Solomon his father when he was alive, saying: How do you counsel to return word to this people?


###### II Chronicles 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:7)


וַיְדַבְּר֨וּ אֵלָ֜יו לֵאמֹ֗ר אִם־תִּהְיֶ֨ה לְט֜וֹב לְהָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ וּרְצִיתָ֔ם וְדִבַּרְתָּ֥ אֲלֵהֶ֖ם דְּבָרִ֣ים טוֹבִ֑ים וְהָי֥וּ לְךָ֛ עֲבָדִ֖ים כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they spoke to him, saying: If you will be good to his people and appease them and speak good things to them, then they will be servants to you all of the days.


###### II Chronicles 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:8)


וַֽיַּעֲזֹ֛ב אֶת־עֲצַ֥ת הַזְּקֵנִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר יְעָצֻ֑הוּ וַיִּוָּעַ֗ץ אֶת־הַיְלָדִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר גָּדְל֣וּ אִתּ֔וֹ הָעֹמְדִ֖ים לְפָנָֽיו׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם מָ֚ה אַתֶּ֣ם נֽוֹעָצִ֔ים וְנָשִׁ֥יב דָּבָ֖ר אֶת־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבְּר֤וּ אֵלַי֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הָקֵל֙ מִן־הָעֹ֔ל אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥ן אָבִ֖יךָ עָלֵֽינוּ׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:10)


וַיְדַבְּר֣וּ אִתּ֗וֹ הַיְלָדִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר גָּדְל֣וּ אִתּוֹ֮ לֵאמֹר֒ כֹּה־תֹאמַ֡ר לָעָם֩ אֲשֶׁר־דִּבְּר֨וּ אֵלֶ֜יךָ לֵאמֹ֗ר אָבִ֙יךָ֙ הִכְבִּ֣יד אֶת־עֻלֵּ֔נוּ וְאַתָּ֖ה הָקֵ֣ל מֵעָלֵ֑ינוּ כֹּ֚ה תֹּאמַ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֔ם קָֽטׇנִּ֥י עָבָ֖ה מִמׇּתְנֵ֥י אָבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the young men who were brought up with him spoke to him, saying, Thus shalt thou answer the people who spoke to thee, saying, Thy father made our yoke heavy, but make thou it somewhat lighter for us; thus shalt thou say to them, My little finger shall be thicker than my father’s loins.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the youngsters who grew up with him spoke, saying: Thus shall you say to this people who spoke to you, saying: Your father made our load heavy, and you, lighten it! — thus shall you answer them: My little finger is thicker than my father's thighs!


###### II Chronicles 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:11)


וְעַתָּ֗ה אָבִי֙ הֶעְמִ֤יס עֲלֵיכֶם֙ עֹ֣ל כָּבֵ֔ד וַאֲנִ֖י אֹסִ֣יף עַֽל־עֻלְּכֶ֑ם אָבִ֗י יִסַּ֤ר אֶתְכֶם֙ בַּשּׁוֹטִ֔ים וַאֲנִ֖י בָּעַקְרַבִּֽים׃{פ}

For whereas my father put a heavy yoke upon you, I will add to your yoke: my father chastised you with whips, but I will chastise you with scorpions.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, my father placed a heavy yoke upon you, and I will add to your load. My father chastened you with whips, but I [will chasten you] with [(a goad that stings like)] scorpions.


###### II Chronicles 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:12)

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yeravam and all the people came to Rechavam on the third day, as the king had spoken, saying: Return to me on the third day.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:13)

וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם כַּעֲצַ֤ת הַיְלָדִים֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר אַכְבִּיד֙ אֶֽת־עֻלְּכֶ֔ם וַאֲנִ֖י אֹסִ֣יף עָלָ֑יו אָבִ֗י יִסַּ֤ר אֶתְכֶם֙ בַּשּׁוֹטִ֔ים וַאֲנִ֖י בָּעַקְרַבִּֽים׃

And he spoke to them according to the counsel of the youngsters, saying: My father made your load heavy, and I will add to it. My father chastened you with whips, but I [will chasten you] with scorpions!


###### II Chronicles 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:15)


וְלֹא־שָׁמַ֥ע הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֶל־הָעָ֑ם כִּֽי־הָיְתָ֤ה נְסִבָּה֙ מֵעִ֣ם הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְמַ֩עַן֩ הָקִ֨ים יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶת־דְּבָר֗וֹ אֲשֶׁ֤ר דִּבֶּר֙ בְּיַד֙ אֲחִיָּ֣הוּ הַשִּׁלוֹנִ֔י אֶל־יָרׇבְעָ֖ם בֶּן־נְבָֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the king did not hearken to the people: for it was brought about by God, that the Lord might perform his word, which he spoke by the hand of Aĥiyyahu the Shilonite to Yorov῾am the son of Nevat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king did not pay heed to the people, for it was so effected by G d, so that the L rd establish His word that He had spoken through Achiyah the Shilonite to Yeravam the son of Nevat.


###### II Chronicles 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:16)


וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כִּ֠י לֹא־שָׁמַ֣ע הַמֶּ֘לֶךְ֮ לָהֶם֒ וַיָּשִׁ֣יבוּ הָעָ֣ם אֶת־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ׀לֵאמֹ֡ר מַה־לָּ֩נוּ֩ חֵ֨לֶק בְּדָוִ֜יד וְלֹא־נַחֲלָ֣ה בְּבֶן־יִשַׁ֗י אִ֤ישׁ לְאֹהָלֶ֙יךָ֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל עַתָּ֕ה רְאֵ֥ה בֵיתְךָ֖ דָּוִ֑יד וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְאֹהָלָֽיו׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when all Yisra᾽el saw that the king would not hearken to them, the people answered the king, saying, What portion have we in David? and we have no inheritance in the son of Yishay: every man to your tents, O Yisra᾽el: and now, David, look to thy house. So all Yisra᾽el went to their tents.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of Israel saw that the king had not heeded them, and the people returned word to the king, saying: What portion do we have in David? Nor (do we have) an inheritance in the son of Yishai. To your tents, Israel! Now, see your house, David [i.e., the Temple that you built will be yours alone!] And Israel went to their tents.


###### II Chronicles 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:17)


וּבְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיִּמְלֹ֥ךְ עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם רְחַבְעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But as for the children of Yisra᾽el who dwelt in the cities of Yehuda, Reĥav῾am reigned over them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/10:18)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֞ח הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ רְחַבְעָ֗ם אֶת־הֲדֹרָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הַמַּ֔ס וַיִּרְגְּמוּ־ב֧וֹ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל אֶ֖בֶן וַיָּמֹ֑ת{ס}וְהַמֶּ֣לֶךְ רְחַבְעָ֗ם הִתְאַמֵּץ֙ לַעֲל֣וֹת בַּמֶּרְכָּבָ֔ה לָנ֖וּס יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיָּ֩בֶן֩ וַיִּפְרֹ֨ץ מִכׇּל־בָּנָ֜יו לְֽכׇל־אַרְצ֧וֹת יְהוּדָ֣ה וּבִנְיָמִ֗ן לְכֹל֙ עָרֵ֣י הַמְּצֻר֔וֹת וַיִּתֵּ֥ן לָהֶ֛ם הַמָּז֖וֹן לָרֹ֑ב וַיִּשְׁאַ֖ל הֲמ֥וֹן נָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he dealt wisely, and dispersed of all his children throughout all the districts of Yehuda and Binyamin, to every fortified city: and he gave them provisions in abundance. And he sought many wives.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [Yeravam,] sensing [a rebellion], scattered of all of his sons to all of the lands of Judah and Benjamin, to all of the fortified cities. And he gave them food in abundance and sought [for them] an abundance of wives.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, in the fifth year of King Rechavam, that Shishak king of Egypt (Pharaoh Nechoh) went up against Jerusalem, for they had been faithless to the L rd.

## Chapter 13

###### II Chronicles 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/13:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֛ת שְׁמוֹנֶ֥ה עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ יָרׇבְעָ֑ם וַיִּמְלֹ֥ךְ אֲבִיָּ֖ה עַל־יְהוּדָֽה׃

###### II Chronicles 13:2

###### II Chronicles 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/13:3)


וַיֶּאְסֹ֨ר אֲבִיָּ֜ה אֶת־הַמִּלְחָמָ֗ה בְּחַ֙יִל֙ גִּבּוֹרֵ֣י מִלְחָמָ֔ה אַרְבַּע־מֵא֥וֹת אֶ֖לֶף אִ֣ישׁ בָּח֑וּר{ס}וְיָרׇבְעָ֗ם עָרַ֤ךְ עִמּוֹ֙ מִלְחָמָ֔ה בִּשְׁמוֹנֶ֨ה מֵא֥וֹת אֶ֛לֶף אִ֥ישׁ בָּח֖וּר גִּבּ֥וֹר חָֽיִל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aviyya set the battle in array with an army of valiant men of war, four hundred thousand chosen men: and Yorov῾am ranged himself in battle against him with eight hundred thousand chosen men, mighty men at arms.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 13:4

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aviyya stood up upon mount Żemarayim, which is in mount Efrayim, and said, Hear me, thou Yorov῾am, and all Yisra᾽el;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

for he took away the altars of the foreign gods, and the high places, and broke down the pillars, and cut down the asherim:

and he commanded Yehuda to seek the Lord God of their fathers, and to carry out the Tora and the commandment.

וַיֹּ֨אמֶר לִיהוּדָ֜ה נִבְנֶ֣ה**׀** אֶת־הֶעָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֗לֶּה וְנָסֵ֨ב חוֹמָ֣ה וּמִגְדָּלִים֮ דְּלָתַ֣יִם וּבְרִיחִים֒ עוֹדֶ֨נּוּ הָאָ֜רֶץ לְפָנֵ֗ינוּ כִּ֤י דָרַ֙שְׁנוּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ דָּרַ֕שְׁנוּ וַיָּ֥נַֽח־לָ֖נוּ מִסָּבִ֑יב וַיִּבְנ֖וּ וַיַּצְלִֽיחוּ׃{פ}

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to Judah: Let us build these cities and encircle (them) with walls and towers, doors and bars, while the land is still before us. For we sought — we sought the L rd our G d, and He gave us peace roundabout. And they built and they succeeded.

And when Asa heard these words, and the prophecy of ῾Oded the prophet, he took courage, and put away the disgusting idols out of all the land of Yehuda and Binyamin, and out of the cities which he had taken from mount Efrayim, and renewed the altar of the Lord, that was before the porch of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 16:1

And Assa took out silver and gold from the treasuries of the house of the L rd and the house of the king, and he sent to Ben Hadad the king of Aram, who sat in Damascus, saying:

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 16:10

וַיִּכְעַ֨ס אָסָ֜א אֶל־הָרֹאֶ֗ה וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֙הוּ֙ בֵּ֣ית הַמַּהְפֶּ֔כֶת כִּֽי־בְזַ֥עַף עִמּ֖וֹ עַל־זֹ֑את וַיְרַצֵּ֥ץ אָסָ֛א מִן־הָעָ֖ם בָּעֵ֥ת הַהִֽיא׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 16:11

וְהִנֵּה֙ דִּבְרֵ֣י אָסָ֔א הָרִאשׁוֹנִ֖ים וְהָאַחֲרוֹנִ֑ים הִנָּ֤ם כְּתוּבִים֙ עַל־סֵ֣פֶר הַמְּלָכִ֔ים לִיהוּדָ֖ה וְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And, behold, the acts of Asa, first and last, lo, they are written in the book of the kings of Yehuda and Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, the events (of the reign) of Assa, the earlier and the later are recorded in the book [of chronicles] of the kings of Judah and Israel.


###### II Chronicles 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/16:12)


וַיֶּחֱלֶ֣א אָסָ֡א בִּשְׁנַת֩ שְׁלוֹשִׁ֨ים וָתֵ֤שַׁע לְמַלְכוּתוֹ֙ בְּרַגְלָ֔יו עַד־לְמַ֖עְלָה חׇלְי֑וֹ וְגַם־בְּחׇלְיוֹ֙ לֹא־דָרַ֣שׁ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֖י בָּרֹפְאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Assa was stricken in the thirty-ninth year of his reign in his legs and (his ailment spread) upwards. And in his sickness, too, he did not seek out the L rd, but the doctors.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/16:13)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֥ב אָסָ֖א עִם־אֲבֹתָ֑יו וַיָּ֕מׇת בִּשְׁנַ֛ת אַרְבָּעִ֥ים וְאַחַ֖ת לְמׇלְכֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Asa slept with his fathers, and died in the forty first year of his reign.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Assa rested with his fathers, and he died in the forty-first year of his reign.


###### II Chronicles 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/16:14)


וַיִּקְבְּרֻ֣הוּ בְקִבְרֹתָ֗יו אֲשֶׁ֣ר כָּרָה־לוֹ֮ בְּעִ֣יר דָּוִיד֒ וַיַּשְׁכִּיבֻ֗הוּ בַּמִּשְׁכָּב֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר מִלֵּא֙ בְּשָׂמִ֣ים וּזְנִ֔ים מְרֻקָּחִ֖ים בְּמִרְקַ֣חַת מַעֲשֶׂ֑ה וַיִּשְׂרְפוּ־ל֥וֹ שְׂרֵפָ֖ה גְּדוֹלָ֥ה עַד־לִמְאֹֽד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they buried him in his own tomb, which he had made for himself in the city of David, and laid him on a bier which was filled with sweet odours and divers kinds of spices prepared by the perfumer’s art: and they made a very great burning for him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they buried him in his burial grounds that he had dug for himself in the city of David. And they laid him in a resting place that he had filled with diverse spices compounded by the perfumer's art. And they made for him a very great pyre [(for the burning of his personal effects)].
