###### II Chronicles 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:1)


וַיְהִ֧י לִיהוֹשָׁפָ֛ט עֹ֥שֶׁר וְכָב֖וֹד לָרֹ֑ב וַיִּתְחַתֵּ֖ן לְאַחְאָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now Yehoshafat had riches and honour in abundance, and he allied himself by marriage with Aĥ᾽av.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoshafat had wealth and honor in abundance and [his son, Yehoram] married [the daughter of] Achav.


###### II Chronicles 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:2)


וַיֵּ֩רֶד֩ לְקֵ֨ץ שָׁנִ֤ים אֶל־אַחְאָב֙ לְשֹׁ֣מְר֔וֹן וַיִּֽזְבַּֽח־ל֨וֹ אַחְאָ֜ב צֹ֤אן וּבָקָר֙ לָרֹ֔ב וְלָעָ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר עִמּ֑וֹ וַיְסִיתֵ֕הוּ לַעֲל֖וֹת אֶל־רָמֹ֥ת גִּלְעָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And after certain years he went down to Aĥ᾽av to Shomron. And Aĥ᾽av killed sheep and oxen for him in abundance, and for the people that he had with him, and persuaded him to go up with him to Ramot-gil῾ad.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And after some years he went down to Achav in Shomron, and Achav slaughtered for him sheep and cattle in abundance, and for the people that were with him, and he enticed him to go up [(to war)] to Ramoth-gilead.


###### II Chronicles 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:3)


וַיֹּ֜אמֶר אַחְאָ֣ב מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אֶל־יְהֽוֹשָׁפָט֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה הֲתֵלֵ֥ךְ עִמִּ֖י רָמֹ֣ת גִּלְעָ֑ד וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֗וֹ כָּמ֤וֹנִי כָמ֙וֹךָ֙ וּכְעַמְּךָ֣ עַמִּ֔י וְעִמְּךָ֖ בַּמִּלְחָמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aĥ᾽av king of Yisra᾽el said to Yehoshafat king of Yehuda, Wilt thou go with me to Ramot-gil῾ad? And he answered him, I am as thou art, and my people are as thy people; and we will be with thee in the war.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Achav king of Israel said to Yehoshafat king of Judah: Will you go with me [to war] in Ramoth-gilead? And he said to him: As you, am I; as your people, are my people, and [we are] with you in war.


###### II Chronicles 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:4)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט אֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל דְּרׇשׁ־נָ֥א כַיּ֖וֹם אֶת־דְּבַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yehoshafat said to the king of Yisra᾽el, Inquire, I pray thee, of the word of the Lord to day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoshafat said to the king of Israel: Seek out, this day, the word of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:5)


וַיִּקְבֹּ֨ץ מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֥ל אֶֽת־הַנְּבִאִים֮ אַרְבַּ֣ע מֵא֣וֹת אִישׁ֒ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם הֲנֵלֵ֞ךְ אֶל־רָמֹ֥ת גִּלְעָ֛ד לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה אִם־אֶחְדָּ֑ל וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ עֲלֵ֔ה וְיִתֵּ֥ן הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים בְּיַ֥ד הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore the king of Yisra᾽el gathered together prophets, four hundred men, and said to them, Shall we go to Ramot-gil῾ad to battle, or shall I forbear? And they said, Go up; for God will deliver it into the king’s hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel gathered the prophets, four hundred men, and he said to them: Shall we go to Ramoth-gilead to war or shall I forbear? And they said: Go up, and G d will deliver (it) to the king.


###### II Chronicles 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:6)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ יְה֣וֹשָׁפָ֔ט הַאֵ֨ין פֹּ֥ה נָבִ֛יא לַיהֹוָ֖ה ע֑וֹד וְנִדְרְשָׁ֖ה מֵאֹתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Yehoshafat said, Is there not here a prophet of the Lord besides, that we might inquire of him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoshafat said [(knowing that no two prophets prophesy in exactly the same words)]: Is there no other prophet of the L rd here that we can inquire of?


###### II Chronicles 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:7)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל׀אֶֽל־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֡ט ע֣וֹד אִישׁ־אֶחָ֡ד לִדְרוֹשׁ֩ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֨ה מֵאֹת֜וֹ וַאֲנִ֣י שְׂנֵאתִ֗יהוּ כִּֽי־אֵ֠ינֶ֠נּוּ מִתְנַבֵּ֨א עָלַ֤י לְטוֹבָה֙ כִּ֣י כׇל־יָמָ֣יו לְרָעָ֔ה ה֖וּא מִיכָ֣יְהוּ בֶן־יִמְלָ֑א וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ יְה֣וֹשָׁפָ֔ט אַל־יֹאמַ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ כֵּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of Yisra᾽el said to Yehoshafat, There is yet one man, by whom we may inquire of the Lord: but I hate him; for he never prophesied good to me, but always evil: he is Mikhayehu the son of Yimla. And Yehoshafat said, Let not the king say so.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel said to Yehoshafat: There is yet another man of whom we may make inquiry of the L rd, but I hate him, for he never prophesies good for me but always bad [viz. I Kings 20:42] — Michayhu the son of Yimlah. And Yehoshafat said: Let the king not speak thus.


###### II Chronicles 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:8)


וַיִּקְרָא֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֶל־סָרִ֖יס אֶחָ֑ד וַיֹּ֕אמֶר מַהֵ֖ר (מיכהו) [מִיכָ֥יְהוּ] בֶן־יִמְלָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the king of Yisra᾽el called for one of his officers, and said, Fetch quickly Mikhayehu the son of Yimla.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel called a certain eunuch and said: Hasten Michayhu the son of Yimlah (here).


###### II Chronicles 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:9)


וּמֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֡ל וִיהוֹשָׁפָ֣ט מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֡ה יוֹשְׁבִים֩ אִ֨ישׁ עַל־כִּסְא֜וֹ מְלֻבָּשִׁ֤ים בְּגָדִים֙ וְיֹשְׁבִ֣ים בְּגֹ֔רֶן פֶּ֖תַח שַׁ֣עַר שֹׁמְר֑וֹן וְכׇ֨ל־הַנְּבִיאִ֔ים מִֽתְנַבְּאִ֖ים לִפְנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of Yisra᾽el and Yehoshafat king of Yehuda sat each of them on his throne, clothed in their robes, and they sat in a threshing floor at the entrance of the gate of Shomeron; and all the prophets prophesied before them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel and Yehoshafat the king of Judah were sitting, each on his throne, dressed in [their royal] attire, in a threshing-floor at the entrance of the gate of Shomron, and all the prophets prophesying before them.


###### II Chronicles 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:10)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ ל֛וֹ צִדְקִיָּ֥הוּ בֶֽן־כְּנַעֲנָ֖ה קַרְנֵ֣י בַרְזֶ֑ל וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ כֹּֽה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה בְּאֵ֛לֶּה תְּנַגַּ֥ח אֶת־אֲרָ֖ם עַד־כַּלּוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Żidqiyyahu the son of Kena῾ana had made for himself horns of iron, and he said, Thus says the Lord, With these thou shalt push Aram until they are wiped out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Tzidkiyah the son of Kena'anah made himself iron horns, and he said: Thus said the L rd: With these shall you gore Aram until you make an end of them.


###### II Chronicles 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:11)


וְכׇ֨ל־הַנְּבִאִ֔ים נִבְּאִ֥ים כֵּ֖ן לֵאמֹ֑ר עֲלֵ֞ה רָמֹ֤ת גִּלְעָד֙ וְהַצְלַ֔ח וְנָתַ֥ן יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּיַ֥ד הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the prophets prophesied in the same sense, saying, Go up to Ramot-gil῾ad, and prosper: for the Lord shall deliver it into the hand of the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the (other) prophets prophesied thus: Go up to Ramoth-gilead and succeed, and the L rd will deliver (it) to the king.


###### II Chronicles 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:12)


וְהַמַּלְאָ֞ךְ אֲשֶׁר־הָלַ֣ךְ**׀** לִקְרֹ֣א לְמִיכָ֗יְהוּ דִּבֶּ֤ר אֵלָיו֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הִנֵּ֞ה דִּבְרֵ֧י הַנְּבִאִ֛ים פֶּה־אֶחָ֥ד ט֖וֹב אֶל־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וִיהִי־נָ֧א דְבָרְךָ֛ כְּאַחַ֥ד מֵהֶ֖ם וְדִבַּ֥רְתָּ טּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the messenger that went to call Mikhayehu spoke to him, saying, Behold, the words of the prophets declare good to the king with one assent; let thy word therefore, I pray thee, be like one of theirs, and speak thou good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the messenger who had gone to call Michayhu said to him: Behold, the words of the prophet, as one voice, are good for the king. Let your words be as (the words) of one of them, and speak good.


###### II Chronicles 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:13)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר מִיכָ֑יְהוּ חַי־יְהֹוָ֕ה כִּ֛י אֶת־אֲשֶׁר־יֹאמַ֥ר אֱלֹהַ֖י אֹת֥וֹ אֲדַבֵּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Mikhayehu said, As the Lord lives, even what my God says, that will I speak.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Michayhu said: As the L rd lives, what G d says to me, that will I speak.


###### II Chronicles 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:14)


וַיָּבֹא֮ אֶל־הַמֶּ֒לֶךְ֒ וַיֹּ֨אמֶר הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ אֵלָ֗יו מִיכָה֙ הֲנֵלֵ֞ךְ אֶל־רָמֹ֥ת גִּלְעָ֛ד לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה אִם־אֶחְדָּ֑ל וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ עֲל֣וּ וְהַצְלִ֔יחוּ וְיִנָּתְנ֖וּ בְּיֶדְכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he was come to the king, the king said to him, Mikha, shall we go to Ramot-gil῾ad to battle, or shall I forbear? And he said, Go up, and prosper, and they shall be delivered into your hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he came to the king, and the king said to him: Michah, Shall we go to Ramoth-gilead to war or shall I forbear? And he said: Go up and succeed, and they will be delivered into your hands.


###### II Chronicles 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:15)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֵלָיו֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ עַד־כַּמֶּ֥ה פְעָמִ֖ים אֲנִ֣י מַשְׁבִּיעֶ֑ךָ אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹֽא־תְדַבֵּ֥ר אֵלַ֛י רַק־אֱמֶ֖ת בְּשֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king said to him, How many times shall I adjure thee that thou say nothing to me but the truth in the name of the Lord?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king said to him: How many times must I adjure you to speak to me only the truth in the name of the L rd?


###### II Chronicles 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:16)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר רָאִ֤יתִי אֶת־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ נְפוֹצִ֣ים עַל־הֶהָרִ֔ים כַּצֹּ֕אן אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֵין־לָהֶ֖ן רֹעֶ֑ה וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ לֹֽא־אֲדֹנִ֣ים לָאֵ֔לֶּה יָשׁ֥וּבוּ אִישׁ־לְבֵית֖וֹ בְּשָׁלֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then he said, I saw all Yisra᾽el scattered upon the mountains, like sheep that have no shepherd: and the Lord said, These have no master; let them return therefore every man to his house in peace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: I saw all of Israel spread out on the mountains like sheep without a shepherd. And the L rd said: These have no master [i.e., you alone will die in the war (viz. 30)], (and) each man will return to his house in peace.


###### II Chronicles 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:17)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אֶל־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֑ט הֲלֹא֙ אָמַ֣רְתִּי אֵלֶ֔יךָ לֹא־יִתְנַבֵּ֥א עָלַ֛י ט֖וֹב כִּ֥י אִם־לְרָֽע׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of Yisra᾽el said to Yehoshafat, Did I not tell thee that he would not prophesy good to me but only evil?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel said to Yehoshafat: Did I not tell you that he would not prophesy good for me, but only evil?


###### II Chronicles 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:18)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר לָכֵ֖ן שִׁמְע֣וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֑ה רָאִ֤יתִי אֶת־יְהֹוָה֙ יוֹשֵׁ֣ב עַל־כִּסְא֔וֹ וְכׇל־צְבָ֤א הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ עֹמְדִ֔ים עַל־יְמִינ֖וֹ וּשְׂמֹאלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said, Therefore hear the word of the Lord; I saw the Lord sitting upon his throne, and all the host of heaven standing on his right hand and on his left.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(Michayhu)] said: Therefore, hear the word of the L rd. I saw the L rd sitting on His throne, and all the host of heaven standing over Him, on his right [(defending)], and on his left [(incriminating)].


###### II Chronicles 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:19)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָ֗ה מִ֤י יְפַתֶּה֙ אֶת־אַחְאָ֣ב מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְיַ֕עַל וְיִפֹּ֖ל בְּרָמ֣וֹת גִּלְעָ֑ד וַיֹּ֕אמֶר זֶ֚ה אֹמֵ֣ר כָּ֔כָה וְזֶ֖ה אֹמֵ֥ר כָּֽכָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said, Who will entice Aĥ᾽av king of Yisra᾽el, that he may go up and fall at Ramot-gil῾ad? And one spoke saying after this manner, and another saying after that manner.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said: Who will entice Achav king of Israel to go up and fall in Ramoth-gilead? And one [(faction)] said thus, and the other, thus.


###### II Chronicles 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:20)


וַיֵּצֵ֣א הָר֗וּחַ וַֽיַּעֲמֹד֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֲנִ֣י אֲפַתֶּ֑נּוּ וַיֹּ֧אמֶר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֵלָ֖יו בַּמָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the spirit came forward, and stood before the Lord, and said, I will entice him. And the Lord said to him, With what?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the spirit [(of Navoth, viz. I Kings 21)] came forth and stood before the L rd and said: I will entice him. And the L rd said to him: How?


###### II Chronicles 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:21)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר אֵצֵא֙ וְהָיִ֙יתִי֙ לְר֣וּחַ שֶׁ֔קֶר בְּפִ֖י כׇּל־נְבִיאָ֑יו וַיֹּ֗אמֶר תְּפַתֶּה֙ וְגַם־תּוּכָ֔ל צֵ֖א וַעֲשֵׂה־כֵֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said, I will go out, and be a lying spirit in the mouth of all his prophets. And he said, Thou shalt entice him, and thou shalt also prevail: go out, and do so.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: I shall go out and be a spirit of falsehood in the mouth of all his prophets. And He said: Entice and prevail; go out and do so.

Now therefore, behold the Lord has put a lying spirit in the mouth of these thy prophets, and the Lord has spoken evil against thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, behold, the L rd has placed a spirit of falsehood in the mouth of all of these, your prophets, and the L rd has spoken evil for you.

וַיִּגַּשׁ֙ צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ בֶֽן־כְּנַעֲנָ֔ה וַיַּ֥ךְ אֶת־מִיכָ֖יְהוּ עַל־הַלֶּ֑חִי וַיֹּ֗אמֶר אֵ֣י זֶ֤ה הַדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ עָבַ֧ר רוּחַ־יְהֹוָ֛ה מֵאִתִּ֖י לְדַבֵּ֥ר אֹתָֽךְ׃

And Tzidkiyahu the son of Kena'anah came forward and smote Michayhu on the cheek. And he said: Which way did the spirit of the L rd pass from me to speak to you?


###### II Chronicles 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:24)

###### II Chronicles 18:25

וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל קְחוּ֙ אֶת־מִיכָ֔יְהוּ וַהֲשִׁיבֻ֖הוּ אֶל־אָמ֣וֹן שַׂר־הָעִ֑יר וְאֶל־יוֹאָ֖שׁ בֶּן־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king of Yisra᾽el said, Take Mikhayehu, and carry him back to Amon the governor of the city, and to Yo᾽ash the king’s son;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel said: Take Michayhu and return him to Amon, the lord of the city, and to Yoash, the son of the king.


###### II Chronicles 18:26

וַאֲמַרְתֶּ֗ם כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ שִׂ֥ימוּ זֶ֖ה בֵּ֣ית הַכֶּ֑לֶא וְהַאֲכִלֻ֜הוּ לֶ֤חֶם לַ֙חַץ֙ וּמַ֣יִם לַ֔חַץ עַ֖ד שׁוּבִ֥י בְשָׁלֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and say, Thus says the king, Put this fellow in the prison, and feed him with scant bread and with scant water, until I return in peace.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:27)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר מִיכָ֔יְהוּ אִם־שׁ֤וֹב תָּשׁוּב֙ בְּשָׁל֔וֹם לֹא־דִבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה בִּ֑י וַיֹּ֕אמֶר שִׁמְע֖וּ עַמִּ֥ים כֻּלָּֽם׃

And Mikhayehu said, If thou at all return in peace, then the Lord has not spoken by me. And he said, Hearken, all you people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיַּ֧עַל מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וִיהוֹשָׁפָ֥ט מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֖ה אֶל־רָמֹ֥ת גִּלְעָֽד׃

So the king of Yisra᾽el and Yehoshafat the king of Yehuda went up to Ramot-gil῾ad.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:29)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ מֶ֨לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל אֶל־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֗ט הִתְחַפֵּשׂ֙ וָב֣וֹא בַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וְאַתָּ֖ה לְבַ֣שׁ בְּגָדֶ֑יךָ וַיִּתְחַפֵּשׂ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ בַּמִּלְחָמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of Yisra᾽el said to Yehoshafat, I will disguise myself, and will go to the battle; but do thou put on thy robes. So the king of Yisra᾽el disguised himself; and they went to the battle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Israel said to Yehoshafat: [(I will)] disguise [(myself)] and come into the war [(for if they recognize me, they will all come out against me)], and you, remain clothed as you are [(for they have no quarrel with the king of Judah)]. And the king of Israel disguised himself and they came into the war.


###### II Chronicles 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:30)


וּמֶ֣לֶךְ אֲרָ֡ם צִוָּה֩ אֶת־שָׂרֵ֨י הָרֶ֤כֶב אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֚א תִּלָּ֣חֲמ֔וּ אֶת־הַקָּטֹ֖ן אֶת־הַגָּד֑וֹל כִּ֛י אִֽם־אֶת־מֶ֥לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְבַדּֽוֹ׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Aram commanded the captains of his chariots, saying: Do not fight the small and the great, but with the king of Israel alone.


###### II Chronicles 18:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:31)


וַיְהִ֡י כִּרְאוֹת֩ שָׂרֵ֨י הָרֶ֜כֶב אֶת־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֗ט וְהֵ֤מָּה אָֽמְרוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל ה֔וּא וַיָּסֹ֥בּוּ עָלָ֖יו לְהִלָּחֵ֑ם וַיִּזְעַ֤ק יְהֽוֹשָׁפָט֙ וַיהֹוָ֣ה עֲזָר֔וֹ וַיְסִיתֵ֥ם אֱלֹהִ֖ים מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when the captains of the chariots saw Yehoshafat, that they said, It is the king of Yisra᾽el. Therefore they surrounded him to fight: but Yehoshafat cried out, and the Lord helped him; and God drew them away from him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when the captains of the chariots saw Yehoshafat, they said: He must be the king of Israel, and they encircled him to fight, and Yehoshafat cried out [(to the L rd)]. And the L rd helped him, and G d turned them away from him.


###### II Chronicles 18:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:32)


וַיְהִ֗י כִּרְאוֹת֙ שָׂרֵ֣י הָרֶ֔כֶב כִּ֥י לֹֽא־הָיָ֖ה מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיָּשׁ֖וּבוּ מֵאַחֲרָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For it came to pass, that, when the captains of the chariots perceived that it was not the king of Yisra᾽el, they turned back from pursuing him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when the captains of the chariots saw that it was not the king of Israel, they turned back.


###### II Chronicles 18:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:33)


וְאִ֗ישׁ מָשַׁ֤ךְ בַּקֶּ֙שֶׁת֙ לְתֻמּ֔וֹ וַיַּךְ֙ אֶת־מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בֵּ֥ין הַדְּבָקִ֖ים וּבֵ֣ין הַשִּׁרְיָ֑ן וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לָרַכָּ֗ב הֲפֹ֧ךְ (ידיך) [יָדְךָ֛] וְהוֹצֵאתַ֥נִי מִן־הַֽמַּחֲנֶ֖ה כִּ֥י הׇֽחֳלֵֽיתִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And a certain man drew his bow at a venture, and smote the king of Yisra᾽el between the joints of the armour: so he said to the driver of his chariot, Turn thy hand, and carry me out of the host; for I am badly wounded.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man [(Na'aman, viz. II Kings 5:1)] casually plied his bow and he struck the king of Israel between the scales and the coat of mail. And he said to his charioteer: Turn your hand and take me out of the camp, for I have sickened.


###### II Chronicles 18:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/18:34)


וַתַּ֤עַל הַמִּלְחָמָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא וּמֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל הָיָ֨ה מַעֲמִ֧יד בַּמֶּרְכָּבָ֛ה נֹ֥כַח אֲרָ֖ם עַד־הָעָ֑רֶב וַיָּ֕מׇת לְעֵ֖ת בּ֥וֹא הַשָּֽׁמֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the battle increased that day: but the king of Yisra᾽el propped himself up in his chariot against Aram until evening: and about the time of sunset he died.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the [intensity of the] war mounted on that day, and the king forced himself to stand in the chariot against Aram [(lest his army flee)], until the evening, and he died at the setting of the sun.



## Chapter 19

###### II Chronicles 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:1)


וַ֠יָּ֠שׇׁב יְהוֹשָׁפָ֨ט מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֧ה אֶל־בֵּית֛וֹ בְּשָׁל֖וֹם לִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yehoshafat the king of Yehuda returned to his house in peace to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoshafat the king of Judah returned to his home in peace in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:2)


וַיֵּצֵ֣א אֶל־פָּנָ֗יו יֵה֣וּא בֶן־חֲנָ֘נִי֮ הַחֹזֶה֒ וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ יְהוֹשָׁפָ֔ט הֲלָרָשָׁ֣ע לַעְזֹ֔ר וּלְשֹׂנְאֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה תֶּאֱהָ֑ב וּבָזֹאת֙ עָלֶ֣יךָ קֶּ֔צֶף מִלִּפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yehu the son of Ḥanani the seer went out to meet him, and said to king Yehoshafat, Shouldst thou help the wicked, and love those who hate the Lord? therefore wrath is come upon thee from before the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there went out before him Chanani the seer, and he said to the king: Would you help the wicked one [(Achav)] and would you love the foes of the L rd! And because of this there is wrath upon you from before the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:3)


אֲבָ֕ל דְּבָרִ֥ים טוֹבִ֖ים נִמְצְא֣וּ עִמָּ֑ךְ כִּֽי־בִעַ֤רְתָּ הָאֲשֵׁרוֹת֙ מִן־הָאָ֔רֶץ וַהֲכִינ֥וֹתָ לְבָבְךָ֖ לִדְרֹ֥שׁ הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless there are good things found in thee, in that thou hast taken away the asherot out of the land, and hast directed thy heart to seek God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But good things have been found with you, for you have removed the asheiroth from the land and you have readied your heart to seek out G d.


###### II Chronicles 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:4)


וַיֵּ֥שֶׁב יְהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיָּ֜שׇׁב וַיֵּצֵ֣א בָעָ֗ם מִבְּאֵ֥ר שֶׁ֙בַע֙ עַד־הַ֣ר אֶפְרַ֔יִם וַיְשִׁיבֵ֕ם אֶל־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י אֲבוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yehoshafat dwelt at Yerushalayim: and he went out again among the people from Be᾽er-sheva to mount Efrayim, and brought them back to the Lord God of their fathers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoshafat sat in Jerusalem and he went out [(from time to time)] among the people from Beersheva until Mount Ephraim, and he returned them to the L rd, the G d of their fathers.


###### II Chronicles 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:5)


וַיַּעֲמֵ֨ד שֹׁפְטִ֜ים בָּאָ֗רֶץ בְּכׇל־עָרֵ֧י יְהוּדָ֛ה הַבְּצֻר֖וֹת לְעִ֥יר וָעִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he set judges in the land throughout all the fortified cities of Yehuda, city by city.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he set up judges in the land in all the fortified cities of Judah, for every city.


###### II Chronicles 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:6)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֶל־הַשֹּׁפְטִ֗ים רְאוּ֙ מָה־אַתֶּ֣ם עֹשִׂ֔ים כִּ֣י לֹ֧א לְאָדָ֛ם תִּשְׁפְּט֖וּ כִּ֣י לַיהֹוָ֑ה וְעִמָּכֶ֖ם בִּדְבַ֥ר מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said to the judges, take heed what you do: for you do not judge for man, but for the Lord, who is with you in the judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to the judges: Look to what you are doing; for you are not judging for man but for the L rd, and [He is] with you in judgment.


###### II Chronicles 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:7)


וְעַתָּ֕ה יְהִ֥י פַחַד־יְהֹוָ֖ה עֲלֵיכֶ֑ם שִׁמְר֣וּ וַעֲשׂ֔וּ כִּֽי־אֵ֞ין עִם־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ עַוְלָ֛ה וּמַשֹּׂ֥א פָנִ֖ים וּמִקַּח־שֹֽׁחַד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now let the fear of the Lord be upon you; take heed and do it: for there is no iniquity with the Lord our God, nor respect of persons, nor taking of gifts.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, let the fear of the L rd be upon you. Take heed and do, for there is no wrong, or favoritism, or bribe-taking with the L rd our G d.


###### II Chronicles 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/19:8)

וְכׇל־רִיב֩ אֲשֶׁר־יָב֨וֹא עֲלֵיכֶ֜ם מֵאֲחֵיכֶ֣ם**׀** הַיֹּשְׁבִ֣ים בְּעָרֵיהֶ֗ם בֵּֽין־דָּ֣ם׀לְדָם֮ בֵּין־תּוֹרָ֣ה לְמִצְוָה֮ לְחֻקִּ֣ים וּלְמִשְׁפָּטִים֒ וְהִזְהַרְתֶּ֣ם אֹתָ֔ם וְלֹ֤א יֶאְשְׁמוּ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וְהָיָה־קֶ֥צֶף עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם וְעַל־אֲחֵיכֶ֑ם כֹּ֥ה תַעֲשׂ֖וּן וְלֹ֥א תֶאְשָֽׁמוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And whatever cause shall come to you from your brethren who dwell in their cities, between blood and blood, concerning Tora and commandment, and statutes and judgments, you shall warn them not to trespass against the Lord, so as to bring wrath upon you, and upon your brethren: this do, and you shall not trespass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Any litigation that comes to you [(for a decision)] from your brothers [(the judges you have appointed)] who sit [in judgment] in their cities — whether [it be a judgment] between blood and blood or [a judgment of] Torah [as it applies] to mitzvah, statutes or judgments — you shall exhort them [(to abide by your decisions)] so that they not be guilty before the L rd and [His] wrath descend upon you and your brothers. Thus shall you do and you will not bear guilt.

###### II Chronicles 20:16

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [Yehoshafat] joined him [Achazyah] with him to make ships to go to Tarshish. And they made ships in Etzyon-gaver.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/20:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/20:37)


וַיִּתְנַבֵּ֞א אֱלִיעֶ֤זֶר בֶּן־דֹּדָוָ֙הוּ֙ מִמָּ֣רֵשָׁ֔ה עַל־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט לֵאמֹ֑ר כְּהִֽתְחַבֶּרְךָ֣ עִם־אֲחַזְיָ֗הוּ פָּרַ֤ץ יְהֹוָה֙ אֶֽת־מַעֲשֶׂ֔יךָ וַיִּשָּׁבְר֣וּ אֳנִיּ֔וֹת וְלֹ֥א עָצְר֖וּ לָלֶ֥כֶת אֶל־תַּרְשִֽׁישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Eli῾ezer the son of Dodavahu of Maresha prophesied against Yehoshafat, saying, Because thou hast joined thyself with Aĥazyahu, the Lord will destroy what thou hast done. And the ships were wrecked, so that they were not able to go to Tarshish.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Eliezer the son of Dodavah of Mareshah prophesied over Yehoshafat: "Because you joined yourself with Achazyahu, the L rd has breached your works." And the ships were broken and they were unable to go to Tarshish.

###### II Chronicles 21:1

###### II Chronicles 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/21:20)


בֶּן־שְׁלֹשִׁ֤ים וּשְׁתַּ֙יִם֙ הָיָ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וּשְׁמוֹנֶ֣ה שָׁנִ֔ים מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיֵּ֙לֶךְ֙ בְּלֹ֣א חֶמְדָּ֔ה וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֙הוּ֙ בְּעִ֣יר דָּוִ֔יד וְלֹ֖א בְּקִבְר֥וֹת הַמְּלָכִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thirty two years old was he when he began to reign, and he reigned in Yerushalayim for eight years, and departed joyless. And they buried him in the city of David, but not in the tombs of the kings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He was thirty-two years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned eight years in Jerusalem. And he went [from the world] without (having experienced any) happiness. And they buried him in the city of David, but not in the crypts of the kings.



## Chapter 22

###### II Chronicles 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:1)


וַיַּמְלִ֩יכוּ֩ יוֹשְׁבֵ֨י יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם אֶת־אֲחַזְיָ֨הוּ בְנ֤וֹ הַקָּטֹן֙ תַּחְתָּ֔יו כִּ֤י כׇל־הָרִֽאשֹׁנִים֙ הָרַ֣ג הַגְּד֔וּד הַבָּ֥א בָעַרְבִ֖ים לַֽמַּחֲנֶ֑ה וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ אֲחַזְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־יְהוֹרָ֖ם מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the inhabitants of Yerushalayim made Aĥazyahu his youngest son king in his stead: for the band of men that came with the ῾Arvim to the camp had slain all the eldest. So Aĥazyahu the son of Yehoram king of Yehuda reigned.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the dwellers of Jerusalem made Achazyahu, his youngest son, king in his place. For all of the first (sons) had been killed by the Arab troop that had come to the camp [viz. 21:17]. And Achazyahu the son of Yehoram king of Judah reigned.


###### II Chronicles 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:2)


בֶּן־אַרְבָּעִ֨ים וּשְׁתַּ֤יִם שָׁנָה֙ אֲחַזְיָ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְשָׁנָ֣ה אַחַ֔ת מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ עֲתַלְיָ֖הוּ בַּת־עׇמְרִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Forty two years old was Aĥazyahu when he began to reign, and he reigned one year in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was ῾Atalyahu the daughter of ῾Omri.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Achazyahu was forty-two years old when he [began] to rule. And he reigned one year in Jerusalem. His mother's name was Atalyahu, the [grand-] daughter of Omri, king of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:3)


גַּם־ה֣וּא הָלַ֔ךְ בְּדַרְכֵ֖י בֵּ֣ית אַחְאָ֑ב כִּ֥י אִמּ֛וֹ הָיְתָ֥ה יוֹעַצְתּ֖וֹ לְהַרְשִֽׁיעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He also walked in the ways of the house of Aĥ᾽av: for his mother was his counsellor to do wickedly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He, too, went in the ways of Achav, his mother counseling him to evil.


###### II Chronicles 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:4)


וַיַּ֧עַשׂ הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה כְּבֵ֣ית אַחְאָ֑ב כִּי־הֵ֜מָּה הָיוּ־ל֣וֹ יוֹעֲצִ֗ים אַחֲרֵ֛י מ֥וֹת אָבִ֖יו לְמַשְׁחִ֥ית לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So he did evil in the sight of the Lord like the house of Aĥ᾽av: for they were his counsellors after the death of his father to his destruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was evil in the eyes of the L rd, as (did) the house of Achav, for they were his counselors after his father's death, to corrupt him.


###### II Chronicles 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:5)


גַּ֣ם בַּעֲצָתָם֮ הָלַךְ֒ וַיֵּ֩לֶךְ֩ אֶת־יְהוֹרָ֨ם בֶּן־אַחְאָ֜ב מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל לַמִּלְחָמָ֛ה עַל־חֲזָאֵ֥ל מֶלֶךְ־אֲרָ֖ם בְּרָמ֣וֹת גִּלְעָ֑ד וַיַּכּ֥וּ הָרַמִּ֖ים אֶת־יוֹרָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He also followed their counsel, and went with Yehoram the son of Aĥ᾽av king of Yisra᾽el to war against Ḥaza᾽el king of Aram at Ramot-gil῾ad: and the Arammim smote Yoram.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Their counsel, too, he followed. And Yehoram, the son of Achav, king of Israel, went to war against Chazael, king of Aram, in Ramoth-gilead. And the Aramites wounded Yoram.


###### II Chronicles 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:6)


וַיָּ֜שׇׁב לְהִתְרַפֵּ֣א בְיִזְרְעֶ֗אל כִּ֤י הַמַּכִּים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִכֻּ֣הוּ בָרָמָ֔ה בְּהִלָּ֣חֲמ֔וֹ אֶת־חֲזָהאֵ֖ל מֶ֣לֶךְ אֲרָ֑ם וַעֲזַרְיָ֨הוּ בֶן־יְהוֹרָ֜ם מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֗ה יָרַ֡ד לִרְא֞וֹת אֶת־יְהוֹרָ֧ם בֶּן־אַחְאָ֛ב בְּיִזְרְעֶ֖אל כִּי־חֹלֶ֥ה הֽוּא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he returned to be healed in Yizre᾽el because of the wounds which were given him at Rama, when he fought with Ḥaza᾽el king of Aram. And ῾Azaryahu the son of Yehoram king of Yehuda went down to see Yehoram the son of Aĥ᾽av at Yizre᾽el, because he was sick.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he returned to recuperate in Yizre'el from the wounds inflicted upon him in Ramah when he warred with Chazael, king of Aram. And Azaryahu [(Achazyahu)] the son of Yehoram king of Judah went down to see Yehoram the son of Achav in Yizre'el, for he was ill.


###### II Chronicles 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:7)


וּמֵאֱלֹהִ֗ים הָֽיְתָה֙ תְּבוּסַ֣ת אֲחַזְיָ֔הוּ לָב֖וֹא אֶל־יוֹרָ֑ם וּבְבֹא֗וֹ יָצָ֤א עִם־יְהוֹרָם֙ אֶל־יֵה֣וּא בֶן־נִמְשִׁ֔י אֲשֶׁ֣ר מְשָׁח֣וֹ יְהֹוָ֔ה לְהַכְרִ֖ית אֶת־בֵּ֥ית אַחְאָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the destruction of Aĥazyahu came from God in that he went to Yoram: for when he was come, he went out with Yehoram against Yehu the son of Nimshi, whom the Lord had anointed to cut off the house of Aĥ᾽av.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from G d was the downfall of Achazyahu, his coming to Yoram. And when he came, he went out with Yehoram to Yehu the son of Nimshi, whom the L rd had anointed [(as king over Israel)] to cut off the house of Achav [viz. II Kings 9].


###### II Chronicles 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:8)


וַיְהִ֕י כְּהִשָּׁפֵ֥ט יֵה֖וּא עִם־בֵּ֣ית אַחְאָ֑ב וַיִּמְצָא֩ אֶת־שָׂרֵ֨י יְהוּדָ֜ה וּבְנֵ֨י אֲחֵ֧י אֲחַזְיָ֛הוּ מְשָׁרְתִ֥ים לַאֲחַזְיָ֖הוּ וַיַּהַרְגֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, that, when Yehu was executing judgment upon the house of Aĥ᾽av, he found the princes of Yehuda, and the sons of Aĥazyahu’s brothers, who attended Aĥazyahu, and he slew them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when Yehu executed judgment upon the house of Achav, that he found the officers of Judah and the sons of Achazyahu's brothers ministering to Achazyahu, and he killed them.


###### II Chronicles 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:9)


וַיְבַקֵּשׁ֩ אֶת־אֲחַזְיָ֨הוּ וַֽיִּלְכְּדֻ֜הוּ וְה֧וּא מִתְחַבֵּ֣א בְשֹׁמְר֗וֹן וַיְבִאֻ֣הוּ אֶל־יֵהוּא֮ וַיְמִיתֻ֒הוּ֒ וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֔הוּ כִּ֤י אָֽמְרוּ֙ בֶּן־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֣ט ה֔וּא אֲשֶׁר־דָּרַ֥שׁ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּכׇל־לְבָב֑וֹ וְאֵין֙ לְבֵ֣ית אֲחַזְיָ֔הוּ לַעְצֹ֥ר כֹּ֖חַ לְמַמְלָכָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he sought Aĥazyahu: and they caught him, (for he was hid in Shomeron,) and they brought him to Yehu: and when they had slain him, they buried him: Because, said they, he is the son of Yehoshafat, who sought the Lord with all his heart. So the house of Aĥazyahu had no one able to hold the kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he sought Achazyahu and they caught him while he was hiding in Shomron, and they brought him to Yehu and he put him to death. And they buried him, saying: He is the [grand-] son of Yehoshafat, who sought out the L rd with all his heart. And there were none in the house of Achazyahu who could summon up strength for kingdom [(all of Achazyah's children being very young)].


###### II Chronicles 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:10)


וַעֲתַלְיָ֙הוּ֙ אֵ֣ם אֲחַזְיָ֔הוּ רָאֲתָ֖ה כִּ֣י מֵ֣ת בְּנָ֑הּ וַתָּ֗קׇם וַתְּדַבֵּ֛ר אֶת־כׇּל־זֶ֥רַע הַמַּמְלָכָ֖ה לְבֵ֥ית יְהוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But when ῾Atalyahu the mother of Aĥazyahu saw that her son was dead, she arose and destroyed all the royal seed of the house of Yehuda.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Atalyahu, the mother of Achazyahu, saw that her son had died, and she arose and poisoned all the seed of the kingdom of the house of Judah.


###### II Chronicles 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:11)


וַתִּקַּח֩ יְהוֹשַׁבְעַ֨ת בַּת־הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ אֶת־יוֹאָ֣שׁ בֶּן־אֲחַזְיָ֗הוּ וַתִּגְנֹ֤ב אֹתוֹ֙ מִתּ֤וֹךְ בְּנֵֽי־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַמּ֣וּמָתִ֔ים וַתִּתֵּ֥ן אֹת֛וֹ וְאֶת־מֵינִקְתּ֖וֹ בַּחֲדַ֣ר הַמִּטּ֑וֹת וַתַּסְתִּירֵ֡הוּ יְהוֹשַׁבְעַ֣ת בַּת־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ יְהוֹרָ֡ם אֵ֩שֶׁת֩ יְהוֹיָדָ֨ע הַכֹּהֵ֜ן כִּ֣י הִיא֩ הָיְתָ֨ה אֲח֧וֹת אֲחַזְיָ֛הוּ מִפְּנֵ֥י עֲתַלְיָ֖הוּ וְלֹ֥א הֱמִיתָֽתְהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Yehoshav’at, the daughter of the king, took Yo᾽ash the son of Aĥazyahu, and stole him from among the king’s sons who were slain, and put him and his nurse in a bedchamber. So Yehoshav῾at, the daughter of king Yehoram, the wife of Yehoyada the priest, (for she was the sister of Aĥazyahu,) hid him from ῾Atalyahu so that she could not slay him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoshavath the daughter of the king [(Yehoram)] took Yoash the son of Achazyahu and stole him from amidst the sons of the king who had been put to death and placed him and his nurse in the "bed-chamber" [(an upper chamber of the holy of holies)]. And Yehoshavath the daughter of King Yehoram, the wife of Yehoyada the [high-] priest, concealed him (for she was the sister of Achazyahu) from Atalyahu, so that she did not put him to death.


###### II Chronicles 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/22:12)


וַיְהִ֤י אִתָּם֙ בְּבֵ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים מִתְחַבֵּ֖א שֵׁ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֑ים וַעֲתַלְיָ֖ה מֹלֶ֥כֶת עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he was with them hid in the house of God for six years: and ῾Atalya reigned over the land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he was with them in the house of G d, hidden for six years. And Atalyah reigned over the land.



## Chapter 23

###### II Chronicles 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:1)


וּבַשָּׁנָ֨ה הַשְּׁבִעִ֜ית הִתְחַזַּ֣ק יְהוֹיָדָ֗ע וַיִּקַּ֣ח אֶת־שָׂרֵ֣י הַמֵּא֡וֹת לַעֲזַרְיָ֣הוּ בֶן־יְרֹחָ֡ם וּלְיִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־יְ֠הוֹחָנָ֠ן וְלַעֲזַרְיָ֨הוּ בֶן־עוֹבֵ֜ד וְאֶת־מַעֲשֵׂיָ֧הוּ בֶן־עֲדָיָ֛הוּ וְאֶת־אֱלִישָׁפָ֥ט בֶּן־זִכְרִ֖י עִמּ֥וֹ בַבְּרִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the seventh year Yehoyada strengthened himself, and took the captains of hundreds, ῾Azarya the son of Yeroĥam, and Yishma᾽el the son of Yehoĥanan, and ῾Azaryahu the son of ῾Oved, and Ma῾aseyahu the son of ῾Adayahu, and Elishafat the son of Zikhri, into league with him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the seventh year Yehoyada strengthened himself and took the captains of hundreds: Azaryah the son of Yerocham and Yishmael the son of Yehochanan and Azaryahu the son of Oved and Ma'aseyahu the son of Adayahu and Elishafat the son of Zichri with him into a covenant [(to assist him in crowning Yoash)].


###### II Chronicles 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:2)


וַיָּסֹ֙בּוּ֙ בִּֽיהוּדָ֔ה וַיִּקְבְּצ֤וּ אֶת־הַלְוִיִּם֙ מִכׇּל־עָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וְרָאשֵׁ֥י הָאָב֖וֹת לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ אֶל־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they went about in Yehuda, and gathered the Levites out of all the cities of Yehuda, and the heads of the fathers’ houses of Yisra᾽el, and they came to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they went throughout Judah, and they gathered the Levites from all the cities of Judah and the heads of the [fathers'] houses of Israel, and they came to Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:3)


וַיִּכְרֹ֨ת כׇּל־הַקָּהָ֥ל בְּרִ֛ית בְּבֵ֥ית הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים עִם־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לָהֶ֗ם הִנֵּ֤ה בֶן־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ יִמְלֹ֔ךְ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה עַל־בְּנֵ֥י דָוִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the congregation made a covenant with the king in the house of God. And he said to them, Behold, the king’s son shall reign, as the Lord has said of the sons of David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the entire congregation made a covenant with the king in the house of G d. And he said to them: Behold, the son of the king will reign, as the L rd spoke of the sons of David.


###### II Chronicles 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:4)


זֶ֥ה הַדָּבָ֖ר אֲשֶׁ֣ר תַּעֲשׂ֑וּ הַשְּׁלִשִׁ֨ית מִכֶּ֜ם בָּאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֗ת לַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וְלַלְוִיִּ֔ם לְשֹׁעֲרֵ֖י הַסִּפִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the thing that you shall do; A third part of you who come in on the sabbath, of the priests and of the Levites, shall be on duty at the gates;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the thing that you shall do: A third of you [(of the alternating priestly and Levitical watches, one coming in and one going out each Sabbath)], (a third of) those coming in for the Sabbath, of the priests and the Levites, shall be watchers at the door-posts.


###### II Chronicles 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:5)


וְהַשְּׁלִשִׁית֙ בְּבֵ֣ית הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְהַשְּׁלִשִׁ֖ית בְּשַׁ֣עַר הַיְס֑וֹד וְכׇ֨ל־הָעָ֔ם בְּחַצְר֖וֹת בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and a third part shall be at the king’s house; and a third part at the bottom gate: and all the people shall be in the courts of the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a third in the house of the king, and a third at the Yesod gate, and all the people, at the courts of the house of the L rd [viz. II Kings 11].


###### II Chronicles 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:6)


וְאַל־יָב֣וֹא בֵית־יְהֹוָ֗ה כִּ֤י אִם־הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וְהַמְשָׁרְתִ֣ים לַלְוִיִּ֔ם הֵ֥מָּה יָבֹ֖אוּ כִּי־קֹ֣דֶשׁ הֵ֑מָּה וְכׇ֨ל־הָעָ֔ם יִשְׁמְר֖וּ מִשְׁמֶ֥רֶת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But let none come into the house of the Lord, except the priests, and those of the Levites who minister; they may go in, for they are holy: but all the people shall keep the Lord’s charge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there shall come to the house of the L rd only the priests and the Levitical ministrants. They shall come, for they are consecrated [(to the Temple)]. And all the people shall keep the keeping of the L rd [(not to enter areas forbidden to them)].


###### II Chronicles 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:7)


וְהִקִּ֩יפוּ֩ הַלְוִיִּ֨ם אֶת־הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ סָבִ֗יב אִ֚ישׁ וְכֵלָ֣יו בְּיָד֔וֹ וְהַבָּ֥א אֶל־הַבַּ֖יִת יוּמָ֑ת וִֽהְי֥וּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בְּבֹא֥וֹ וּבְצֵאתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Levites shall surround the king, every man with his weapons in his hand; and whoever else comes into the house, he shall be put to death: but be with the king when he comes in, and when he goes out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites shall surround the king, each with his weapons in his hand. And one [(a stranger)] who comes to the house shall be put to death. And they shall be with the king in his coming in and in his going out.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:8)


וַיַּעֲשׂ֨וּ הַלְוִיִּ֜ם וְכׇל־יְהוּדָ֗ה כְּכֹ֣ל אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּה֮ יְהוֹיָדָ֣ע הַכֹּהֵן֒ וַיִּקְחוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־אֲנָשָׁ֔יו בָּאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת עִ֖ם יוֹצְאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֑ת כִּ֣י לֹ֥א פָטַ֛ר יְהוֹיָדָ֥ע הַכֹּהֵ֖ן אֶת־הַֽמַּחְלְקֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the Levites and all Yehuda did according to all the things that Yehoyada the priest had commanded, and every man took his men who were due to come in on the sabbath, with those who were due to go out on the sabbath: for Yehoyada the priest did not dismiss the duty watches.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites and all of Judah did according to all they were charged by Yehoyada the priest. And they took, each, his men, those coming in for the Sabbath and those leaving for the Sabbath, for Yehoyada the priest did not dismiss the [(outgoing)] divisions.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:10)

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they brought forth the king's son, and they placed upon him the crown and the testimony [(the Torah scroll)], and they made him king. And Yehoyada and his sons anointed him, and they said: Long live the king!


###### II Chronicles 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:12)

Now when ῾Atalyahu heard the noise of the people running and praising the king, she came to the people into the house of the Lord:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 23:13

וַתֵּ֡רֶא וְהִנֵּ֣ה הַמֶּ֩לֶךְ֩ עוֹמֵ֨ד עַֽל־עַמּוּד֜וֹ בַּמָּב֗וֹא וְהַשָּׂרִ֣ים וְהַחֲצֹצְרוֹת֮ עַל־הַמֶּ֒לֶךְ֒ וְכׇל־עַ֨ם הָאָ֜רֶץ שָׂמֵ֗חַ וְתוֹקֵ֙עַ֙ בַּחֲצֹ֣צְר֔וֹת וְהַמְשֽׁוֹרְרִים֙ בִּכְלֵ֣י הַשִּׁ֔יר וּמוֹדִיעִ֖ים לְהַלֵּ֑ל וַתִּקְרַ֤ע עֲתַלְיָ֙הוּ֙ אֶת־בְּגָדֶ֔יהָ וַתֹּ֖אמֶר קֶ֥שֶׁר קָֽשֶׁר׃{ס}

and she looked, and behold, the king stood at his pillar at the entrance, and the princes and the trumpeters by the king: and all the people of the land rejoiced, and sounded trumpets, also the singers with instruments of music leading the celebration. Then ῾Atalyahu rent her clothes, and cried, Treason, Treason.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And she saw, and, behold, the king standing on his [(the king's)] platform in the entrance [(to the Temple court)], and the officers and the trumpeters by the king, and all the people of the land rejoicing and blowing trumpets, and the singers with their instruments leading the laudation. And Atalyah rent her garments and cried out: "Conspiracy!" "Conspiracy!"

וַיּוֹצֵא֩ יְהוֹיָדָ֨ע הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֶת־שָׂרֵ֥י הַמֵּא֣וֹת**׀** פְּקוּדֵ֣י הַחַ֗יִל וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲלֵהֶם֙ הוֹצִיא֙וּהָ֙ אֶל־מִבֵּ֣ית הַשְּׂדֵר֔וֹת וְהַבָּ֥א אַחֲרֶ֖יהָ יוּמַ֣ת בֶּחָ֑רֶב כִּ֚י אָמַ֣ר הַכֹּהֵ֔ן לֹ֥א תְמִית֖וּהָ בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃

Then Yehoyada the priest brought out the captains of hundreds who were set over the host, and said to them, Bring her out between the ranks: and whoever follows her, let him be slain with the sword. For the priest said, Do not slay her in the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoyada the priest went out to the captains of the hundreds, appointed over the army, and he said to them: Take her out, outside the sederoth [(and from there to the king's house. Do not allow her to leave from the Temple court to the city, lest she find confederates)]; and if anyone comes after her [(to her assistance)], let him be slain by the sword. For the priest said: Do not put her to death (in) the house of the L rd.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיִּכְרֹ֤ת יְהֽוֹיָדָע֙ בְּרִ֔ית בֵּינ֕וֹ וּבֵ֥ין כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם וּבֵ֣ין הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ לִהְי֥וֹת לְעָ֖ם לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃

And Yehoyada made a covenant between him, and between all the people, and between the king, that they should be the Lord’s people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיָּבֹ֨אוּ כׇל־הָעָ֤ם בֵּית־הַבַּ֙עַל֙ וַֽיִּתְּצֻ֔הוּ וְאֶת־מִזְבְּחֹתָ֥יו וְאֶת־צְלָמָ֖יו שִׁבֵּ֑רוּ וְאֵ֗ת מַתָּן֙ כֹּהֵ֣ן הַבַּ֔עַל הָרְג֖וּ לִפְנֵ֥י הַֽמִּזְבְּחֽוֹת׃

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/23:18)


וַיָּ֩שֶׂם֩ יְהוֹיָדָ֨ע פְּקֻדּ֜וֹת בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֗ה בְּיַ֨ד הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים הַלְוִיִּם֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָלַ֣ק דָּוִיד֮ עַל־בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָה֒ לְֽהַעֲל֞וֹת עֹל֣וֹת יְהֹוָ֗ה כַּכָּת֛וּב בְּתוֹרַ֥ת מֹשֶׁ֖ה בְּשִׂמְחָ֣ה וּבְשִׁ֑יר עַ֖ל יְדֵ֥י דָוִֽיד׃

And Yehoyada appointed the offices of the house of the Lord under the hand of the priests the Levites, whom David had given charge over the house of the Lord, to offer the burnt offerings of the Lord, as it is written in the Tora of Moshe, with rejoicing and with singing, as ordained by David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoyada placed watchers over the house of the L rd [(Atalya having abolished the order of the watches of the Temple)], by the hand of the priests, the Levites, whom David had divided [(into watches)] over the house of the L rd to sacrifice the burnt-offerings of the L rd, as written in the Torah of Moses, with joy and song, (as set forth) by David.

And he set the gatekeepers at the gates of the house of the Lord, so that no one who was unclean in anything should enter in.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he stationed the gatekeepers at the gates of the house of the L rd that no [ritually] unclean person of any kind enter.

וַיִּקַּ֣ח אֶת־שָׂרֵ֣י הַמֵּא֡וֹת וְאֶת־הָאַדִּירִים֩ וְאֶת־הַמּוֹשְׁלִ֨ים בָּעָ֜ם וְאֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־עַ֣ם הָאָ֗רֶץ וַיּ֤וֹרֶד אֶת־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ מִבֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיָּבֹ֛אוּ בְּתֽוֹךְ־שַׁ֥עַר הָעֶלְי֖וֹן בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיּוֹשִׁ֙יבוּ֙ אֶת־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ עַ֖ל כִּסֵּ֥א הַמַּמְלָכָֽה׃

And he took the captains of hundreds, and the nobles, and the governors of the people, and all the people of the land, and brought down the king from the house of the Lord: and they came through the upper gate into the king’s house, and set the king upon the throne of the kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he took the captains of the hundreds and the mighty men and the rulers of the people, and he brought down the king from the house of the L rd, and they came within the upper gate (into) the king's house, and they sat the king on the throne of kingdom.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the people of the land rejoiced: and the city was quiet, after they had slain ῾Atalyahu with the sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 24

###### II Chronicles 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:1)


בֶּן־שֶׁ֤בַע שָׁנִים֙ יֹאָ֣שׁ בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְאַרְבָּעִ֣ים שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ צִבְיָ֖ה מִבְּאֵ֥ר שָֽׁבַע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yo᾽ash was seven years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for forty years in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was Żivya of Be᾽er-sheva.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Seven years old was Yoash when he [began] to reign, and forty years did he reign in Jerusalem. His mother's name was Tzivyah of Be'ersheva.


###### II Chronicles 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:2)


וַיַּ֧עַשׂ יוֹאָ֛שׁ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כׇּל־יְמֵ֖י יְהוֹיָדָ֥ע הַכֹּהֵֽן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yo᾽ash did that which was right in the sight of the Lord all the days of Yehoyada the priest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yoash did what was just in the eyes of the L rd all the days of Yehoyada the priest.


###### II Chronicles 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:3)


וַיִּשָּׂא־ל֥וֹ יְהוֹיָדָ֖ע נָשִׁ֣ים שְׁתָּ֑יִם וַיּ֖וֹלֶד בָּנִ֥ים וּבָנֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yehoyada took for himself two wives; and he begot sons and daughters.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yehoyada took for himself two wives and he begot sons and daughters.


###### II Chronicles 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:4)


וַיְהִ֖י אַחֲרֵי־כֵ֑ן הָיָה֙ עִם־לֵ֣ב יוֹאָ֔שׁ לְחַדֵּ֖שׁ אֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass after this, that Yo᾽ash was minded to repair the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, after this, that it was in the heart of Yoash to renew the house of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:5)


וַיִּקְבֹּץ֮ אֶת־הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים וְהַלְוִיִּם֒ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לָהֶ֡ם צְא֣וּ לְעָרֵ֪י יְהוּדָ֟ה וְקִבְצוּ֩ מִכׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֨ל כֶּ֜סֶף לְחַזֵּ֣ק**׀** אֶת־בֵּ֣ית אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֗ם מִדֵּ֤י שָׁנָה֙ בְּשָׁנָ֔ה וְאַתֶּ֖ם תְּמַהֲר֣וּ לַדָּבָ֑ר וְלֹ֥א מִהֲר֖וּ הַלְוִיִּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he gathered the priests and the Levites, and said to them, Go out to the cities of Yehuda, and collect money of all Yisra᾽el to repair the house of your God from year to year, and see that you hasten the matter. But the Levites did not hasten it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he gathered the priests and the Levites and said to them: Go to the cities of Judah and collect from all of Israel money to strengthen the house of your G d from year to year. And you, haste to [do] this thing. But the Levites did not hasten.


###### II Chronicles 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:6)


וַיִּקְרָ֣א הַמֶּ֘לֶךְ֮ לִיהוֹיָדָ֣ע הָרֹאשׁ֒ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֗וֹ מַדּ֙וּעַ֙ לֹא־דָרַ֣שְׁתָּ עַל־הַלְוִיִּ֔ם לְהָבִ֞יא מִיהוּדָ֣ה וּמִירוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם אֶת־מַשְׂאַת֙ מֹשֶׁ֣ה עֶבֶד־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהַקָּהָ֖ל לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל לְאֹ֖הֶל הָעֵדֽוּת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king called for Yehoyada the chief, and said to him. Why hast thou not required of the Levites to bring in out of Yehuda and out of Yerushalayim the tax of Moshe the servant of the Lord, and of the congregation of Yisra᾽el, for the tabernacle of the Testimony?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king called to Yehoyada, the head, and said to him: Why did you not demand of the Levites to bring from Judah and from Jerusalem the levy of Moses, the servant of the L rd [viz. II Kings 12:5], and [what] the congregation of Israel [was wont to bring] for the tent of testimony?

כִּ֤י עֲתַלְיָ֙הוּ֙ הַמִּרְשַׁ֔עַת בָּנֶ֥יהָ פָרְצ֖וּ אֶת־בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְגַם֙ כׇּל־קׇדְשֵׁ֣י בֵית־יְהֹוָ֔ה עָשׂ֖וּ לַבְּעָלִֽים׃

For the sons of ῾Atalyahu, that wicked woman, had broken up the house of God; and they had also bestowed all the dedicated things of the house of the Lord upon the Be῾alim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For Atalyahu, the wicked one — her sons had breached the house of G d, and also, all the consecrated things of the house of the L rd they had used for the ba'alim.

וַיֹּ֣אמֶר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֖וּ אֲר֣וֹן אֶחָ֑ד וַֽיִּתְּנֻ֛הוּ בְּשַׁ֥עַר בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה חֽוּצָה׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיִּתְּנוּ־ק֞וֹל בִּיהוּדָ֣ה וּבִירוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם לְהָבִ֤יא לַֽיהֹוָה֙ מַשְׂאַ֞ת מֹשֶׁ֧ה עֶבֶד־הָאֱלֹהִ֛ים עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בַּמִּדְבָּֽר׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיִּשְׂמְח֥וּ כׇל־הַשָּׂרִ֖ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֑ם וַיָּבִ֛יאוּ וַיַּשְׁלִ֥יכוּ לָאָר֖וֹן עַד־לְכַלֵּֽה׃

###### II Chronicles 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:11)


וַיְהִ֡י בְּעֵת֩ יָבִ֨יא אֶת־הָאָר֜וֹן אֶל־פְּקֻדַּ֣ת הַמֶּ֘לֶךְ֮ בְּיַ֣ד הַלְוִיִּם֒ וְכִרְאוֹתָ֞ם כִּֽי־רַ֣ב הַכֶּ֗סֶף וּבָ֨א סוֹפֵ֤ר הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וּפְקִיד֙ כֹּהֵ֣ן הָרֹ֔אשׁ וִיעָ֙רוּ֙ אֶת־הָ֣אָר֔וֹן וְיִשָּׂאֻ֖הוּ וִישִׁיבֻ֣הוּ אֶל־מְקֹמ֑וֹ כֹּ֤ה עָשׂוּ֙ לְי֣וֹם׀בְּי֔וֹם וַיַּאַסְפוּ־כֶ֖סֶף לָרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now it came to pass, that whenever the chest was brought to the king’s officers by the hand of the Levites, and when they saw that there was much money, the king’s scribe and the high priest’s officer came and emptied the chest, and took it, and carried it back to its place. Thus they did day by day, and they gathered a great sum of money.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when they brought the chest by the king's command to the Levites, and when they saw that the money had accumulated, that the king's scribe would come and the high-priest's deputy, and they would empty out the chest and carry it back to its place. So would they do from day to day, and they gathered money in abundance.


###### II Chronicles 24:12

וַיִּתְּנֵ֨הוּ הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ וִיהוֹיָדָ֗ע אֶל־עוֹשֵׂה֙ מְלֶ֙אכֶת֙ עֲבוֹדַ֣ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיִּהְי֤וּ שֹֽׂכְרִים֙ חֹצְבִ֣ים וְחָרָשִׁ֔ים לְחַדֵּ֖שׁ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וְ֠גַ֠ם לְחָרָשֵׁ֤י בַרְזֶל֙ וּנְחֹ֔שֶׁת לְחַזֵּ֖ק אֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king and Yehoyada gave it to such as did the work of the service of the house of the Lord, and hired masons and carpenters to repair the house of the Lord, and also workers in iron and brass to mend the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king and Yehoyada gave it to the doers of the work of the service of the house of the L rd. And they would hire [stone-] cutters and carpenters to renew the house of the L rd, and also workers in iron and copper to strengthen the house of the L rd.

וַֽיַּעֲשׂוּ֙ עֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה וַתַּ֧עַל אֲרוּכָ֛ה לַמְּלָאכָ֖ה בְּיָדָ֑ם וַֽיַּעֲמִ֜ידוּ אֶת־בֵּ֧ית הָאֱלֹהִ֛ים עַל־מַתְכֻּנְתּ֖וֹ וַֽיְאַמְּצֻֽהוּ׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 24:14

וּֽכְכַלּוֹתָ֡ם הֵבִ֣יאוּ לִפְנֵי֩ הַמֶּ֨לֶךְ וִיהוֹיָדָ֜ע אֶת־שְׁאָ֣ר הַכֶּ֗סֶף וַיַּעֲשֵׂ֨הוּ כֵלִ֤ים לְבֵית־יְהֹוָה֙ כְּלֵ֣י שָׁרֵ֔ת וְהַעֲל֣וֹת וְכַפּ֔וֹת וּכְלֵ֥י זָהָ֖ב וָכָ֑סֶף וַ֠יִּהְי֠וּ מַעֲלִ֨ים עֹל֤וֹת בְּבֵית־יְהֹוָה֙ תָּמִ֔יד כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֥י יְהוֹיָדָֽע׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when they had finished it, they brought the rest of the money before the king and Yehoyada, and of it were made vessels for the house of the Lord, vessels for service and for offering, and spoons, and vessels of gold and silver. And they offered burnt offerings in the house of the Lord continually all the days of Yehoyada.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when they finished, they brought before the king and Yehoyada the rest of the money, and he made of it [i.e., he used it for] vessels for the house of the L rd: ministering vessels and pestles and spoons and vessels of gold and silver. And they sacrificed burnt-offerings continually in the house of the L rd all the days of Yehoyada.

וַיִּקְבְּרֻ֥הוּ בְעִיר־דָּוִ֖יד עִם־הַמְּלָכִ֑ים כִּֽי־עָשָׂ֤ה טוֹבָה֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְעִ֥ם הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים וּבֵיתֽוֹ׃{פ}

וְאַחֲרֵ֥י מוֹת֙ יְה֣וֹיָדָ֔ע בָּ֚אוּ שָׂרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֖וּ לַמֶּ֑לֶךְ אָ֛ז שָׁמַ֥ע הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃

וַיַּעַזְב֗וּ אֶת־בֵּ֤ית יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֽוֹתֵיהֶ֔ם וַיַּעַבְד֥וּ אֶת־הָאֲשֵׁרִ֖ים וְאֶת־הָעֲצַבִּ֑ים וַֽיְהִי־קֶ֗צֶף עַל־יְהוּדָה֙ וִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם בְּאַשְׁמָתָ֖ם זֹֽאת׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they abandoned the house of the L rd, the G d of their fathers, and they served the asherim and the idols. And there was wrath over Judah and Jerusalem in this their guilt.


###### II Chronicles 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:19)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֤ח בָּהֶם֙ נְבִאִ֔ים לַהֲשִׁיבָ֖ם אֶל־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיָּעִ֥ידוּ בָ֖ם וְלֹ֥א הֶאֱזִֽינוּ׃{ס}

###### II Chronicles 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:20)


וְר֣וּחַ אֱלֹהִ֗ים לָֽבְשָׁה֙ אֶת־זְכַרְיָה֙ בֶּן־יְהוֹיָדָ֣ע הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֖ד מֵעַ֣ל לָעָ֑ם וַיֹּ֨אמֶר לָהֶ֜ם כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר הָאֱלֹהִ֗ים לָמָה֩ אַתֶּ֨ם עֹבְרִ֜ים אֶת־מִצְוֺ֤ת יְהֹוָה֙ וְלֹ֣א תַצְלִ֔יחוּ כִּֽי־עֲזַבְתֶּ֥ם אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיַּעֲזֹ֥ב אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the spirit of God came upon Zekharya the son of Yehoyada the priest, and he stood above the people, and said to them, Thus says God, Why do you transgress the commandments of the Lord, though you cannot succeed? because you have forsaken the Lord, he has also forsaken you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the spirit of G d clothed Zecharyah the son of Yehoyada the priest and he stood above the people and said to them: Thus says G d: Why would you transgress the mitzvoth of the L rd and not prosper? For you have forsaken the L rd, and He has forsaken you.


###### II Chronicles 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:21)


וַיִּקְשְׁר֣וּ עָלָ֔יו וַיִּרְגְּמֻ֥הוּ אֶ֖בֶן בְּמִצְוַ֣ת הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ בַּחֲצַ֖ר בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they conspired against him, and stoned him with stones at the commandment of the king in the court of the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they banded against him and stoned him by the king's command in the court of the house of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:22)


וְלֹא־זָכַ֞ר יוֹאָ֣שׁ הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ הַחֶ֙סֶד֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֜ה יְהוֹיָדָ֤ע אָבִיו֙ עִמּ֔וֹ וַֽיַּהֲרֹ֖ג אֶת־בְּנ֑וֹ וּכְמוֹת֣וֹ אָמַ֔ר יֵ֥רֶא יְהֹוָ֖ה וְיִדְרֹֽשׁ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus Yo᾽ash the king did not remember the faithful love which Yehoyada his father had shown him, but he slew his son. And when he died, he said, May the Lord see and revenge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Yoash did not remember the lovingkindness that his father Yehoyada had done with him, and he killed his son, who, in dying, said: "Let the L rd see and demand [my blood]!"


###### II Chronicles 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:23)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** לִתְקוּפַ֣ת הַשָּׁנָ֗ה עָלָ֣ה עָלָיו֮ חֵ֣יל אֲרָם֒ וַיָּבֹ֗אוּ אֶל־יְהוּדָה֙ וִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַיַּשְׁחִ֛יתוּ אֶת־כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֥י הָעָ֖ם מֵעָ֑ם וְכׇל־שְׁלָלָ֥ם שִׁלְּח֖וּ לְמֶ֥לֶךְ דַּרְמָֽשֶׂק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass at the end of the year, that the host of Aram came up against him: and they came to Yehuda and Yerushalayim, and destroyed all the princes of the people from among the people, and sent all their spoil to the king of Darmeseq.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, at the turn of the year, that the army of Aram went up against him [(Yoash)], and they came to Judah and Jerusalem, and they destroyed all the officers of the people from the (midst of) the people, and all their spoils they sent to the king to Damascus.


###### II Chronicles 24:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:24)


כִּי֩ בְמִצְעַ֨ר אֲנָשִׁ֜ים בָּ֣אוּ**׀** חֵ֣יל אֲרָ֗ם וַֽיהֹוָה֙ נָתַ֨ן בְּיָדָ֥ם חַ֙יִל֙ לָרֹ֣ב מְאֹ֔ד כִּ֣י עָֽזְב֔וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֑ם וְאֶת־יוֹאָ֖שׁ עָשׂ֥וּ שְׁפָטִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though the army of Aram came with a small company of men, the Lord delivered a very great host into their hand, because they had forsaken the Lord God of their fathers. So they executed judgment against Yo᾽ash.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For with a small number of men did the army of Aram come, and the L rd delivered into their hand a very large army. For they had forsaken the L rd, the G d of their fathers; and upon Yoash they executed judgment.


###### II Chronicles 24:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:25)


וּבְלֶכְתָּ֣ם מִמֶּ֗נּוּ כִּי־עָזְב֣וּ אֹתוֹ֮ בְּמַחֲלֻיִ֣ים רַבִּים֒ הִתְקַשְּׁר֨וּ עָלָ֜יו עֲבָדָ֗יו בִּדְמֵי֙ בְּנֵי֙ יְהֽוֹיָדָ֣ע הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וַיַּהַרְגֻ֥הוּ עַל־מִטָּת֖וֹ וַיָּמֹ֑ת וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֙הוּ֙ בְּעִ֣יר דָּוִ֔יד וְלֹ֥א קְבָרֻ֖הוּ בְּקִבְר֥וֹת הַמְּלָכִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when they were departed from him, (for they left him in great diseases,) his own servants conspired against him for the blood of the sons of Yehoyada the priest, and slew him in his bed, and he died: and they buried him in the city of David, but they did not bury him in the tombs of the kings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when they went from him (for they left him in [the throes of] many afflictions), his servants banded against him for [revenge of] the blood of the sons of Yehoyada the priest, and they killed him on his bed and he died. And they buried him in the city of David, but they did not bury him in the crypts of the kings.


###### II Chronicles 24:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:26)


וְאֵ֖לֶּה הַמִּתְקַשְּׁרִ֣ים עָלָ֑יו זָבָ֗ד בֶּן־שִׁמְעָת֙ הָעַמּוֹנִ֔ית וִיה֣וֹזָבָ֔ד בֶּן־שִׁמְרִ֖ית הַמּוֹאָבִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And these are they who conspired against him; Zavad the son of Shim῾at an ῾Ammonite woman, and Yehozavad the son of Shim῾at a Mo᾽avite woman.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these are the ones who banded against him: Zavad the son of Shimath the Amonitess and Yehozavad the son of Shimrith the Moavitess.


###### II Chronicles 24:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/24:27)


וּבָנָ֞יו (ורב) [יִ֧רֶב] הַמַּשָּׂ֣א עָלָ֗יו וִיסוֹד֙ בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים הִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֔ים עַל־מִדְרַ֖שׁ סֵ֣פֶר הַמְּלָכִ֑ים וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ אֲמַצְיָ֥הוּ בְנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now concerning his sons, and the greatness of the burdens laid upon him, and the repairing of the house of God, behold, they are written in the commentary of the book of the kings. And Amażyahu his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [accounts of] his sons and the many prophecies concerning him and the founding [by him] of the house of G d — behold, they are recorded in the midrash in the book of [chronicles] of the kings. And Amatzyah his son reigned in his place.



## Chapter 25

###### II Chronicles 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:1)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְחָמֵ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ מָלַ֣ךְ אֲמַצְיָ֔הוּ וְעֶשְׂרִ֤ים וָתֵ֙שַׁע֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ יְהוֹעַדָּ֖ן מִירוּשָׁלָֽיִם׃

Amażyahu was twenty five years old when he began to reign, and he reigned twenty nine years in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was Yeho῾addan of Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Amatzyahu was twenty-five years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for twenty-five years in Jerusalem. His mother's name was Yehoadan of Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה רַ֕ק לֹ֖א בְּלֵבָ֥ב שָׁלֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did that which was right in the sight of the Lord, but not with a perfect heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was just in the eyes of the L rd, but not with a whole heart.


###### II Chronicles 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:3)


וַיְהִ֕י כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר חָזְקָ֥ה הַמַּמְלָכָ֖ה עָלָ֑יו וַֽיַּהֲרֹג֙ אֶת־עֲבָדָ֔יו הַמַּכִּ֖ים אֶת־הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ אָבִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now it came to pass, when the kingdom was established to him, that he slew his servants who had killed the king his father.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when the kingdom became secure with him, that he smote his servants, who had smitten the king, his father.


###### II Chronicles 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:4)


וְאֶת־בְּנֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֣א הֵמִ֑ית כִּ֣י כַכָּת֣וּב בַּתּוֹרָ֡ה בְּסֵ֣פֶר מֹשֶׁה֩ אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֨ה יְהֹוָ֜ה לֵאמֹ֗ר לֹא־יָמ֨וּתוּ אָב֤וֹת עַל־בָּנִים֙ וּבָנִים֙ לֹא־יָמ֣וּתוּ עַל־אָב֔וֹת כִּ֛י אִ֥ישׁ בְּחֶטְא֖וֹ יָמֽוּתוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he did not slay their children, but did as it is written in the Tora, in the book of Moshe, where the Lord commanded, saying, Fathers shall not die for children, nor shall children die for fathers, but every man shall die for his own sin.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But their sons he did not put to death, as it is written in the Torah of Moses that the L rd commanded, saying [Deuteronomy 24:16]: "Fathers shall not be put to death because of the sons, and sons shall not be put to death because of the fathers, but each man shall be put to death for his (own) sin."


###### II Chronicles 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:5)


וַיִּקְבֹּ֤ץ אֲמַצְיָ֙הוּ֙ אֶת־יְהוּדָ֔ה וַיַּעֲמִידֵ֣ם לְבֵית־אָב֗וֹת לְשָׂרֵ֤י הָאֲלָפִים֙ וּלְשָׂרֵ֣י הַמֵּא֔וֹת לְכׇל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וּבִנְיָמִ֑ן וַֽיִּפְקְדֵ֗ם לְמִבֶּ֨ן עֶשְׂרִ֤ים שָׁנָה֙ וָמַ֔עְלָה וַיִּמְצָאֵ֗ם שְׁלֹשׁ־מֵא֨וֹת אֶ֤לֶף בָּחוּר֙ יוֹצֵ֣א צָבָ֔א אֹחֵ֖ז רֹ֥מַח וְצִנָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover Amażyahu gathered Yehuda together, and made them captains over thousands, and captains over hundreds, according to the houses of their fathers, throughout all Yehuda and Binyamin: and he numbered them from twenty years old and upwards, and found them three hundred thousand choice men, able to go out to war, that could handle spear and shield.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Amatzyahu gathered Judah, and he stood them up by the fathers' houses, by the officers of thousands and the officers of hundreds, for all of Judah and Benjamin. And he counted them from twenty years old and above, and he found them to be three hundred thousand select (men), wielding spear and shield.


###### II Chronicles 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:6)


וַיִּשְׂכֹּ֣ר מִיִּשְׂרָאֵ֗ל מֵ֥אָה אֶ֛לֶף גִּבּ֥וֹר חָ֖יִל בְּמֵאָ֥ה כִכַּר־כָּֽסֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He hired also a hundred thousand mighty men at arms out of Yisra᾽el for a hundred talents of silver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he hired from Israel one hundred thousand warriors for a hundred talents of silver.


###### II Chronicles 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:7)


וְאִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֗ים בָּ֤א אֵלָיו֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הַמֶּ֕לֶךְ אַל־יָב֥וֹא עִמְּךָ֖ צְבָ֣א יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֣י אֵ֤ין יְהֹוָה֙ עִם־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כֹּ֖ל בְּנֵ֥י אֶפְרָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But there came a man of God to him, saying, O king, let not the army of Yisra᾽el go with thee; for the Lord is not with Yisra᾽el, with all these children of Efrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a man of G d came to him and said: O king, let the army of Israel not come with you. For the L rd is not with Israel, (with) all the sons of Ephraim.


###### II Chronicles 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:8)


כִּ֚י אִם־בֹּ֣א אַתָּ֔ה עֲשֵׂ֖ה חֲזַ֣ק לַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה יַכְשִֽׁילְךָ֤ הָאֱלֹהִים֙ לִפְנֵ֣י אוֹיֵ֔ב כִּ֥י יֶשׁ־כֹּ֛חַ בֵּאלֹהִ֖ים לַעְז֥וֹר וּלְהַכְשִֽׁיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if thou wilt go, and even if thou engage valiantly in the battle: God shall make thee fall before the enemy: for God has power to help, and to cast down.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For if you do come (with them), you may strengthen yourself for war, (but) G d will cause you to stumble before the foe. For there is power in G d to help or to hinder.


###### II Chronicles 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:9)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲמַצְיָ֙הוּ֙ לְאִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וּמַֽה־לַּעֲשׂוֹת֙ לִמְאַ֣ת הַכִּכָּ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לִגְד֣וּד יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים יֵ֚שׁ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה לָ֥תֶת לְךָ֖ הַרְבֵּ֥ה מִזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Amażyahu said to the man of God, But what shall we do for the hundred talents which I have given to the army of Yisra᾽el? And the man of God answered, The Lord is able to give thee much more than this.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Amatzyahu said to the man of G d: But what is to be done about the hundred talents that I have given for the troop of Israel? And the man of G d said: The L rd has the power to give you much more than this.


###### II Chronicles 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:10)


וַיַּבְדִּילֵ֣ם אֲמַצְיָ֗הוּ לְהַגְּדוּד֙ אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֤א אֵלָיו֙ מֵאֶפְרַ֔יִם לָלֶ֖כֶת לִמְקוֹמָ֑ם וַיִּ֨חַר אַפָּ֤ם מְאֹד֙ בִּֽיהוּדָ֔ה וַיָּשׁ֥וּבוּ לִמְקוֹמָ֖ם בׇּחֳרִי־אָֽף׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Amażyahu separated them, namely that army that was come to him out of Efrayim, and bade them go home again: so that their anger burned strongly against Yehuda, and they returned home in great anger.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Amatzyahu separated the troop that had come to him from Ephraim to return to their place. And their wrath burned greatly against Judah, and they returned to their places in burning wrath.


###### II Chronicles 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:11)


וַאֲמַצְיָ֙הוּ֙ הִתְחַזַּ֔ק וַיִּנְהַג֙ אֶת־עַמּ֔וֹ וַיֵּ֖לֶךְ גֵּ֣יא הַמֶּ֑לַח וַיַּ֥ךְ אֶת־בְּנֵֽי־שֵׂעִ֖יר עֲשֶׂ֥רֶת אֲלָפִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Amażyahu strengthened himself, and led his people, and went to the valley of salt, and smote ten thousand of the children of Se῾ir.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:12)


וַעֲשֶׂ֨רֶת אֲלָפִ֜ים חַיִּ֗ים שָׁבוּ֙ בְּנֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וַיְבִיא֖וּם לְרֹ֣אשׁ הַסָּ֑לַע וַיַּשְׁלִיכ֥וּם מֵֽרֹאשׁ־הַסֶּ֖לַע וְכֻלָּ֥ם נִבְקָֽעוּ׃

And another ten thousand did the children of Yehuda carry away alive captive, and brought them to the top of the rock, and they cast them down from the top of the rock, so that they all were broken in pieces.

###### II Chronicles 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/25:13)


וּבְנֵ֣י הַגְּד֗וּד אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵשִׁ֤יב אֲמַצְיָ֙הוּ֙ מִלֶּ֤כֶת עִמּוֹ֙ לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וַֽיִּפְשְׁטוּ֙ בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה מִשֹּׁמְר֖וֹן וְעַד־בֵּ֣ית חוֹר֑וֹן וַיַּכּ֤וּ מֵהֶם֙ שְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת אֲלָפִ֔ים וַיָּבֹ֖זּוּ בִּזָּ֥ה רַבָּֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the soldiers of the army which Amażyahu sent back, so that they should not go with him to battle, fell upon the cities of Yehuda, from Shomeron as far as Bet-ĥoron, and smote three thousand of them, and took much spoil.


###### II Chronicles 25:14

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, after Amatzyahu came back from smiting the Edomites, that he brought back the gods of the sons of Seir, and he set them up for himself as gods, and to them did he bow down, and to them did he burn incense.


###### II Chronicles 25:15

וַיִּֽחַר־אַ֥ף יְהֹוָ֖ה בַּאֲמַצְיָ֑הוּ וַיִּשְׁלַ֤ח אֵלָיו֙ נָבִ֔יא וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֗וֹ לָ֤מָּה דָרַ֙שְׁתָּ֙ אֶת־אֱלֹהֵ֣י הָעָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־הִצִּ֥ילוּ אֶת־עַמָּ֖ם מִיָּדֶֽךָ׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the wrath of the L rd burned against Amatzyahu, and he sent him a prophet, who said to him: Why have you sought out the gods of the people, who could not save their people from your hand!

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when he spoke to him that he said to him: Have we made you an adviser to the king? Forbear! Why should they smite you? And the prophet forbore, and he said: I know that the L rd has counseled to destroy you because you have done this and not heeded my counsel.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yoash, king of Israel, sent to Amatzyahu, king of Judah, saying: The thornbush in the Levanon [(Shechem, the son of Chamor)] sent to the cedar in the Levanon [(Jacob)], saying: Give your daughter to my son as a wife. And the animals of the Levanon passed by and trod down the thornbush [(i.e., the sons of Jacob despoiled the slain [(You, too, in your haughtiness, will be trodden down by my cohorts])].

## Chapter 26

###### II Chronicles 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:1)


וַיִּקְח֞וּ כׇּל־עַ֤ם יְהוּדָה֙ אֶת־עֻזִּיָּ֔הוּ וְה֕וּא בֶּן־שֵׁ֥שׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה שָׁנָ֑ה וַיַּמְלִ֣יכוּ אֹת֔וֹ תַּ֖חַת אָבִ֥יו אֲמַצְיָֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then all the people of Yehuda took ῾Uzziyyahu, who was sixteen years old, and made him king in the place of his father Amażyahu.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the people of Judah took Uzziyahu when he was sixteen years old, and they made him king in place of his father Amatzyahu.


###### II Chronicles 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:2)


ה֚וּא בָּנָ֣ה אֶת־אֵיל֔וֹת וַיְשִׁיבֶ֖הָ לִֽיהוּדָ֑ה אַחֲרֵ֥י שְׁכַֽב־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ עִם־אֲבֹתָֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
