**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He built up Eiloth and restored it to Judah after the king rested with his fathers.


###### II Chronicles 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:3)


בֶּן־שֵׁ֨שׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֤ה שָׁנָה֙ עֻזִּיָּ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וַחֲמִשִּׁ֤ים וּשְׁתַּ֙יִם֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ (יכיליה) [יְכׇלְיָ֖ה] מִן־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Sixteen years old was ῾Uzziyyahu when he began to reign, and he reigned for fifty two years in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was Yekholya of Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Uzziyahu was sixteen years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for fifty-two years in Jerusalem. His mother's name was Yechalyah of Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/26:4)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה אֲמַצְיָ֥הוּ אָבִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did that which was right in the sight of the Lord, according to all that his father Amażyahu had done.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was just in the eyes of the L rd, as all that Amatzyahu his father had done.


###### II Chronicles 26:5

## Chapter 27

###### II Chronicles 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:1)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְחָמֵ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ יוֹתָ֣ם בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְשֵׁשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ יְרוּשָׁ֖ה בַּת־צָדֽוֹק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yotam was twenty five years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for sixteen years in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was Yerusha, the daughter of Żadoq.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yotham was twenty-five years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned sixteen years in Jerusalem. His mother's name was Yerushah the daughter of Tzaddok.


###### II Chronicles 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:2)


וַיַּ֨עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֜ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה כְּכֹ֤ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂה֙ עֻזִּיָּ֣הוּ אָבִ֔יו רַ֕ק לֹא־בָ֖א אֶל־הֵיכַ֣ל יְהֹוָ֑ה וְע֥וֹד הָעָ֖ם מַשְׁחִיתִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did that which was right in the sight of the Lord, according to all that his father ῾Uzziyyahu had done: but he did not enter into the temple of the Lord, and the people still acted corruptly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was just in the eyes of the L rd, as all that his father Uzziyahu had done. Only, he did not come to the sanctuary of the L rd, and the people still dealt corruptly.


###### II Chronicles 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:3)


ה֗וּא בָּנָ֛ה אֶת־שַׁ֥עַר בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה הָעֶלְי֑וֹן וּבְחוֹמַ֥ת הָעֹ֛פֶל בָּנָ֖ה לָרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He built the high gate of the house of the Lord, and on the wall of the ῾Ofel he built much.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He built the upper gate of the house of the L rd, and he did much building in the wall of the Ophel.


###### II Chronicles 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:4)


וְעָרִ֥ים בָּנָ֖ה בְּהַר־יְהוּדָ֑ה וּבֶחֳרָשִׁ֣ים בָּנָ֔ה בִּירָנִיּ֖וֹת וּמִגְדָּלִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he built cities in the mountains of Yehuda, and in the forests he built castles and towers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he built cities in the mountain of Judah, and in the forests he built palaces and towers.


###### II Chronicles 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:5)


וְ֠ה֠וּא נִלְחַ֞ם עִם־מֶ֣לֶךְ בְּנֵֽי־עַמּוֹן֮ וַיֶּחֱזַ֣ק עֲלֵיהֶם֒ וַיִּתְּנוּ־ל֨וֹ בְנֵי־עַמּ֜וֹן בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַהִ֗יא מֵאָה֙ כִּכַּר־כֶּ֔סֶף וַעֲשֶׂ֨רֶת אֲלָפִ֤ים כֹּרִים֙ חִטִּ֔ים וּשְׂעוֹרִ֖ים עֲשֶׂ֣רֶת אֲלָפִ֑ים זֹ֗את הֵשִׁ֤יבוּ לוֹ֙ בְּנֵ֣י עַמּ֔וֹן וּבַשָּׁנָ֥ה הַשֵּׁנִ֖ית וְהַשְּׁלִשִֽׁית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he fought with the king of the children of ῾Ammon, and prevailed against them. And the children of ῾Ammon gave him in the same year a hundred talents of silver, and ten thousand measures of wheat, and ten thousand of barley. So much did the children of ῾Ammon pay to him, also in the second year, and the third.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he fought with the king of the children of Ammon and he overcame them. And the children of Ammon gave him in that year one hundred talents of silver and ten thousand kors of wheat and ten thousand of barley. This the children of Ammon rendered him, and (likewise) in the second year and the third.


###### II Chronicles 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:6)


וַיִּתְחַזֵּ֖ק יוֹתָ֑ם כִּ֚י הֵכִ֣ין דְּרָכָ֔יו לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Yotam became mighty, because he directed his ways aright before the Lord his God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yotham grew in strength, for he had set his deeds aright before the L rd his G d.


###### II Chronicles 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:7)


וְיֶ֙תֶר֙ דִּבְרֵ֣י יוֹתָ֔ם וְכׇל־מִלְחֲמֹתָ֖יו וּדְרָכָ֑יו הִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֔ים עַל־סֵ֥פֶר מַלְכֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וִיהוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the rest of the acts of Yotam, and all his wars, and his ways, lo, they are written in the book of the kings of Yisra᾽el and Yehuda.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the events (of the reign) of Yotham and all of his wars and his ways, behold, they are recorded in the book [of the chronicles] of the kings of Israel and Judah.


###### II Chronicles 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:8)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֧ים וְחָמֵ֛שׁ שָׁנָ֖ה הָיָ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֑וֹ וְשֵׁשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He was twenty five years old when he began to reign, and reigned for sixteen years in Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He was twenty-five years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned sixteen years in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/27:9)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֤ב יוֹתָם֙ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֔יו וַיִּקְבְּר֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ בְּעִ֣יר דָּוִ֑יד וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ אָחָ֥ז בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yotam slept with his fathers, and they buried him in the city of David: and Aĥaz his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yotham rested with his fathers and they buried him in the city of David, and Achaz his son reigned in his place.



## Chapter 28

###### II Chronicles 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:1)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֤ים שָׁנָה֙ אָחָ֣ז בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְשֵׁשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְלֹא־עָשָׂ֧ה הַיָּשָׁ֛ר בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה כְּדָוִ֥יד אָבִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Aĥaz was twenty years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for sixteen years in Yerushalayim: but he did not do that which was right in the sight of the Lord, like David his father:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Achaz was twenty years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned sixteen years in Jerusalem, and he did not do what was just in the eyes of the L rd as (had) David his father.


###### II Chronicles 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:2)


וַיֵּ֕לֶךְ בְּדַרְכֵ֖י מַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְגַ֧ם מַסֵּכ֛וֹת עָשָׂ֖ה לַבְּעָלִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for he followed the ways of the kings of Yisra᾽el, and made also molten images for the Be῾alim.

###### II Chronicles 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:3)


וְה֥וּא הִקְטִ֖יר בְּגֵ֣יא בֶן־הִנֹּ֑ם וַיַּבְעֵ֤ר אֶת־בָּנָיו֙ בָּאֵ֔שׁ כְּתֹֽעֲבוֹת֙ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁר֙ הֹרִ֣ישׁ יְהֹוָ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover he burnt incense in the valley of the son of Hinnom, and burnt his children in the fire, according to the abominations of the nations whom the Lord had cast out before the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he burnt incense in the valley of Ben Hinnom, and he also passed his son through the fire, according to the abominations of the nations that the L rd had driven out before the children of Israel.,


###### II Chronicles 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:4)


וַיְזַבֵּ֧חַ וַיְקַטֵּ֛ר בַּבָּמ֖וֹת וְעַל־הַגְּבָע֑וֹת וְתַ֖חַת כׇּל־עֵ֥ץ רַעֲנָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he sacrificed and burnt incense in the high places, and on the hills, and under every green tree.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:5)


וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֜הוּ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָיו֮ בְּיַ֣ד מֶ֣לֶךְ אֲרָם֒ וַיַּ֨כּוּ־ב֔וֹ וַיִּשְׁבּ֤וּ מִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ שִׁבְיָ֣ה גְדוֹלָ֔ה וַיָּבִ֖יאוּ דַּרְמָ֑שֶׂק וְ֠גַ֠ם בְּיַד־מֶ֤לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ נִתָּ֔ן וַיַּךְ־בּ֖וֹ מַכָּ֥ה גְדוֹלָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So that the Lord his God delivered him into the hand of the king of Aram; and they smote him, and carried away a great multitude of them captives, and brought them to Darmeseq. And he was also delivered into the hand of the king of Yisra᾽el, who smote him with a great slaughter.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd his G d delivered him into the hand of the king of Aram, and they smote him, and they took from them a great captivity, and they brought (them) to Damascus. And he was also delivered into the hand of the king of Israel, and he smote him a great blow.


###### II Chronicles 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:6)


וַיַּהֲרֹג֩ פֶּ֨קַח בֶּן־רְמַלְיָ֜הוּ בִּיהוּדָ֗ה מֵאָ֨ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֥ים אֶ֛לֶף בְּי֥וֹם אֶחָ֖ד הַכֹּ֣ל בְּנֵי־חָ֑יִל בְּעׇזְבָ֕ם אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י אֲבוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For Peqaĥ the son of Remalyahu slew in Yehuda a hundred and twenty thousand in one day, who were all valiant men; because they had forsaken the Lord God of their fathers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Pekach the son of Remalyahu slew of Judah one hundred and twenty thousand in one day, all men of valor, their having forsaken the L rd, the G d of their fathers.


###### II Chronicles 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:7)


וַֽיַּהֲרֹ֞ג זִכְרִ֣י**׀** גִּבּ֣וֹר אֶפְרַ֗יִם אֶת־מַעֲשֵׂיָ֙הוּ֙ בֶּן־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְאֶת־עַזְרִיקָ֖ם נְגִ֣יד הַבָּ֑יִת וְאֶת־אֶלְקָנָ֖ה מִשְׁנֵ֥ה הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Zikhri, a mighty man of Efrayim, slew Ma῾aseyahu the king’s son, and ῾Azriqam the governor of the house, and Elqana who was next to the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Zichri the hero of Ephraim slew Ma'aseyahu, the king's son, and Azrikam, the palace chamberlain, and Elkanah, the viceroy.


###### II Chronicles 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:8)


וַיִּשְׁבּוּ֩ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֨ל מֵאֲחֵיהֶ֜ם מָאתַ֣יִם אֶ֗לֶף נָשִׁים֙ בָּנִ֣ים וּבָנ֔וֹת וְגַם־שָׁלָ֥ל רָ֖ב בָּזְז֣וּ מֵהֶ֑ם וַיָּבִ֥יאוּ אֶת־הַשָּׁלָ֖ל לְשֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the children of Yisra᾽el carried away captive of their brethren two hundred thousand, women, sons, and daughters, and took also away much spoil from them, and brought the spoil to Shomeron.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they took captive of their brothers two hundred thousand women, boys, and girls. And also much spoil did they take of them, and they brought the spoil to Shomron.


###### II Chronicles 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:9)


וְ֠שָׁ֠ם הָיָ֨ה נָבִ֥יא לַֽיהֹוָה֮ עֹדֵ֣ד שְׁמוֹ֒ וַיֵּצֵ֗א לִפְנֵ֤י הַצָּבָא֙ הַבָּ֣א לְשֹׁמְר֔וֹן וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לָהֶ֗ם הִ֠נֵּ֠ה בַּחֲמַ֨ת יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵֽי־אֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֛ם עַל־יְהוּדָ֖ה נְתָנָ֣ם בְּיֶדְכֶ֑ם וַתַּהַרְגוּ־בָ֣ם בְּזַ֔עַף עַ֥ד לַשָּׁמַ֖יִם הִגִּֽיעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But a prophet of the Lord was there, whose name was ῾Oded: and he went out before the army that came to Shomeron, and he said to them. Behold, because the Lord God of your fathers was angry with Yehuda, he has delivered them into your hand, and you have slain them in rage that reaches up to heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there was a prophet of the L rd there, Oded by name. And he went out before the host coming to Shomron and said to them: Behold, in the wrath of the L rd, the G d of your fathers, against Judah, He delivered them into your hand, and you slew them in a fury reaching the heavens.


###### II Chronicles 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:10)


וְ֠עַתָּ֠ה בְּנֵֽי־יְהוּדָ֤ה וִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ אַתֶּ֣ם אֹֽמְרִ֔ים לִכְבֹּ֛שׁ לַעֲבָדִ֥ים וְלִשְׁפָח֖וֹת לָכֶ֑ם הֲלֹ֤א רַק־אַתֶּם֙ עִמָּכֶ֣ם אֲשָׁמ֔וֹת לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃

And now you purpose to keep under the children of Yehuda and Yerushalayim to be bondmen and bondwomen to you: but are there not with you, even with you, crimes against the Lord your God?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, sons of Judah and Jerusalem, you wish to take manservants and maidservants for yourselves. Would you not thereby only render yourselves guilty before the L rd your G d!


###### II Chronicles 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:11)


וְעַתָּ֣ה שְׁמָע֔וּנִי וְהָשִׁ֙יבוּ֙ הַשִּׁבְיָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר שְׁבִיתֶ֖ם מֵאֲחֵיכֶ֑ם כִּ֛י חֲר֥וֹן אַף־יְהֹוָ֖ה עֲלֵיכֶֽם׃{ס}

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:12)


וַיָּקֻ֨מוּ אֲנָשִׁ֜ים מֵרָאשֵׁ֣י בְנֵי־אֶפְרַ֗יִם עֲזַרְיָ֤הוּ בֶן־יְהֽוֹחָנָן֙ בֶּרֶכְיָ֣הוּ בֶן־מְשִׁלֵּמ֔וֹת וִֽיחִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ בֶּן־שַׁלֻּ֔ם וַעֲמָשָׂ֖א בֶּן־חַדְלָ֑י עַל־הַבָּאִ֖ים מִן־הַצָּבָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then certain of the heads of the children of Efrayim, ῾Azaryahu the son of Yehoĥanan, Berekhyahu the son of Meshillemot, and Yeĥizqiyyahu the son of Shallum, and ῾Amasa the son of Ḥadlay, stood up against those who came from the war.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And men of the heads of the sons of Ephraim — Azaryahu the son of Yochanan, Berechyahu the son of Meshilemoth and Yechitzkiyahu the son of Shalum and Amassa the son of Chadlai — stood up against those coming from the host.


###### II Chronicles 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:13)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ לָהֶ֗ם לֹֽא־תָבִ֤יאוּ אֶת־הַשִּׁבְיָה֙ הֵ֔נָּה כִּי֩ לְאַשְׁמַ֨ת יְהֹוָ֤ה עָלֵ֙ינוּ֙ אַתֶּ֣ם אֹֽמְרִ֔ים לְהֹסִ֥יף עַל־חַטֹּאתֵ֖נוּ וְעַל־אַשְׁמָתֵ֑נוּ כִּֽי־רַבָּ֤ה אַשְׁמָה֙ לָ֔נוּ וַחֲר֥וֹן אָ֖ף עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they said to them, You shall not bring in the captives here: for whereas we have offended against the Lord already, you intend to add more to our sins and to our guilt: for our trespass is great, and there is fierce wrath against Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they said to them: Do not bring the captives here, for you do so to bring guilt upon us before the L rd, to add to our sins and our guilt, for there is great guilt in us and burning wrath upon Israel.


###### II Chronicles 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:14)


וַיַּעֲזֹ֣ב הֶחָל֗וּץ אֶת־הַשִּׁבְיָה֙ וְאֶת־הַבִּזָּ֔ה לִפְנֵ֥י הַשָּׂרִ֖ים וְכׇל־הַקָּהָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the armed men left the captives and the spoil before the princes and all the congregation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the vanguard left the captives and the spoils before the officers and the entire congregation.


###### II Chronicles 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:15)


וַיָּקֻ֣מוּ הָאֲנָשִׁים֩ אֲשֶׁר־נִקְּב֨וּ בְשֵׁמ֜וֹת וַיַּחֲזִ֣יקוּ בַשִּׁבְיָ֗ה וְכׇֽל־מַעֲרֻמֵּיהֶם֮ הִלְבִּ֣ישׁוּ מִן־הַשָּׁלָל֒ וַיַּלְבִּשֻׁ֣ם וַ֠יַּנְעִל֠וּם וַיַּאֲכִל֨וּם וַיַּשְׁק֜וּם וַיְסֻכ֗וּם וַיְנַהֲל֤וּם בַּֽחֲמֹרִים֙ לְכׇל־כּוֹשֵׁ֔ל וַיְבִיא֛וּם יְרֵח֥וֹ עִיר־הַתְּמָרִ֖ים אֵ֣צֶל אֲחֵיהֶ֑ם וַיָּשׁ֖וּבוּ שֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the men who were expressly named rose up, and took the captives, and with the spoil clothed all who were naked among them, and dressed them, and shod them, and gave them to eat and to drink, and anointed them, and carried all the feeble ones of them upon asses, and brought them to Yereĥo, the city of palmtrees, to their brethren: then they returned to Shomeron.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the aforementioned men arose and took hold of the captives and clothed all their nakedness from the spoils. And they dressed them and shod them and fed them and gave them to drink and anointed them and led all the frail ones on asses and brought them to Jericho, the city of palms, to their brethren, and they returned to Shomron.


###### II Chronicles 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:16)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֗יא שָׁלַ֞ח הַמֶּ֧לֶךְ אָחָ֛ז עַל־מַלְכֵ֥י אַשּׁ֖וּר לַעְזֹ֥ר לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At that time king Aĥaz sent to the kings of Ashshur to help him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At that time, King Achaz sent to the kings of Ashur to help him.


###### II Chronicles 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:17)


וְע֥וֹד אֲדוֹמִ֖ים בָּ֑אוּ וַיַּכּ֥וּ בִיהוּדָ֖ה וַיִּשְׁבּוּ־שֶֽׁבִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For again the Adomim had come and smitten Yehuda, and carried away captives.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Edomites came again and smote Judah and took captives.


###### II Chronicles 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:18)


וּפְלִשְׁתִּ֣ים פָּֽשְׁט֗וּ בְּעָרֵ֨י הַשְּׁפֵלָ֣ה וְהַנֶּ֘גֶב֮ לִֽיהוּדָה֒ וַֽ֠יִּלְכְּד֠וּ אֶת־בֵּֽית־שֶׁ֨מֶשׁ וְאֶת־אַיָּל֜וֹן וְאֶת־הַגְּדֵר֗וֹת וְאֶת־שׂוֹכ֤וֹ וּבְנוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ וְאֶת־תִּמְנָ֣ה וּבְנוֹתֶ֔יהָ וְאֶת־גִּמְז֖וֹ וְאֶת־בְּנֹתֶ֑יהָ וַיֵּשְׁב֖וּ שָֽׁם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Pelishtim had invaded the cities of the coastal plain, and of the south of Yehuda, and had taken Bet-shemesh, and Ayyalon, and Gederot, and Sokho with its hamlets, and Timna and its hamlets, and Gimzo and its hamlets: and they dwelt there.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Philistines spread out in the cities of the lowlands and the southland of Judah and they took Beth-shemesh and Ayalon and Gederoth and Socho and its villages and Timnah and its villages and Gimzo and its villages, and they dwelt there.


###### II Chronicles 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:19)


כִּֽי־הִכְנִ֤יעַ יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־יְהוּדָ֔ה בַּעֲב֖וּר אָחָ֣ז מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֤י הִפְרִ֙יעַ֙ בִּֽיהוּדָ֔ה וּמָע֥וֹל מַ֖עַל בַּיהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord brought Yehuda low because of Aĥaz king of Yisra᾽el; for he caused disorder in Yehuda, and transgressed greatly against the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L rd had humbled Judah because of Achaz king of Israel, for he exposed [(the weakness)] of Judah and was faithless to the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:20)


וַיָּבֹ֣א עָלָ֔יו תִּלְּגַ֥ת פִּלְנְאֶ֖סֶר מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר וַיָּ֥צַר ל֖וֹ וְלֹ֥א חֲזָקֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Tilgat-piln᾽eser king of Ashshur came to him, and distressed him, but did not strengthen him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there came to him [(ostensibly to aid him [viz. II Kings 16:7])] Tilgath-Pilnessser, the king of Ashur, but [(instead of aiding him)] he oppressed him and did not strengthen him.


###### II Chronicles 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:21)


כִּֽי־חָלַ֤ק אָחָז֙ אֶת־בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאֶת־בֵּ֥ית הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וְהַשָּׂרִ֑ים וַיִּתֵּן֙ לְמֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר וְלֹ֥א לְעֶזְרָ֖ה לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For Aĥaz took away a portion out of the house of the Lord, and out of the house of the king, and of the princes, and gave it to the king of Ashshur: but he did not help him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For Achaz had divided [(the treasures)] of the house of the L rd and the house of the king and the princes, and he had given [half] to the king of Ashur, but he was of no help to him.


###### II Chronicles 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:22)


וּבְעֵת֙ הָצֵ֣ר ל֔וֹ וַיּ֖וֹסֶף לִמְע֣וֹל בַּֽיהֹוָ֑ה ה֖וּא הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ אָחָֽז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in the time of his distress he trespassed still more against the Lord: this is that king Aĥaz.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But [(even)] when he oppressed him, he continued to be faithless to the L rd — he remained King Achaz!


###### II Chronicles 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:23)


וַיִּזְבַּ֗ח לֵאלֹהֵ֣י דַרְמֶ֘שֶׂק֮ הַמַּכִּ֣ים בּוֹ֒ וַיֹּ֗אמֶר כִּ֠י אֱלֹהֵ֤י מַלְכֵֽי־אֲרָם֙ הֵ֚ם מַעְזְרִ֣ים אֹתָ֔ם לָהֶ֥ם אֲזַבֵּ֖חַ וְיַעְזְר֑וּנִי וְהֵ֛ם הָיוּ־ל֥וֹ לְהַכְשִׁיל֖וֹ וּלְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he sacrificed to the gods of Darmeseq, who smote him: and he said, Because the gods of the kings of Aram help them, I will sacrifice to them, that they may help me. But they were the ruin of him, and of all Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he sacrificed to the gods of Damascus, [(i.e., of the Syrians)], who had smitten him, saying: For the gods of the kings of Aram — they help them. To them will I sacrifice and they will help me. And they were a stumbling [block] to him and to all of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:24)


וַיֶּאֱסֹ֨ף אָחָ֜ז אֶת־כְּלֵ֣י בֵית־הָאֱלֹהִ֗ים וַיְקַצֵּץ֙ אֶת־כְּלֵ֣י בֵית־הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וַיִּסְגֹּ֖ר אֶת־דַּלְת֣וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיַּ֨עַשׂ ל֧וֹ מִזְבְּח֛וֹת בְּכׇל־פִּנָּ֖ה בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aĥaz gathered together the vessels of the house of God, and cut in pieces the vessels of the house of God, and shut up the doors of the house of the Lord, and he made himself altars in every corner of Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Achaz gathered the vessels of the house of G d, and he cut [(off the gold from)] the vessels of the house of G d, and he closed the doors of the house of the L rd, and he made altars for himself in every corner in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:25)


וּבְכׇל־עִ֨יר וָעִ֤יר לִֽיהוּדָה֙ עָשָׂ֣ה בָמ֔וֹת לְקַטֵּ֖ר לֵאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים וַיַּכְעֵ֕ס אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י אֲבֹתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in every city of Yehuda he made high places to burn incense to other gods, and provoked to anger the Lord God of his fathers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in every city of Judah he made mounds to burn incense to strange gods. And he angered the L rd, the G d of his fathers.


###### II Chronicles 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:26)


וְיֶ֤תֶר דְּבָרָיו֙ וְכׇל־דְּרָכָ֔יו הָרִאשֹׁנִ֖ים וְהָאַחֲרוֹנִ֑ים הִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֔ים עַל־סֵ֥פֶר מַלְכֵֽי־יְהוּדָ֖ה וְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the rest of his acts and of all his ways, first and last, behold, they are written in the book of the kings of Yehuda and Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the events of his reign and all of his ways, the earlier and the later, are recorded in the book [of the chronicles] of the kings of Judah and Israel.


###### II Chronicles 28:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/28:27)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֨ב אָחָ֜ז עִם־אֲבֹתָ֗יו וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֤הוּ בָעִיר֙ בִּיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם כִּ֚י לֹ֣א הֱבִיאֻ֔הוּ לְקִבְרֵ֖י מַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ יְחִזְקִיָּ֥הוּ בְנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Aĥaz slept with his fathers, and they buried him in the city, in Yerushalayim: but they did not bring him into the tombs of the kings of Yisra᾽el: and Yeĥizqiyyahu his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Achaz rested with his fathers, and they buried him in the city of Jerusalem, for they did not bring him to the graves of the kings of Israel. And Yechizkiyahu his son ruled in his place.



## Chapter 29

###### II Chronicles 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:1)


יְחִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ מָלַ֗ךְ בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֤ים וְחָמֵשׁ֙ שָׁנָ֔ה וְעֶשְׂרִ֤ים וָתֵ֙שַׁע֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ אֲבִיָּ֖ה בַּת־זְכַרְיָֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yeĥizqiyyahu began to reign when he was twenty five years old, and he reigned twenty nine years in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was Aviyya, the daughter of Zekharyahu.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yechizkiyahu [began] to reign when he was twenty-five years old, and he reigned twenty-nine years in Jerusalem. His mother's name was Aviyah the daughter of Zecharyahu.


###### II Chronicles 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה דָּוִ֥יד אָבִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did that which was right in the sight of the Lord, according to all that David his father had done.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was just in the eyes of the L rd, as all that his father David had done.


###### II Chronicles 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:3)


ה֣וּא בַשָּׁנָה֩ הָרִאשׁוֹנָ֨ה לְמׇלְכ֜וֹ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָרִאשׁ֗וֹן פָּתַ֛ח אֶת־דַּלְת֥וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה וַֽיְחַזְּקֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He in the first year of his reign, in the first month, opened the doors of the house of the Lord, and repaired them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He, in the first year of his reign, in the first month, opened the doors of the house of the L rd and strengthened them.


###### II Chronicles 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:4)


וַיָּבֵ֥א אֶת־הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים וְאֶת־הַלְוִיִּ֑ם וַיַּאַסְפֵ֖ם לִרְח֥וֹב הַמִּזְרָֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he brought in the priests and the Levites, and gathered them together into the east street,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he brought the priests and the Levites and he gathered them into the eastern square.


###### II Chronicles 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:5)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר לָהֶ֖ם שְׁמָע֣וּנִי הַלְוִיִּ֑ם עַתָּ֣ה הִֽתְקַדְּשׁ֗וּ וְקַדְּשׁוּ֙ אֶת־בֵּ֤ית יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֹתֵיכֶ֔ם וְהוֹצִ֥יאוּ אֶת־הַנִּדָּ֖ה מִן־הַקֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he said to them, Hear me, you Levites, sanctify yourselves now, and sanctify the house of the Lord God of your fathers, and carry out the filth from the holy place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to them: Hear me, Levites: Now, cleanse yourselves and cleanse the house of the L rd, the G d of your fathers, and remove the "menstruant" [i.e., idolatry] from the sanctuary.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיִּשְׁחָטוּם֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים וַֽיְחַטְּא֤וּ אֶת־דָּמָם֙ הַמִּזְבֵּ֔חָה לְכַפֵּ֖ר עַל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֤י לְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אָמַ֣ר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ הָעוֹלָ֖ה וְהַחַטָּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the priests killed them, and they made a sin offering with their blood upon the altar, to make atonement for all Yisra᾽el: for the king commanded that the burnt offering and the sin offering should be made for all Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests slaughtered them, and they applied their blood on the [four corners of the] altar to atone for all of Israel. For on behalf of all of Israel did the king command to bring the burnt-offerings and the sin-offerings.


###### II Chronicles 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:25)


וַיַּעֲמֵ֨ד אֶת־הַלְוִיִּ֜ם בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֗ה בִּמְצִלְתַּ֙יִם֙ בִּנְבָלִ֣ים וּבְכִנֹּר֔וֹת בְּמִצְוַ֥ת דָּוִ֛יד וְגָ֥ד חֹזֵֽה־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וְנָתָ֣ן הַנָּבִ֑יא כִּ֧י בְיַד־יְהֹוָ֛ה הַמִּצְוָ֖ה בְּיַד־נְבִיאָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he set the Levites in the house of the Lord with cymbals, with lutes, and with lyres, according to the commandment of David, and of Gad the king’s seer, and Natan the prophet: for so was the commandment of the Lord by his prophets.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he stood the Levites in the house of the L rd with cymbals, with psalteries and with harps as commanded by David and Gad the king's seer and Nathan the prophet. For from the L rd was the commandment, through His prophets.


###### II Chronicles 29:26

וַיַּעַמְד֤וּ הַלְוִיִּם֙ בִּכְלֵ֣י דָוִ֔יד וְהַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים בַּחֲצֹצְרֽוֹת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Levites stood with the instruments of David, and the priests with the trumpets.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites stood with David's instruments, and the priests, with trumpets.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:27)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ לְהַעֲל֥וֹת הָעֹלָ֖ה לְהַמִּזְבֵּ֑חַ וּבְעֵ֞ת הֵחֵ֣ל הָעוֹלָ֗ה הֵחֵ֤ל שִׁיר־יְהֹוָה֙ וְהַחֲצֹ֣צְר֔וֹת וְעַ֨ל־יְדֵ֔י כְּלֵ֖י דָּוִ֥יד מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Ḥizqiyyahu commanded to offer the burnt offering upon the altar. And when the burnt offering began, the song of the Lord began also with the trumpets, and with the instruments of David king of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Chizkiyahu ordered that the burnt-offering be brought up on the altar. And when the [sacrifice of] the burnt-offering began, the song of the L rd began — and the trumpets — accompanied by the instruments of David, king of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 29:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:28)


וְכׇל־הַקָּהָל֙ מִֽשְׁתַּחֲוִ֔ים וְהַשִּׁ֣יר מְשׁוֹרֵ֔ר וְהַחֲצֹצְר֖וֹת (מחצצרים) [מַחְצְרִ֑ים] הַכֹּ֕ל עַ֖ד לִכְל֥וֹת הָעֹלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the congregation prostrated themselves, and the singers sang, and the trumpeters sounded: and all this continued until the burnt offering was finished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the congregation prostrated themselves, and the song was sung and the trumpets sounded — all until the end [of the sacrifice] of the burnt-offering.


###### II Chronicles 29:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:29)


וּכְכַלּ֖וֹת לְהַעֲל֑וֹת כָּֽרְע֗וּ הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ וְכׇֽל־הַנִּמְצְאִ֥ים אִתּ֖וֹ וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲוֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when they had made an end of offering, the king and all who were present with him bowed down, and prostrated themselves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when the sacrifice had ended, the king and all those with him knelt, and they prostrated themselves.


###### II Chronicles 29:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:30)


וַ֠יֹּ֠אמֶר יְחִזְקִיָּ֨הוּ הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ וְהַשָּׂרִים֙ לַלְוִיִּ֔ם לְהַלֵּל֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה בְּדִבְרֵ֥י דָוִ֖יד וְאָסָ֣ף הַחֹזֶ֑ה וַֽיְהַלְלוּ֙ עַד־לְשִׂמְחָ֔ה וַֽיִּקְּד֖וּ וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲוֽוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover Yeĥizqiyyahu the king and the princes commanded the Levites to sing praise to the Lord with the words of David, and of Asaf the seer. And they sang praises with gladness, and they bowed their heads and worshipped.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Yechizkiyahu and the officers asked the Levites to praise the L rd with the words of David and Asaph the seer. And they praised until [they were filled with] joy, and they bowed down and prostrated themselves.


###### II Chronicles 29:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:31)


וַיַּ֨עַן יְחִזְקִיָּ֜הוּ וַיֹּ֗אמֶר עַתָּה֙ מִלֵּאתֶ֤ם יֶדְכֶם֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה גֹּ֧שׁוּ וְהָבִ֛יאוּ זְבָחִ֥ים וְתוֹד֖וֹת לְבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיָּבִ֤יאוּ הַקָּהָל֙ זְבָחִ֣ים וְתוֹד֔וֹת וְכׇל־נְדִ֥יב לֵ֖ב עֹלֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Yeĥizqiyyahu answered and said, Now you have consecrated yourselves to the Lord, come near and bring sacrifices and thank offerings into the house of the Lord. And the congregation brought in sacrifices and thank offerings; and all who were of a willing heart brought burnt offerings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu proclaimed: Now, you have invested yourselves in [the service of] the L rd. Come forward and bring peace-offerings and thank-offerings to the house of the L rd. And the congregation brought peace-offerings and thank-offerings, and all the generous of heart, burnt-offerings [(which are completely burnt, no part of it being eaten by the donor)].


###### II Chronicles 29:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:32)


וַיְהִ֞י מִסְפַּ֣ר הָעֹלָה֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֵבִ֣יאוּ הַקָּהָל֒ בָּקָ֣ר שִׁבְעִ֔ים אֵילִ֥ים מֵאָ֖ה כְּבָשִׂ֣ים מָאתָ֑יִם לְעֹלָ֥ה לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה כׇּל־אֵֽלֶּה׃

And the number of the burnt offerings, which the congregation brought, was seventy bullocks, a hundred rams, and two hundred lambs: all these were for a burnt offering to the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:33)


וְֽהַקֳּדָשִׁ֑ים בָּקָר֙ שֵׁ֣שׁ מֵא֔וֹת וְצֹ֖אן שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת אֲלָפִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the consecrated things were six hundred oxen and three thousand sheep.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 29:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/29:34)


רַ֤ק הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ הָי֣וּ לִמְעָ֔ט וְלֹ֣א יָכְל֔וּ לְהַפְשִׁ֖יט אֶת־כׇּל־הָעֹל֑וֹת וַֽיְחַזְּק֞וּם אֲחֵיהֶ֣ם הַלְוִיִּ֗ם עַד־כְּל֤וֹת הַמְּלָאכָה֙ וְעַ֣ד יִתְקַדְּשׁ֣וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֔ים כִּ֤י הַלְוִיִּם֙ יִשְׁרֵ֣י לֵבָ֔ב לְהִתְקַדֵּ֖שׁ מֵהַכֹּהֲנִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the priests were too few, so that they could not flay all the burnt offerings: so that their brethren the Levites did help them, till the work was ended, and until the other priests had sanctified themselves: for the Levites were more upright in heart than the priests in sanctifying themselves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the priests were few, and they could not flay all of the burnt-offerings [by themselves], and their brethren, the Levites, strengthened them, until the work [(of flaying)] was completed and until the priests [i.e., until more priests] cleansed themselves [(for the sacred service)]. For the Levites were just of heart [(and therefore)] more expeditious [(to cleanse themselves)] than the priests.

וְגַם־עֹלָ֨ה לָרֹ֜ב בְּחֶלְבֵ֧י הַשְּׁלָמִ֛ים וּבַנְּסָכִ֖ים לָֽעֹלָ֑ה וַתִּכּ֖וֹן עֲבוֹדַ֥ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And also the burnt offerings were in abundance, with the fat of the peace offerings, and the drink offerings for every burnt offering. So the service of the house of the Lord was firmly established.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(the Levites were, indeed, sorely needed)], for there was an abundance of burnt-offerings, and the fats of the peace-offerings, and the libations for the burnt-offerings; and the service of the house of the L rd [(with the help of the Levites)] was consummated.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yeĥizqiyyahu rejoiced, and all the people, because of what God had prepared for the people: for the thing was done suddenly.

And Yechizkiyahu and all the people rejoiced over G d's having girded the people, for the [whole] thing had happened of a sudden.



## Chapter 30

###### II Chronicles 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:1)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֨ח יְחִזְקִיָּ֜הוּ עַל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל וִיהוּדָ֗ה וְגַֽם־אִגְּרוֹת֙ כָּתַב֙ עַל־אֶפְרַ֣יִם וּמְנַשֶּׁ֔ה לָב֥וֹא לְבֵית־יְהֹוָ֖ה בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת פֶּ֔סַח לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yeĥizqiyyahu sent to all Yisra᾽el and Yehuda, and wrote letters also to Efrayim and Menashshe, that they should come to the house of the Lord at Yerushalayim, to keep the passover to the Lord God of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu sent to all of Israel and Judah, and he also wrote letters to Ephraim and Menasheh to come to the house of the L rd in Jerusalem to perform the Pesach (sacrifice) to the L rd, the G d of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:2)


וַיִּוָּעַ֨ץ הַמֶּ֧לֶךְ וְשָׂרָ֛יו וְכׇל־הַקָּהָ֖ל בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת הַפֶּ֖סַח בַּחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַשֵּׁנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the king had taken counsel, and his princes, and all the congregation in Yerushalayim, to keep the passover in the second month.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king and his officers and all the congregation took counsel in Jerusalem to perform the Pesach in the second month.


###### II Chronicles 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:3)


כִּ֣י לֹ֧א יָכְל֛וּ לַעֲשֹׂת֖וֹ בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֑יא כִּ֤י הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ לֹֽא־הִתְקַדְּשׁ֣וּ לְמַדַּ֔י וְהָעָ֖ם לֹא־נֶאֶסְפ֥וּ לִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they could not keep it in its time, because the priests had not sanctified themselves sufficiently, nor had the people gathered themselves together to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they could not perform it at that time [(in the first month, Nissan, as prescribed)], for a sufficient number of priests had not (yet) cleansed themselves, and [all] the people had not yet gathered in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:4)


וַיִּישַׁ֥ר הַדָּבָ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וּבְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־הַקָּהָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the thing pleased the king and all the congregation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the thing was just in the eyes of the king and in the eyes of all the congregation.


###### II Chronicles 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:5)


וַיַּעֲמִ֣ידוּ דָבָ֗ר לְהַעֲבִ֨יר ק֤וֹל בְּכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מִבְּאֵֽר־שֶׁ֣בַע וְעַד־דָּ֔ן לָב֞וֹא לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת פֶּ֛סַח לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם כִּ֣י לֹ֥א לָרֹ֛ב עָשׂ֖וּ כַּכָּתֽוּב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So they established a decree to make proclamation throughout all Yisra᾽el, from Be᾽er-sheva as far as Dan, that they should come to keep the passover to the Lord God of Yisra᾽el at Yerushalayim: for they had not done it for long as it was written.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they resolved that it be proclaimed in all of Israel, from Beersheva until Dan, to come to perform the Pesach to the L rd, the G d of Israel, in Jerusalem, because for many (years) they had not performed it as prescribed.


###### II Chronicles 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:6)


וַיֵּלְכוּ֩ הָרָצִ֨ים בָּאִגְּר֜וֹת מִיַּ֧ד הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ וְשָׂרָ֗יו בְּכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וִֽיהוּדָ֔ה וּכְמִצְוַ֥ת הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ לֵאמֹ֑ר בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל שׁ֚וּבוּ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֱלֹהֵי֙ אַבְרָהָם֙ יִצְחָ֣ק וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְיָשֹׁב֙ אֶל־הַפְּלֵיטָ֔ה הַנִּשְׁאֶ֣רֶת לָכֶ֔ם מִכַּ֖ף מַלְכֵ֥י אַשּֽׁוּר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the runners went with the letters from the king and his princes throughout all Yisra᾽el and Yehuda, and according to the commandment of the king, saying, You children of Yisra᾽el, turn back to the Lord God of Avraham, Yiżĥaq, and Yisra᾽el, and he will turn again to the remnant of you, who have escaped out of the hand of the kings of Ashshur.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the runners went with letters from the hand of the king and his officers throughout all of Israel and Judah, saying, by command of the king: Children of Israel, return to the L rd, the G d of Abraham, Isaac, and Israel, and He will return to the remnant left of you from the hand of the kings of Ashur.


###### II Chronicles 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/30:7)


וְאַל־תִּֽהְי֗וּ כַּאֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ וְכַ֣אֲחֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָעֲל֔וּ בַּיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֑ם וַיִּתְּנֵ֣ם לְשַׁמָּ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר אַתֶּ֥ם רֹאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And be not like your fathers, and like your brethren, who trespassed against the Lord God of their fathers, who therefore gave them up to desolation, as you see.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וַיָּקֻ֜מוּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֤ים הַלְוִיִּם֙ וַיְבָרְכ֣וּ אֶת־הָעָ֔ם וַיִּשָּׁמַ֖ע בְּקוֹלָ֑ם וַתָּב֧וֹא תְפִלָּתָ֛ם לִמְע֥וֹן קׇדְשׁ֖וֹ לַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the priests the Levites arose and blessed the people: and their voice was heard, and their prayer came up to his holy dwelling place, to heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests, the Levites, arose and blessed the people, and their voice was heeded. And their prayer came to His holy abode in heaven.



## Chapter 31

###### II Chronicles 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:1)


וּכְכַלּ֣וֹת כׇּל־זֹ֗את יָצְא֨וּ כׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֥ל הַֽנִּמְצְאִים֮ לְעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָה֒ וַיְשַׁבְּר֣וּ הַמַּצֵּב֣וֹת וַיְגַדְּע֣וּ הָאֲשֵׁרִ֡ים וַיְנַתְּצ֣וּ אֶת־הַ֠בָּמ֠וֹת וְאֶת־הַֽמִּזְבְּח֞וֹת מִכׇּל־יְהוּדָ֧ה וּבִנְיָמִ֛ן וּבְאֶפְרַ֥יִם וּמְנַשֶּׁ֖ה עַד־לְכַלֵּ֑ה וַיָּשׁ֜וּבוּ כׇּל־בְּנֵ֧י יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל אִ֥ישׁ לַאֲחֻזָּת֖וֹ לְעָרֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now when all this was finished, all Yisra᾽el that were present went out to the cities of Yehuda, and broke the images in pieces, and cut down the asherim, and broke down the high places and the altars out of all Yehuda and Binyamin, in Efrayim also and Menashshe, until they had destroyed them all. Then all the children of Yisra᾽el returned, every man to his possession, to their own cities.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when all this was ended, all of Israel who were found [in Jerusalem] went out to the cities of Judah, and they broke the monuments and uprooted the asherim and razed the high places and the altars from all of Judah and Benjamin and Ephraim and Menasheh until they were completely destroyed. And all the men of Israel returned, each to his holding, to their cities.


###### II Chronicles 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:2)


וַיַּעֲמֵ֣ד יְחִזְקִיָּ֡הוּ אֶת־מַחְלְק֣וֹת הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים וְ֠הַלְוִיִּ֠ם עַֽל־מַחְלְקוֹתָ֞ם אִ֣ישׁ**׀** כְּפִ֣י עֲבֹדָת֗וֹ לַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וְלַלְוִיִּ֔ם לְעֹלָ֖ה וְלִשְׁלָמִ֑ים לְשָׁרֵת֙ וּלְהֹד֣וֹת וּלְהַלֵּ֔ל בְּשַׁעֲרֵ֖י מַחֲנ֥וֹת יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yeĥizqiyyahu anointed the duty watches of the priests and the Levites after their divisions, every man according to his service, the priests and the Levites for burnt offerings and for peace offerings, to minister, and to give thanks, and to praise in the gates of the tents of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu established the divisions of the priests and the Levites according to their divisions [i.e., their customary watches], each according to his service for the priests and the Levites, for the burnt-offerings and the peace-offerings, to minister and to thank and to praise in the gates of the camps of the L rd [i.e., in the Temple].


###### II Chronicles 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:3)


וּמְנָת֩ הַמֶּ֨לֶךְ מִן־רְכוּשׁ֜וֹ לָעֹל֗וֹת לְעֹלוֹת֙ הַבֹּ֣קֶר וְהָעֶ֔רֶב וְהָ֣עֹל֔וֹת לַשַּׁבָּת֖וֹת וְלֶחֳדָשִׁ֣ים וְלַמֹּעֲדִ֑ים כַּכָּת֖וּב בְּתוֹרַ֥ת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He appointed also the king’s portion of his substance for the burnt offerings, that is, for the morning and evening burnt offerings, and the burnt offerings for the sabbaths, and for the new moons, and for the appointed seasons, as it is written in the Tora of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king's portion [i.e., his gift] from his revenues [went for] burnt-offerings: the burnt-offerings of the morning and the evening, and the burnt-offerings of Sabbaths and New Moons and festivals, as prescribed in the Torah of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:4)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לָעָם֙ לְיוֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם לָתֵ֕ת מְנָ֥ת הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֑ם לְמַ֥עַן יֶחֶזְק֖וּ בְּתוֹרַ֥ת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover he commanded the people who dwelt in Yerushalayim to give the portion of the priests and the Levites, that they might adhere firmly to the Tora of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he told the people, the dwellers of Jerusalem, to give the portion of the priests and the Levites [(terumah and ma'aser)], so that they could strengthen themselves in the Torah of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:5)


וְכִפְרֹ֣ץ הַדָּבָ֗ר הִרְבּ֤וּ בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ רֵאשִׁ֣ית דָּגָ֗ן תִּיר֤וֹשׁ וְיִצְהָר֙ וּדְבַ֔שׁ וְכֹ֖ל תְּבוּאַ֣ת שָׂדֶ֑ה וּמַעְשַׂ֥ר הַכֹּ֛ל לָרֹ֖ב הֵבִֽיאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And as soon as the commandment came abroad, the children of Yisra᾽el brought in abundance the firstfruits of corn, wine, and oil, and honey, and of all the increase of the field; and they brought in the tithe of all things in plenty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when this was under way, the children of Israel increased [their offerings of] the first of corn [(terumah)], wine, oil, and honey, and all the produce of the field; and tithes of all did they bring in abundance.


###### II Chronicles 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:6)


וּבְנֵ֧י יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל וִיהוּדָ֗ה הַיּוֹשְׁבִים֮ בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָה֒ גַּם־הֵ֗ם מַעְשַׂ֤ר בָּקָר֙ וָצֹ֔אן וּמַעְשַׂ֣ר קׇֽדָשִׁ֔ים הַמְקֻדָּשִׁ֖ים לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם הֵבִ֕יאוּ וַֽיִּתְּנ֖וּ עֲרֵמ֥וֹת עֲרֵמֽוֹת׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And concerning the children of Yisra᾽el and Yehuda, who dwelt in the cities of Yehuda, they also brought in the tithe of oxen and sheep, and the tithe of holy things which were consecrated to the Lord their God, and laid them up by heaps.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the children of Israel and Judah who lived in the cities of Judah, they, too, brought tithes of cattle and sheep and tithes of the consecrated things that they consecrated to the L rd their G d [(though these are exempt from tithes)], making heaps and heaps (of them).


###### II Chronicles 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:7)


בַּחֹ֙דֶשׁ֙ הַשְּׁלִשִׁ֔י הֵחֵ֥לּוּ הָעֲרֵמ֖וֹת לְיִסּ֑וֹד וּבַחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֖י כִּלּֽוּ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the the third month the heaps began to pile up, and in the seventh month they were finished.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the third month the heaps began to take form, and in the seventh month they were completed.


###### II Chronicles 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:8)


וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ יְחִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ וְהַשָּׂרִ֔ים וַיִּרְא֖וּ אֶת־הָעֲרֵמ֑וֹת וַֽיְבָרְכוּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאֵ֖ת עַמּ֥וֹ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yeĥizqiyyahu and the princes came and saw the heaps, and they blessed the Lord, and his people Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu and the officers came and they saw the heaps and they blessed the L rd and His people Israel.


###### II Chronicles 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:9)


וַיִּדְרֹ֣שׁ יְחִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ עַל־הַכֹּ֥הֲנִ֛ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֖ם עַל־הָעֲרֵמֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Yeĥiz­qiyyahu questioned the priests and the Levites concerning the heaps.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu inquired of the priests and the Levites concerning the heaps [(wondering how they could be so large if the priests and the Levites had taken their share from them)].


###### II Chronicles 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:10)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלָ֗יו עֲזַרְיָ֧הוּ הַכֹּהֵ֛ן הָרֹ֖אשׁ לְבֵ֣ית צָד֑וֹק וַ֠יֹּ֠אמֶר מֵהָחֵ֨ל הַתְּרוּמָ֜ה לָבִ֣יא בֵית־יְהֹוָ֗ה אָכ֨וֹל וְשָׂב֤וֹעַ וְהוֹתֵר֙ עַד־לָר֔וֹב כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ בֵּרַ֣ךְ אֶת־עַמּ֔וֹ וְהַנּוֹתָ֖ר אֶת־הֶהָמ֥וֹן הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And ῾Azaryahu the chief priest of the house of Żadoq answered him, and said, Since the people began to bring the offerings into the house of the Lord, we have had enough to eat, and have left plenty: for the Lord has blessed his people; and that which is left is this great store.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Azaryahu the priest, the head [(the high-priest] of the house of Tzaddok, said to him: From the beginning of the bringing of the terumah to the house of the L rd, [we have] eaten, and been sated, and left over in abundance, for the L rd has blessed His people, and this abundance is what is left over.


###### II Chronicles 31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:11)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְחִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ לְהָכִ֧ין לְשָׁכ֛וֹת בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיָּכִֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Yeĥizqiyyahu commanded to prepare chambers in the house of the Lord; and they prepared them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu directed that they prepare chambers in the house of the L rd [(for the storing of terumah)], and they prepared (them).


###### II Chronicles 31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:12)


וַיָּבִ֨יאוּ אֶת־הַתְּרוּמָ֧ה וְהַֽמַּעֲשֵׂ֛ר וְהַקֳּדָשִׁ֖ים בֶּאֱמוּנָ֑ה וַעֲלֵיהֶ֤ם נָגִיד֙ (כונניהו) [כׇּֽנַנְיָ֣הוּ] הַלֵּוִ֔י וְשִׁמְעִ֥י אָחִ֖יהוּ מִשְׁנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they brought in the offerings and the tithes and the dedicated things faithfully: over which Kananyahu the Levite was ruler, and Shim῾i his brother was second.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they brought the terumah and the ma'aser and the consecrations [to the chambers] in good faith; and over them, the administrator, Kenanyahu the Levite, and Shim'i his brother, (his) second.


###### II Chronicles 31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:13)


וִיחִיאֵ֡ל וַ֠עֲזַזְיָ֠הוּ וְנַ֨חַת וַעֲשָׂהאֵ֜ל וִירִימ֤וֹת וְיֽוֹזָבָד֙ וֶאֱלִיאֵ֣ל וְיִסְמַכְיָ֔הוּ וּמַ֖חַת וּבְנָיָ֑הוּ פְּקִידִ֗ים מִיַּ֤ד (כונניהו) [כׇּֽנַנְיָ֙הוּ֙] וְשִׁמְעִ֣י אָחִ֔יו בְּמִפְקַד֙ יְחִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַעֲזַרְיָ֖הוּ נְגִ֥יד בֵּית־הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yeĥi᾽el, and ῾Azazyahu, and Naĥat, and ῾Asa᾽el, and Yerimot, and Yozavad, and Eli᾽el, and Yismakhyahu, and Maĥat, and Benayahu, were overseers under the hand of Kananyahu and Shim῾i his brother, at the commandment of Yeĥizqiyyahu the king, and ῾Azaryahu the ruler of the house of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechiel and Azazyahu and Nachath and Asahel and Yerimoth and Yozavad and Eliel and Yismachyahu and Machath and Benayahu, appointees of Menanyahu and Shim'i his brother, by order of King Yechizkiyahu and Azaryahu, chief [i.e., high-priest] of the house of G d.


###### II Chronicles 31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:14)


וְקוֹרֵ֨א בֶן־יִמְנָ֤ה הַלֵּוִי֙ הַשּׁוֹעֵ֣ר לַמִּזְרָ֔חָה עַ֖ל נִדְב֣וֹת הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים לָתֵת֙ תְּרוּמַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה וְקׇדְשֵׁ֖י הַקֳּדָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Qore the son of Yimna the Levite, the gatekeeper towards the east, was over the freewill offerings of God, to distribute the offerings of the Lord, and the most holy things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Korei the son of Yimnah the Levite, keeper of the eastern gate, over the gifts of [(i.e., prescribed by)] G d, to give the terumah of the L rd [to the priest] and the [flesh of the] holy of holies.


###### II Chronicles 31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:15)


וְעַל־יָד֡וֹ עֵ֣דֶן וּ֠מִנְיָמִ֠ן וְיֵשׁ֨וּעַ וּֽשְׁמַעְיָ֜הוּ אֲמַרְיָ֧הוּ וּשְׁכַנְיָ֛הוּ בְּעָרֵ֥י הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים בֶּאֱמוּנָ֑ה לָתֵ֤ת לַֽאֲחֵיהֶם֙ בְּמַחְלְק֔וֹת כַּגָּד֖וֹל כַּקָּטָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And next to him, were ῾Eden, and Minyamin, and Yeshua, and Shema῾yahu, Amaryahu and Shekhanyahu, in the cities of the priests, faithfully entrusted to give their portions to their brethren by divisions, old and young alike;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, under him, Eden and Minyamin and Yeshua and Sh'mayahu, Amaryahu and Shechanyahu, in the cities of the priests, entrusted with giving [the priestly gifts] to their brethren in the divisions, great and small alike.


###### II Chronicles 31:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:16)


מִלְּבַ֞ד הִתְיַחְשָׂ֣ם לִזְכָרִ֗ים מִבֶּ֨ן שָׁל֤וֹשׁ שָׁנִים֙ וּלְמַ֔עְלָה לְכׇל־הַבָּ֥א לְבֵית־יְהֹוָ֖ה לִדְבַר־י֣וֹם בְּיוֹמ֑וֹ לַעֲב֣וֹדָתָ֔ם בְּמִשְׁמְרוֹתָ֖ם כְּמַחְלְקוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
besides their duty to register the males for their service in their charges according to their divisions, from three years old and upward, including everyone who entered into the house of the Lord for the service of each day;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Aside from [what they gave to] those who traced themselves [to priestly families], to the males, from three years old upwards, to all who came to the house of the L rd, for (their) daily allotment for their service in their watches according to their divisions.


###### II Chronicles 31:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:17)


וְאֵ֨ת הִתְיַחֵ֤שׂ הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ לְבֵ֣ית אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֔ם וְהַ֨לְוִיִּ֔ם מִבֶּ֛ן עֶשְׂרִ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה וּלְמָ֑עְלָה בְּמִשְׁמְרוֹתֵיהֶ֖ם בְּמַחְלְקוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and to register the priests by the houses of their fathers, and the Levites from twenty years old upwards, in their charges by their divisions;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [they distributed terumah] to the priests who traced themselves to their fathers' houses, and [ma'aser to] the Levites, from twenty years old upwards in their watches in their divisions,


###### II Chronicles 31:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/31:18)


וּלְהִתְיַחֵ֗שׂ בְּכׇל־טַפָּ֛ם נְשֵׁיהֶ֛ם וּבְנֵיהֶ֥ם וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶ֖ם לְכׇל־קָהָ֑ל כִּ֥י בֶאֱמוּנָתָ֖ם יִתְקַדְּשׁוּ־קֹֽדֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they had also the duty of registering them by genealogy with all their little ones, their wives, and their sons, and their daughters, through all the congregation: for in their office of trust they sanctified themselves in holiness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and [to] those traced (to them), of all their infants, their wives, their sons and their daughters, of all the congregation [of the Levites]; for in their faith, they guarded what was holy [from defilement].


###### II Chronicles 31:19

וְלִבְנֵי֩ אַהֲרֹ֨ן הַכֹּהֲנִ֜ים בִּשְׂדֵ֨י מִגְרַ֤שׁ עָֽרֵיהֶם֙ בְּכׇל־עִ֣יר וָעִ֔יר אֲנָשִׁ֕ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִקְּב֖וּ בְּשֵׁמ֑וֹת לָתֵ֣ת מָנ֗וֹת לְכׇל־זָכָר֙ בַּכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים וּלְכׇל־הִתְיַחֵ֖שׂ בַּלְוִיִּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also for the sons of Aharon the priests, who were in the fields of pasture land belonging to their cities, in every city, there were men who were expressly named, to give portions to all the males among the priests, and to all who were reckoned by genealogies among the Levites.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the sons of Aaron the priests, in the fields of the open lands of their cities, in every city, [there were appointed] the aforementioned men to give portions to every male among the priests and to all who were traced to the Levites.

וַיַּ֧עַשׂ כָּזֹ֛את יְחִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּכׇל־יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיַּ֨עַשׂ הַטּ֤וֹב וְהַיָּשָׁר֙ וְהָ֣אֱמֶ֔ת לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thus did Yeĥizqiyyahu do throughout all Yehuda, and he did that which was good and right and true before the Lord his God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

And every act that he began, in the service of the house of G d and in Torah and in mitzvah, to seek his G d, he wrought with all his heart and he succeeded.



## Chapter 32

###### II Chronicles 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:1)


אַחֲרֵ֨י הַדְּבָרִ֤ים וְהָֽאֱמֶת֙ הָאֵ֔לֶּה בָּ֖א סַנְחֵרִ֣יב מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֑וּר וַיָּבֹ֣א בִיהוּדָ֗ה וַיִּ֙חַן֙ עַל־הֶעָרִ֣ים הַבְּצֻר֔וֹת וַיֹּ֖אמֶר לְבִקְעָ֥ם אֵלָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After these things and these deeds of integrity, Sanĥeriv king of Ash­shur came, and entered into Yehuda, and encamped against the forti­fied cities, and thought to win them for himself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[But even] after these things and the truth [(pursued by Chizkiyahu)], Sancheriv king of Ashur came against Judah [(because of their back-sliding)], and he encamped against the fortified cities, and he devised how to breach them for himself.


###### II Chronicles 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:2)


וַיַּרְא֙ יְחִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ כִּי־בָ֖א סַנְחֵרִ֑יב וּפָנָ֕יו לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when Yeĥizqiyyahu saw that Sanĥeriv was come, and that he intended to fight against Yerushalayim,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu saw that Sancheriv had come and that his face was set for war against Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:3)


וַיִּוָּעַ֗ץ עִם־שָׂרָיו֙ וְגִבֹּרָ֔יו לִסְתּוֹם֙ אֶת־מֵימֵ֣י הָעֲיָנ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֖ר מִח֣וּץ לָעִ֑יר וַֽיַּעְזְרֽוּהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he took counsel with his princes and his mighty men to stop the water of the springs which were outside the city: and they helped him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he took counsel with his officers and his warriors to stop up the waters of the springs outside the city, and they aided him.


###### II Chronicles 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:4)


וַיִּקָּבְצ֣וּ עַם־רָ֔ב וַֽיִּסְתְּמוּ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַמַּעְיָנ֔וֹת וְאֶת־הַנַּ֛חַל הַשּׁוֹטֵ֥ף בְּתוֹךְ־הָאָ֖רֶץ לֵאמֹ֑ר לָ֤מָּה יָב֙וֹאוּ֙ מַלְכֵ֣י אַשּׁ֔וּר וּמָצְא֖וּ מַ֥יִם רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So a great many people were gathered together, who stopped up all the springs, and also the wadi that ran through the midst of the land, saying, Why should the kings of Ashshur come, and find much water?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a great throng gathered and stopped up all of the springs and the stream that flowed in the midst of the land [(outside Jerusalem)], saying: Why should the kings of Ashur come and find an abundance of water?


###### II Chronicles 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:5)


וַיִּתְחַזַּ֡ק וַיִּ֩בֶן֩ אֶת־כׇּל־הַחוֹמָ֨ה הַפְּרוּצָ֜ה וַיַּ֣עַל עַל־הַמִּגְדָּל֗וֹת וְלַח֙וּצָה֙ הַחוֹמָ֣ה אַחֶ֔רֶת וַיְחַזֵּ֥ק אֶת־הַמִּלּ֖וֹא עִ֣יר דָּוִ֑יד וַיַּ֥עַשׂ שֶׁ֛לַח לָרֹ֖ב וּמָגִנִּֽים׃

Also he took courage, and built up all the wall that was broken, and raised it up to the towers, and another wall outside that, and he strengthened the Millo in the city of David, and made weapons and shields in abundance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he strengthened himself and built the entire breached wall so that it rose above the towers, and outside [this wall he built] another wall, and he strengthened the Millo, the city of David, and he made many swords and shields.

And he set captains of war over the people, and gathered them together to him in the road place at the gate of the city, and spoke encouragement to them saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:7)


חִזְק֣וּ וְאִמְצ֔וּ אַל־תִּֽירְא֣וּ וְאַל־תֵּחַ֗תּוּ מִפְּנֵי֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר וּמִלִּפְנֵ֖י כׇּל־הֶהָמ֣וֹן אֲשֶׁר־עִמּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־עִמָּ֥נוּ רַ֖ב מֵֽעִמּֽוֹ׃

Be strong and courageous, be not afraid or dismayed on account of the king of Ashshur, or on account of the multitude that is with him: for there are more with us than with him:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Strengthen yourselves and be firm. Do not fear and do not break before the king of Ashur and before all the multitude that is with him, for with us there is more than with him.


###### II Chronicles 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:8)


עִמּוֹ֙ זְר֣וֹעַ בָּשָׂ֔ר וְעִמָּ֜נוּ יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֵ֙ינוּ֙ לְעׇזְרֵ֔נוּ וּלְהִלָּחֵ֖ם מִלְחֲמֹתֵ֑נוּ וַיִּסָּמְכ֣וּ הָעָ֔ם עַל־דִּבְרֵ֖י יְחִזְקִיָּ֥הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָֽה׃{פ}

with him is an arm of flesh; but with us is the Lord our God to help us, and to fight our battles. And the people took confidence from the words of Yeĥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With him is an arm of flesh, but with us is the L rd our G d to aid us and to fight our wars. And the people took support from the words of Yechizkiyahu king of Judah.


###### II Chronicles 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:9)


אַ֣חַר זֶ֗ה שָׁ֠לַ֠ח סַנְחֵרִ֨יב מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֤וּר עֲבָדָיו֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔יְמָה וְהוּא֙ עַל־לָכִ֔ישׁ וְכׇל־מֶמְשַׁלְתּ֖וֹ עִמּ֑וֹ עַל־יְחִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה וְעַל־כׇּל־יְהוּדָ֛ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר בִּירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם לֵאמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After this Sanĥeriv king of Ashshur sent his servants to Yerushalayim, (for he himself laid siege to Lakhish, and all his power with him,) to Yeĥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda, and to all Yehuda who were at Yerushalayim, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After this, Sancherev king of Ashur sent his servants to Jerusalem — (while he was besieging Lachish, and all his officers with him) — to Chizkiyahu king of Judah and to all of Judah in Jerusalem, saying:


###### II Chronicles 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:10)


כֹּ֣ה אָמַ֔ר סַנְחֵרִ֖יב מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר עַל־מָה֙ אַתֶּ֣ם בֹּטְחִ֔ים וְיֹשְׁבִ֥ים בְּמָצ֖וֹר בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus says Sanĥeriv king of Ashshur, On what do you trust, that you stand siege in Yerushalayim?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Thus says Sancheriv king of Ashur: In what do you trust, sitting under siege in Jerusalem?


###### II Chronicles 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:11)


הֲלֹ֤א יְחִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ מַסִּ֣ית אֶתְכֶ֔ם לָתֵ֣ת אֶתְכֶ֔ם לָמ֛וּת בְּרָעָ֥ב וּבְצָמָ֖א לֵאמֹ֑ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ יַצִּילֵ֕נוּ מִכַּ֖ף מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not Yeĥizqiyyahu persuading you to give yourselves over to die by famine and by thirst, saying, The Lord our God shall deliver us out of the hand of the king of Ashshur?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is not Chizkiyahu enticing you to deliver you unto death in famine and in thirst, saying: "The L rd our G d will save us from the hand of the king of Ashur"?


###### II Chronicles 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:12)


הֲלֹא־הוּא֙ יְחִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ הֵסִ֥יר אֶת־בָּמֹתָ֖יו וְאֶת־מִזְבְּחֹתָ֑יו וַיֹּ֨אמֶר לִיהוּדָ֤ה וְלִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לִפְנֵ֨י מִזְבֵּ֧חַ אֶחָ֛ד תִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֖וּ וְעָלָ֥יו תַּקְטִֽירוּ׃

Has not the same Yeĥizqiyyahu taken away his high places and his altars, and commanded Yehuda and Yerushalayim, saying, You shall worship before one altar, and burn incense upon it?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did not he, Yechizkiyahu, himself, remove His high places and His altars [(thus limiting His service)] and say to Judah and to Jerusalem: Before one altar [(the Temple altar)] shall you bow down, and upon it shall you burn incense?


###### II Chronicles 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:13)


הֲלֹ֣א תֵדְע֗וּ מֶ֤ה עָשִׂ֙יתִי֙ אֲנִ֣י וַאֲבוֹתַ֔י לְכֹ֖ל עַמֵּ֣י הָאֲרָצ֑וֹת הֲיָכ֣וֹל יָֽכְל֗וּ אֱלֹהֵי֙ גּוֹיֵ֣ הָאֲרָצ֔וֹת לְהַצִּ֥יל אֶת־אַרְצָ֖ם מִיָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do you not know what I and my fathers have done to all the peoples of other lands? were the gods of the nations of those lands in any way able to deliver their lands out of my hand?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you not know what I and my fathers did to all the peoples of the lands? Could the gods of the nations of the lands deliver their lands from my hand?


###### II Chronicles 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:14)


מִ֠י בְּֽכׇל־אֱלֹהֵ֞י הַגּוֹיִ֤ם הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֶחֱרִ֣ימוּ אֲבוֹתַ֔י אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָכ֔וֹל לְהַצִּ֥יל אֶת־עַמּ֖וֹ מִיָּדִ֑י כִּ֤י יוּכַל֙ אֱלֹ֣הֵיכֶ֔ם לְהַצִּ֥יל אֶתְכֶ֖ם מִיָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who among all the gods of those nations which my fathers utterly destroyed, could deliver his people out of my hand, that your God should be able to deliver you out of my hand?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who among all the gods of these nations that my fathers destroyed could rescue his people from my hand that G d should be able to rescue you from my hand?


###### II Chronicles 32:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:15)


וְעַתָּ֡ה אַל־יַשִּׁיא֩ אֶתְכֶ֨ם חִזְקִיָּ֜הוּ וְאַל־יַסִּ֨ית אֶתְכֶ֣ם כָּזֹאת֮ וְאַל־תַּאֲמִ֣ינוּ לוֹ֒ כִּֽי־לֹ֣א יוּכַ֗ל כׇּל־אֱל֙וֹהַּ֙ כׇּל־גּ֣וֹי וּמַמְלָכָ֔ה לְהַצִּ֥יל עַמּ֛וֹ מִיָּדִ֖י וּמִיַּ֣ד אֲבוֹתָ֑י אַ֚ף כִּ֣י אֱֽלֹהֵיכֶ֔ם לֹא־יַצִּ֥ילוּ אֶתְכֶ֖ם מִיָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore let not Ḥizqiyyahu deceive you, or persuade you in this manner, and do not believe him: for no god of any nation or kingdom was able to deliver his people out of my hand, and out of the hand of my fathers: how much less shall your God deliver you out of my hand?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, let Chizkiyahu not deceive you and let him not entice you thus, and do not trust in him. For no god of any nation or kingdom could rescue his people from my hand or from the hand of my fathers; how much more so will your G d not rescue you from my hand!


###### II Chronicles 32:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:16)


וְעוֹד֙ דִּבְּר֣וּ עֲבָדָ֔יו עַל־יְהֹוָ֖ה הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְעַ֖ל יְחִזְקִיָּ֥הוּ עַבְדּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And his servants spoke yet more against the Lord God, and against his servant Yeĥizqiyyahu.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And more [(of the same)] did his servants speak against the L rd G d and against Yechizkiyahu His servant.


###### II Chronicles 32:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:17)


וּסְפָרִ֣ים כָּתַ֔ב לְחָרֵ֕ף לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְלֵאמֹ֨ר עָלָ֜יו לֵאמֹ֗ר כֵּאלֹהֵ֞י גּוֹיֵ֤י הָֽאֲרָצוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־הִצִּ֤ילוּ עַמָּם֙ מִיָּדִ֔י כֵּ֣ן לֹֽא־יַצִּ֞יל אֱלֹהֵ֧י יְחִזְקִיָּ֛הוּ עַמּ֖וֹ מִיָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He also wrote letters to insult the Lord God of Yisra᾽el, and to speak against him, saying, As the gods of the nations of other lands have not delivered their people out of my hand, so shall the God of Yeĥizqiyyahu not deliver his people out of my hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he wrote letters to blaspheme the L rd G d of Israel, saying about Him: "Just as the gods of the nations of the lands, who did not rescue their people from my hand, so will the G d of Yechizkiyahu not rescue his people from my hand."


###### II Chronicles 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:18)


וַיִּקְרְא֨וּ בְקוֹל־גָּד֜וֹל יְהוּדִ֗ית עַל־עַ֤ם יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הַחוֹמָ֔ה לְיָֽרְאָ֖ם וּֽלְבַהֲלָ֑ם לְמַ֖עַן יִלְכְּד֥וּ אֶת־הָעִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then they cried with a loud voice in the language of Yehuda to the people of Yerushalayim who were on the wall, to affright them, and to terrify them; that they might take the city.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they called out in the language of Judah to the people of Jerusalem who were on the wall to frighten and confound them in order to take the city [viz. II Kings 19 and Isaiah 37].


###### II Chronicles 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:19)


וַֽיְדַבְּר֔וּ אֶל־אֱלֹהֵ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם כְּעַ֗ל אֱלֹהֵי֙ עַמֵּ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה יְדֵ֥י הָאָדָֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they spoke against the God of Yerushalayim, as against the gods of the peoples of the earth, which were the work of the hands of man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they spoke of the G d of Jerusalem as they did of the gods of the people of the land, the handiwork of man.


###### II Chronicles 32:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:20)


וַיִּתְפַּלֵּ֞ל יְחִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וִישַׁעְיָ֧הוּ בֶן־אָמ֛וֹץ הַנָּבִ֖יא עַל־זֹ֑את וַֽיִּזְעֲק֖וּ הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And for this Yeĥizqiyyahu the king, and the prophet Yesha῾yahu the son of Amoż, prayed and cried to heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King Yechizkiyahu and Isaiah the son of Amotz the prophet prayed over this, and they cried out to Heaven.


###### II Chronicles 32:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:21)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֤ח יְהֹוָה֙ מַלְאָ֔ךְ וַיַּכְחֵ֞ד כׇּל־גִּבּ֥וֹר חַ֙יִל֙ וְנָגִ֣יד וְשָׂ֔ר בְּמַחֲנֵ֖ה מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר וַיָּ֩שׇׁב֩ בְּבֹ֨שֶׁת פָּנִ֜ים לְאַרְצ֗וֹ וַיָּבֹא֙ בֵּ֣ית אֱלֹהָ֔יו (ומיציאו) [וּמִיצִיאֵ֣י] מֵעָ֔יו שָׁ֖ם הִפִּילֻ֥הוּ בֶחָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord sent an angel, who cut off all the mighty men at arms, and the leaders and captains in the camp of the king of Ashshur. So he returned with shame of face to his own land. And when he was come into the house of his god, his own offspring slew him there with the sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd sent an angel and he wiped out every warrior, leader, and officer in the camp of the king of Ashur, and he returned shamefaced to his land. And he came into the house of his god, and [(two)] of the issue of his loins [i.e., his sons] struck him down there with the sword.


###### II Chronicles 32:22

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus the Lord saved Yeĥizqiyyahu and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim from the hand of Sanĥeriv the king of Ashshur, and from the hand of all other, and guided them on every side.

And the L rd saved Yechizkiyahu and the dwellers of Jerusalem from the hand of Sancheriv king of Ashur and from the hand of all [(their foes)], and He led them [(with compassion)] roundabout.


###### II Chronicles 32:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:23)


וְ֠רַבִּ֠ים מְבִיאִ֨ים מִנְחָ֤ה לַֽיהֹוָה֙ לִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וּמִ֨גְדָּנ֔וֹת לִֽיחִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיִּנַּשֵּׂ֛א לְעֵינֵ֥י כׇל־הַגּוֹיִ֖ם מֵאַחֲרֵי־כֵֽן׃{פ}

And many brought tribute to the Lord to Yerushalayim, and presents to Yeĥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda: so that he was magnified in the sight of all nations from thenceforth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And many brought offerings to the L rd to Jerusalem and precious objects to Yechizkiyahu king of Judah, and he was exalted in the eyes of all the nations afterwards.


###### II Chronicles 32:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:24)


בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֔ם חָלָ֥ה יְחִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ עַד־לָמ֑וּת וַיִּתְפַּלֵּל֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֔וֹ וּמוֹפֵ֖ת נָ֥תַן לֽוֹ׃

In those days Yeĥizqiyyahu fell mortally sick, and he prayed to the Lord: and he spoke to him, and he gave him a sign.

###### II Chronicles 32:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:25)


וְלֹֽא־כִגְמֻ֤ל עָלָיו֙ הֵשִׁ֣יב יְחִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ כִּ֥י גָבַ֖הּ לִבּ֑וֹ וַֽיְהִ֤י עָלָיו֙ קֶ֔צֶף וְעַל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃

But not as He had bestowed upon him did Yechizkiyahu repay Him, for his heart grew proud, and the wrath [of the L rd] was upon him and (upon) Judah and Jerusalem [viz. II Kings 20:13].

Nevertheless Yeĥizqiyyahu did humble himself for the pride of his heart, both he and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, so that the wrath of the Lord did not come upon them in the days of Yeĥizqiyyahu.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu was humbled in the haughtiness of his heart, he and the dwellers of Jerusalem, and the wrath of the L rd did not come upon them in the days of Yechizkiyahu.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu had very much wealth and honor, and he made himself treasuries for silver and gold and precious stones and spices and shields and all (kinds of) precious vessels.


###### II Chronicles 32:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:28)


וּמִ֨סְכְּנ֔וֹת לִתְבוּאַ֥ת דָּגָ֖ן וְתִיר֣וֹשׁ וְיִצְהָ֑ר וְאֻֽרָוֺת֙ לְכׇל־בְּהֵמָ֣ה וּבְהֵמָ֔ה וַעֲדָרִ֖ים לָאֲוֵרֹֽת׃

storehouses also for the increase of corn, and wine, and oil; and stables for all manner of beasts, and folds for sheep.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And storehouses for corn produce, wine, and oil, and stalls for all kinds of beasts, and (he acquired) flocks for the stalls.


###### II Chronicles 32:29

וְעָרִים֙ עָ֣שָׂה ל֔וֹ וּמִקְנֵה־צֹ֥אן וּבָקָ֖ר לָרֹ֑ב כִּ֤י נָֽתַן־לוֹ֙ אֱלֹהִ֔ים רְכ֖וּשׁ רַ֥ב מְאֹֽד׃

Moreover he provided cities, and possessions of flocks and herds for himself in abundance: for God had given him very great substance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
