###### II Chronicles 32:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:30)


וְה֣וּא יְחִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ סָתַם֙ אֶת־מוֹצָ֞א מֵימֵ֤י גִיחוֹן֙ הָֽעֶלְי֔וֹן וַֽיַּישְּׁרֵ֥ם לְמַֽטָּה־מַּעְרָ֖בָה לְעִ֣יר דָּוִ֑יד וַיַּצְלַ֥ח יְחִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּכׇֽל־מַעֲשֵֽׂהוּ׃

This same Yeĥizqiyyahu also stopped up the upper watercourse of Giĥon, and brought it straight down to the west side of the city of David. And Yeĥizqiyyahu prospered in all his works.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he, Yechizkiyahu, stopped up the source of the waters of the upper Gichon and led them down on the west of the city of David, and Yechizkiyahu prospered in all of his works.


###### II Chronicles 32:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:31)


וְכֵ֞ן בִּמְלִיצֵ֣י**׀** שָׂרֵ֣י בָבֶ֗ל הַֽמְשַׁלְּחִ֤ים עָלָיו֙ לִדְרֹ֗שׁ הַמּוֹפֵת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה בָאָ֔רֶץ עֲזָב֖וֹ הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים לְנַ֨סּוֹת֔וֹ לָדַ֖עַת כׇּל־בִּלְבָבֽוֹ׃

Howbeit in the matter of the ambassadors of the princes of Bavel, who sent to him to inquire of the wonder that was done in the land, God left him, to try him, that he might know all that was in his heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And thus, [(just as in most of his enterprises the L rd was with him and prospered him)], in [the incident of the messengers of the officers of Bavel, who were sent to him to make inquiry of the wonder that had transpired in the land [viz. II Kings 12-15], the L rd left him [(to his own resources)] to try him, to know all that was in his heart.


###### II Chronicles 32:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:32)


וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י יְחִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ וַחֲסָדָ֑יו הִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים בַּחֲז֞וֹן יְשַׁעְיָ֤הוּ בֶן־אָמוֹץ֙ הַנָּבִ֔יא עַל־סֵ֥פֶר מַלְכֵֽי־יְהוּדָ֖ה וְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the rest of the acts of Yeĥizqiyyahu, and his goodness, behold, they are written in the vision of Yesha῾yahu the prophet, the son of Amoż, and in the book of the kings of Yehuda and Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the account of the reign of Yechizkiyahu and his kindnesses, behold, they are recorded in the prophecy of Isaiah the son of Amotz the prophet in the book of [the chronicles of] the kings of Judah and Israel.


###### II Chronicles 32:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/32:33)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֨ב יְחִזְקִיָּ֜הוּ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֗יו וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֘הוּ֮ בְּֽמַעֲלֵה֮ קִבְרֵ֣י בְנֵי־דָוִיד֒ וְכָבוֹד֙ עָשׂוּ־ל֣וֹ בְמוֹת֔וֹ כׇּל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וְיֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ מְנַשֶּׁ֥ה בְנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yeĥizqiyyahu slept with his fathers, and they buried him in the ascent of the tombs of the sons of David: and all Yehuda and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim did him honour at his death. And Menashshe his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yechizkiyahu rested with his fathers, and they buried him in the choicest of the graves of the sons of David. And all of Judah and the dwellers of Judah did him honor in his death, and Menasheh his son reigned in his place.



## Chapter 33

###### II Chronicles 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:1)


בֶּן־שְׁתֵּ֥ים עֶשְׂרֵ֛ה שָׁנָ֖ה מְנַשֶּׁ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֑וֹ וַחֲמִשִּׁ֤ים וְחָמֵשׁ֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Menashshe was twelve years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for fifty five years in Yerushalayim:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Menasheh was twelve years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for fifty-five years in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּתֽוֹעֲבוֹת֙ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁר֙ הוֹרִ֣ישׁ יְהֹוָ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but he did that which was evil in the sight of the Lord, like the abominations of the nations, whom the Lord had cast out before the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was evil in the eyes of the L rd, as the abominations of the nations which the L rd had driven out before the children of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:3)


וַיָּ֗שׇׁב וַיִּ֙בֶן֙ אֶת־הַבָּמ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִתַּ֖ץ יְחִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ אָבִ֑יו וַיָּ֨קֶם מִזְבְּח֤וֹת לַבְּעָלִים֙ וַיַּ֣עַשׂ אֲשֵׁר֔וֹת וַיִּשְׁתַּ֙חוּ֙ לְכׇל־צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַֽיַּעֲבֹ֖ד אֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he built again the high places which Yeĥizqiyyahu his father had broken down, and he reared up altars for the Be῾alim, and made asherot, and worshipped all the host of heaven, and served them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he rebuilt the mounds that Chizkiyahu his father had destroyed, and he set up altars to the ba'alim, and he made asheiroth, and he bowed down to all the host of heaven and served them.


###### II Chronicles 33:4

וּבָנָ֥ה מִזְבְּח֖וֹת בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה בִּירוּשָׁלַ֥͏ִם יִֽהְיֶה־שְּׁמִ֖י לְעוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also he built altars in the house of the Lord, of which the Lord had said, In Yerushalayim shall my name be for ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he built altars in the house of the L rd, of which the L rd had said: In Jerusalem will I place My name forever.

וַיִּ֥בֶן מִזְבְּח֖וֹת לְכׇל־צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם בִּשְׁתֵּ֖י חַצְר֥וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָֽה׃

And he built altars for all the host of heaven in the two courts of the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he built altars to all the host of heaven in the two courts of the house of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:6)


וְהוּא֩ הֶעֱבִ֨יר אֶת־בָּנָ֤יו בָּאֵשׁ֙ בְּגֵ֣י בֶן־הִנֹּ֔ם וְעוֹנֵ֤ן וְנִחֵשׁ֙ וְֽכִשֵּׁ֔ף וְעָ֥שָׂה א֖וֹב וְיִדְּעוֹנִ֑י הִרְבָּ֗ה לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהַכְעִיסֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he caused his children to pass through the fire in the valley of the son of Hinnom: also he practised divination, and sorcery, and witchcraft, and dealt with mediums, and with wizards: he did much evil in the sight of the Lord, to provoke him to anger.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he passed his sons through fire in the valley of Ben-hinnom, and onein and nichesh and kishef [viz. Deuteronomy 18:10] and dealt with ov and yidoni [viz. Leviticus 19:31]. He was profuse in doing evil in the eyes of the L rd to anger Him.

וְלֹ֣א אֹסִ֗יף לְהָסִיר֙ אֶת־רֶ֣גֶל יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מֵעַל֙ הָֽאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֶעֱמַ֖דְתִּי לַאֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם רַ֣ק**׀** אִם־יִשְׁמְר֣וּ לַעֲשׂ֗וֹת אֵ֚ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוִּיתִ֔ים לְכׇל־הַתּוֹרָ֛ה וְהַחֻקִּ֥ים וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֖ים בְּיַד־מֹשֶֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
nor will I remove the foot of Yisra᾽el again from out of the land which I have appointed for your fathers; if only they will take heed to do all that I have commanded them, according to the whole Tora and the statutes and the ordinances by the hand of Moshe.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

And he did what was evil in the eyes of the L rd, as Menasheh his father had done. And Amon sacrificed to all the graven images that Menasheh his father had made, and he served them.


###### II Chronicles 33:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:23)


וְלֹ֤א נִכְנַע֙ מִלִּפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה כְּהִכָּנַ֖ע מְנַשֶּׁ֣ה אָבִ֑יו כִּ֛י ה֥וּא אָמ֖וֹן הִרְבָּ֥ה אַשְׁמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he did not humble himself before the Lord, as Menashshe his father had humbled himself; but Amon trespassed more and more.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he was not humbled before the L rd as Menasheh his father had humbled himself, for he, Amon, increased guilt.


###### II Chronicles 33:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:24)


וַיִּקְשְׁר֤וּ עָלָיו֙ עֲבָדָ֔יו וַיְמִיתֻ֖הוּ בְּבֵיתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And his servants conspired against him, and slew him in his own house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his servants conspired against him and put him to death in his house.


###### II Chronicles 33:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/33:25)


וַיַּכּוּ֙ עַם־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַקֹּשְׁרִ֖ים עַל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אָמ֑וֹן וַיַּמְלִ֧יכוּ עַם־הָאָ֛רֶץ אֶת־יֹאשִׁיָּ֥הוּ בְנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the people of the land slew all those who had conspired against king Amon; and the people of the land made Yoshiyyahu his son king in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the people of the land smote all the conspirators against King Amon, and the people of the land made Yoshiyahu his son king in his place.



## Chapter 34

###### II Chronicles 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:1)


בֶּן־שְׁמוֹנֶ֥ה שָׁנִ֖ים יֹאשִׁיָּ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֑וֹ וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֤ים וְאַחַת֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yoshiyyahu was eight years old when he began to reign, and he reigned in Yerushalayim for thirty one years.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yoshiyahu was eight years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for thirty-one years in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיֵּ֗לֶךְ בְּדַרְכֵי֙ דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֔יו וְלֹא־סָ֖ר יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹֽאול׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did that which was right in the sight of the Lord, and followed in the ways of David his father, declining neither to the right hand, nor to the left.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was just in the eyes of the L rd, and he went in the ways of David his father, not turning right or left.


###### II Chronicles 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:3)


וּבִשְׁמוֹנֶ֨ה שָׁנִ֜ים לְמׇלְכ֗וֹ וְהוּא֙ עוֹדֶ֣נּוּ נַ֔עַר הֵחֵ֕ל לִדְר֕וֹשׁ לֵאלֹהֵ֖י דָּוִ֣יד אָבִ֑יו וּבִשְׁתֵּ֧ים עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֗ה הֵחֵל֙ לְטַהֵ֗ר אֶת־יְהוּדָה֙ וִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם מִן־הַבָּמוֹת֙ וְהָ֣אֲשֵׁרִ֔ים וְהַפְּסִלִ֖ים וְהַמַּסֵּכֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For in the eighth year of his reign, while he was still young, he began to seek after the God of David his father: and in the twelfth year he began to purge Yehuda and Yerushalayim of the high places, and the asherim, and the carved idols, and the molten images.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in his eighth year, when he became king, when yet a youth, he began to seek out the G d of David his father. And in his twelfth year, he began to purge Judah and Jerusalem of the mounds and the asherim and the graven images and the molten images.


###### II Chronicles 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:4)


וַיְנַתְּצ֣וּ לְפָנָ֗יו אֵ֚ת מִזְבְּח֣וֹת הַבְּעָלִ֔ים וְהַחַמָּנִ֛ים אֲשֶׁר־לְמַ֥עְלָה מֵעֲלֵיהֶ֖ם גִּדֵּ֑עַ וְ֠הָאֲשֵׁרִ֠ים וְהַפְּסִלִ֤ים וְהַמַּסֵּכוֹת֙ שִׁבַּ֣ר וְהֵדַ֔ק וַיִּזְרֹק֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַקְּבָרִ֔ים הַזֹּבְחִ֖ים לָהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they broke down the altars of the Be῾alim in his presence; and the sun images, that were on high above them, he cut down; and the asherim, and the carved idols, and the molten images, he broke in pieces, and made dust of them, and strewed it upon the graves of those who had sacrificed to them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they razed before him the altars of the ba'alim; and the sun images above them he cut down. And the asherim and the graven images and the molten images he broke and ground down, and he cast [(the grindings)] on the face of the graves of those who had sacrificed to them [viz. I Kings 13:2].


###### II Chronicles 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:5)


וְעַצְמוֹת֙ כֹּֽהֲנִ֔ים שָׂרַ֖ף עַל־[מִזְבְּחוֹתָ֑ם] (מזבחותים) וַיְטַהֵ֥ר אֶת־יְהוּדָ֖ה וְאֶת־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he burnt the bones of the priests upon their altars, and cleansed Yehuda and Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the bones of [(dead idolatrous)] priests he burned upon their altars, and he purged Judah and Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:6)


וּבְעָרֵ֨י מְנַשֶּׁ֧ה וְאֶפְרַ֛יִם וְשִׁמְע֖וֹן וְעַד־נַפְתָּלִ֑י (בחר בתיהם) [בְּחַרְבֹתֵיהֶ֖ם] סָבִֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And so he did in the cities of Menashshe, and Efrayim, and Shim῾on even to Naftali, in their ruins round about.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:7)


וַיְנַתֵּ֣ץ אֶת־הַֽמִּזְבְּח֗וֹת וְאֶת־הָאֲשֵׁרִ֤ים וְהַפְּסִלִים֙ כִּתַּ֣ת לְהֵדַ֔ק וְכׇל־הַחַמָּנִ֥ים גִּדַּ֖ע בְּכׇל־אֶ֣רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיָּ֖שׇׁב לִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when he had broken down the altars and the asherim, and had beaten the carved idols into dust, and cut down all the sun images throughout all the land of Yisra᾽el, he returned to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he razed the altars and the asherim; and the graven images he crushed into dust. And all the sun images he cut down in all the land of Israel, and he returned to Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:8)


וּבִשְׁנַ֨ת שְׁמוֹנֶ֤ה עֶשְׂרֵה֙ לְמׇלְכ֔וֹ לְטַהֵ֥ר הָאָ֖רֶץ וְהַבָּ֑יִת שָׁ֠לַ֠ח אֶת־שָׁפָ֨ן בֶּן־אֲצַלְיָ֜הוּ וְאֶת־מַעֲשֵׂיָ֣הוּ שַׂר־הָעִ֗יר וְ֠אֵ֠ת יוֹאָ֤ח בֶּן־יֽוֹאָחָז֙ הַמַּזְכִּ֔יר לְחַזֵּ֕ק אֶת־בֵּ֖ית יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now in the eighteenth year of his reign, when he had purged the land, and the house, he sent Shafan the son of Ażalyahu, and Ma῾aseyahu the governor of the city, and Yo᾽aĥ the son of Yo᾽aĥaz the recorder, to repair the house of the Lord his God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the eighteenth year of his reign, having cleansed the land and the Temple, he sent Shafan the son of Atzalyahu, Ma'aseyahu, the governor of the city, and Yoach the son of Yoachaz the recorder to strengthen the house of the L rd his G d.


###### II Chronicles 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:9)


וַיָּבֹ֜אוּ אֶל־חִלְקִיָּ֣הוּ**׀** הַכֹּהֵ֣ן הַגָּד֗וֹל וַֽיִּתְּנוּ֮ אֶת־הַכֶּ֘סֶף֮ הַמּוּבָ֣א בֵית־אֱלֹהִים֒ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָסְפֽוּ־הַלְוִיִּם֩ שֹׁמְרֵ֨י הַסַּ֜ף מִיַּ֧ד מְנַשֶּׁ֣ה וְאֶפְרַ֗יִם וּמִכֹּל֙ שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וּמִכׇּל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וּבִנְיָמִ֑ן (וישבי) [וַיָּשׁ֖וּבוּ] יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when they came to Ḥilqiyyahu the high priest, they delivered the money that was brought into the house of God, which the Levites who kept the doors had gathered from the hand of Menashshe and Efrayim, and from all the remnant of Yisra᾽el, and from all Yehuda and Binyamin; and they returned to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they came to Chilkiyahu, the high-priest, and they gave him the money brought to the house of G d, collected by the Levites, the gatekeepers, from Menasheh and Ephraim, and from all the remnant of Israel, and from all of Judah and Benjamin, and they returned to Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:10)


וַֽיִּתְּנ֗וּ עַל־יַד֙ עֹשֵׂ֣ה הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה הַמֻּפְקָדִ֖ים בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּתְּנ֨וּ אֹת֜וֹ עוֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר עֹשִׂים֙ בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה לִבְדּ֥וֹק וּלְחַזֵּ֖ק הַבָּֽיִת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they put it in the hand of the workmen who had the oversight of the house of the Lord, and they gave it to the workmen who were employed in the house of the Lord, to repair and mend the house:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they gave it to the [treasurers] over the doers of the work appointed in the house of the L rd, and they gave it to the workers in the house of the L rd to seek out and repair [the breaches in] the house.


###### II Chronicles 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:11)


וַֽיִּתְּנ֗וּ לֶחָֽרָשִׁים֙ וְלַבֹּנִ֔ים לִקְנוֹת֙ אַבְנֵ֣י מַחְצֵ֔ב וְעֵצִ֖ים לַֽמְחַבְּר֑וֹת וּלְקָרוֹת֙ אֶת־הַבָּ֣תִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִשְׁחִ֖יתוּ מַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to the carpenters and builders they give it, to buy hewn stone, and timber for couplings, and to make beams for the houses which the kings of Yehuda had destroyed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they gave it to the carpenters and the builders to buy hewn stone and wood for the couplings and to roof the houses which the kings of Judah had destroyed.


###### II Chronicles 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:12)


וְהָאֲנָשִׁים֩ עֹשִׂ֨ים בֶּאֱמוּנָ֜ה בַּמְּלָאכָ֗ה וַעֲלֵיהֶ֣ם׀מֻ֠פְקָדִ֠ים יַ֣חַת וְעֹבַדְיָ֤הוּ הַלְוִיִּם֙ מִן־בְּנֵ֣י מְרָרִ֔י וּזְכַרְיָ֧ה וּמְשֻׁלָּ֛ם מִן־בְּנֵ֥י הַקְּהָתִ֖ים לְנַצֵּ֑חַ וְהַ֨לְוִיִּ֔ם כׇּל־מֵבִ֖ין בִּכְלֵי־שִֽׁיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the men did the work faithfully: and their overseers were Yaĥat and ῾Ovadyahu the Levites, of the sons of Merari; and Zekharya and Meshullam, of the sons of the Qehatim, to supervise it; and other of the Levites, all who had skill in instruments of music.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the men did their work in good faith. And appointed over them were Yachath and Ovadyahu the Levites of the sons of Merari and Zecharyah and Meshulam of the sons of the Kehothites, to supervise (the work), and the Levites, all skilled instrumentalists.


###### II Chronicles 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:13)


וְעַ֣ל הַסַּבָּלִ֗ים וּֽמְנַצְּחִים֙ לְכֹל֙ עֹשֵׂ֣ה מְלָאכָ֔ה לַעֲבוֹדָ֖ה וַעֲבוֹדָ֑ה וּמֵֽהַלְוִיִּ֔ם סוֹפְרִ֥ים וְשֹׁטְרִ֖ים וְשׁוֹעֲרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also they were over bearers of burdens, and they had control over all who were employed in the work in any manner of service: and of the Levites there were scribes, and officers, and gatekeepers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(overseers were appointed)] over the porters, and supervisors over all of the workers, for every (kind of) work. And, of the Levites, scribes, officers, and gatekeepers.


###### II Chronicles 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:14)


וּבְהוֹצִיאָ֣ם אֶת־הַכֶּ֔סֶף הַמּוּבָ֖א בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה מָצָא֙ חִלְקִיָּ֣הוּ הַכֹּהֵ֔ן אֶת־סֵ֥פֶר תּוֹרַת־יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּיַד־מֹשֶֽׁה׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### II Chronicles 34:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:15)


וַיַּ֣עַן חִלְקִיָּ֗הוּ וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֶל־שָׁפָ֣ן הַסּוֹפֵ֔ר סֵ֧פֶר הַתּוֹרָ֛ה מָצָ֖אתִי בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּתֵּ֧ן חִלְקִיָּ֛הוּ אֶת־הַסֵּ֖פֶר אֶל־שָׁפָֽן׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Chilkiyahu spoke and said to Shafan the scribe: I have found the scroll of the Torah in the house of the L rd. And Chilkiyahu gave the scroll to Shafan.


###### II Chronicles 34:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/34:16)


וַיָּבֵ֨א שָׁפָ֤ן אֶת־הַסֵּ֙פֶר֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַיָּ֨שֶׁב ע֧וֹד אֶת־הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ דָּבָ֖ר לֵאמֹ֑ר כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־נִתַּ֥ן בְּיַד־עֲבָדֶ֖יךָ הֵ֥ם עֹשִֽׂים׃

And Shafan carried the book to the king, and further brought the king word back, saying, All that was committed to thy servants they are doing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Shafan brought the scroll to the king, and he also returned word to the king, saying: Everything entrusted to your servants they are doing.


###### II Chronicles 34:17

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

For great is the wrath of the L rd that has been poured out against us because our fathers did not heed the word of the L rd to do according to all that is written in this scroll.

because they have forsaken me, and have burned incense to other gods, that they might provoke me to anger with all the works of their hands; therefore my wrath shall be poured out upon this place, and shall not be quenched.

## Chapter 35

###### II Chronicles 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:1)


וַיַּ֨עַשׂ יֹאשִׁיָּ֧הוּ בִירוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם פֶּ֖סַח לַיהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּשְׁחֲט֣וּ הַפֶּ֔סַח בְּאַרְבָּעָ֥ה עָשָׂ֖ר לַחֹ֥דֶשׁ הָרִאשֽׁוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover Yoshiyyahu kept a passover to the Lord in Yerushalayim: and they killed the paschal lamb on the fourteenth day of the first month.

After all this, when Yoshiyyahu had prepared the temple, Nekho king of Miżrayim came up to fight against Karkemish by Perat: and Yoshiyyahu went out against him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After all this, Yoshiyahu having established [the order of] the house, Nechoh, king of Egypt, went up to war in Karkemish near the Euphrates, and Yoshiyahu went out against him.


###### II Chronicles 35:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:21)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח אֵלָ֣יו מַלְאָכִ֣ים׀לֵאמֹר֩׀מַה־לִּ֨י וָלָ֜ךְ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֗ה לֹא־עָלֶ֨יךָ אַתָּ֤ה הַיּוֹם֙ כִּ֚י אֶל־בֵּ֣ית מִלְחַמְתִּ֔י וֵאלֹהִ֖ים אָמַ֣ר לְבַהֲלֵ֑נִי חֲדַל־לְךָ֛ מֵאֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־עִמִּ֖י וְאַל־יַשְׁחִיתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he sent ambassadors to him, saying, What have I to do with thee, thou king of Yehuda? I come not against thee this day, but against the house with which I am at war: for God commanded me to make haste: forbear from meddling with God, who is with me, so that he does not destroy thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(Nechoh)] sent messengers to him, saying: What [dispute is there between] me and you, king of Judah? Not against you [do I go out] today, but to my house of war [(against the king of Ashur)], and G d has told me to make haste. Forbear from [resisting] G d who is with me, that He not destroy you.


###### II Chronicles 35:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:22)


וְלֹא־הֵסֵב֩ יֹאשִׁיָּ֨הוּ פָנָ֜יו מִמֶּ֗נּוּ כִּ֤י לְהִלָּחֵֽם־בּוֹ֙ הִתְחַפֵּ֔שׂ וְלֹ֥א שָׁמַ֛ע אֶל־דִּבְרֵ֥י נְכ֖וֹ מִפִּ֣י אֱלֹהִ֑ים וַיָּבֹ֕א לְהִלָּחֵ֖ם בְּבִקְעַ֥ת מְגִדּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless Yoshiyyahu would not turn his face from him, but he sought an opportunity to fight with him, and he did not hearken to the words of Nekho from the mouth of God, and came to fight in the valley of Megiddo.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But Yoshiyahu did not turn his face away from him, for in order to fight him he had disguised himself. And he did not pay heed to the words of Nechoh from the mouth of G d, and he came to do battle in the valley of Megiddo.


###### II Chronicles 35:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:23)


וַיֹּרוּ֙ הַיֹּרִ֔ים לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ יֹאשִׁיָּ֑הוּ וַיֹּ֨אמֶר הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ לַעֲבָדָיו֙ הַעֲבִיר֔וּנִי כִּ֥י הׇחֳלֵ֖יתִי מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the archers shot at king Yoshiyyahu; and the king said to his servants. Get me away; for I am grievously wounded.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the archers shot at King Yoshiyahu. And the king said to his servants: Take me away for I am sorely smitten!


###### II Chronicles 35:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:24)


וַיַּעֲבִירֻ֨הוּ עֲבָדָ֜יו מִן־הַמֶּרְכָּבָ֗ה וַֽיַּרְכִּיבֻ֘הוּ֮ עַ֣ל רֶ֣כֶב הַמִּשְׁנֶה֮ אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ֒ וַיּוֹלִיכֻ֙הוּ֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַיָּ֕מׇת וַיִּקָּבֵ֖ר בְּקִבְר֣וֹת אֲבֹתָ֑יו וְכׇל־יְהוּדָה֙ וִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם מִֽתְאַבְּלִ֖ים עַל־יֹאשִׁיָּֽהוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So his servants took him out of that chariot, and put him in the second chariot that he had; and they brought him to Yerushalayim, and he died, and was buried in the tombs of his fathers. And all Yehuda and Yerushalayim mourned for Yoshiyyahu.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his servants transferred him from the chariot and drove him in his second chariot. And they brought him to Jerusalem and he died (there), and he was buried in the crypts of his fathers. And all of Judah and Jerusalem mourned Yoshiyahu.


###### II Chronicles 35:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:25)


וַיְקוֹנֵ֣ן יִרְמְיָ֘הוּ֮ עַל־יֹאשִׁיָּ֒הוּ֒ וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ כׇֽל־הַשָּׁרִ֣ים׀וְ֠הַשָּׁר֠וֹת בְּקִינ֨וֹתֵיהֶ֤ם עַל־יֹאשִׁיָּ֙הוּ֙ עַד־הַיּ֔וֹם וַיִּתְּנ֥וּם לְחֹ֖ק עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְהִנָּ֥ם כְּתוּבִ֖ים עַל־הַקִּינֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yirmeyahu lamented for Yoshiyyahu: and all the singing men and the singing women spoke of Yoshiyyahu in their laments to this day, and made them an ordinance in Yisra᾽el: and, behold, they are written in the laments.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Jeremiah lamented Yoshiyahu. And all the singers and songstresses mentioned Yoshiyahu in their lamentations, (as they do) until this day. And they made them [(such lamentations)] a statute for Israel; and, behold, they [(the lamentations over Yoshiyahu)] are written in the [book of] lamentations [(viz. Eichah 4)].


###### II Chronicles 35:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:26)


וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י יֹאשִׁיָּ֖הוּ וַחֲסָדָ֑יו כַּכָּת֖וּב בְּתוֹרַ֥ת יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the rest of the acts of Yoshiyyahu, and his goodness, according to that which was written in the Tora of the Lord,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the account of the reign of Yoshiyahu and his kindnesses according to what is written in the Torah of the L rd,


###### II Chronicles 35:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/35:27)


וּדְבָרָ֕יו הָרִאשֹׁנִ֖ים וְהָאַחֲרֹנִ֑ים הִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֔ים עַל־סֵ֥פֶר מַלְכֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וִיהוּדָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and his deeds, first and last, behold, they are written in the book of the kings of Yisra᾽el and Yehuda.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and his earlier and later deeds, behold, they are recorded in the book of [the chronicles of] the kings of Israel and Judah.



## Chapter 36

###### II Chronicles 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:1)


וַיִּקְחוּ֙ עַם־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֶת־יְהוֹאָחָ֖ז בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֑הוּ וַיַּמְלִיכֻ֥הוּ תַֽחַת־אָבִ֖יו בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the people of the land took Yeho᾽aĥaz the son of Yoshiyyahu, and made him king in his father’s stead in Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the people of the land took Yehoachaz the son of Yoshiyahu and they made him king in place of his father in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:2)


בֶּן־שָׁל֧וֹשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִ֛ים שָׁנָ֖ה יוֹאָחָ֣ז בְּמׇלְכ֑וֹ וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה חֳדָשִׁ֔ים מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yeho᾽aĥaz was twenty three years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for three months in Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yehoachaz was twenty-three years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for three months in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:3)


וַיְסִירֵ֥הוּ מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֖יִם בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַֽיַּעֲנֹשׁ֙ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ מֵאָ֥ה כִכַּר־כֶּ֖סֶף וְכִכַּ֥ר זָהָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of Miżrayim deposed him at Yerushalayim and mulcted the land a hundred talents of silver and a talent of gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Egypt deposed him in Jerusalem. And he imposed a fine on the land, one hundred talents of silver and a talent of gold.


###### II Chronicles 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:4)


וַיַּמְלֵ֨ךְ מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֜יִם אֶת־אֶלְיָקִ֣ים אָחִ֗יו עַל־יְהוּדָה֙ וִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַיַּסֵּ֥ב אֶת־שְׁמ֖וֹ יְהוֹיָקִ֑ים וְאֶת־יוֹאָחָ֤ז אָחִיו֙ לָקַ֣ח נְכ֔וֹ וַיְבִיאֵ֖הוּ מִצְרָֽיְמָה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king of Miżrayim made Elyaqim his brother king over Yehuda and Yerushalayim, and turned his name to Yehoyaqim. And Nekho took Yeho᾽aĥaz his brother, and carried him to Miżrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king of Egypt made Elyakim his brother king over Judah and Jerusalem, and he changed his name to Yehoyakim. And Necho took Yoachaz his brother and brought him to Egypt.


###### II Chronicles 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:5)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְחָמֵ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ יְהוֹיָקִ֣ים בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְאַחַ֤ת עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיַּ֣עַשׂ הָרַ֔ע בְּעֵינֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yehoyaqim was twenty five years old when began to reign, and he reigned for eleven years in Yerushalayim: and he did that which was evil in the sight of the Lord his God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yehoyakim was twenty-five years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for eleven years in Jerusalem. And he did what was evil in the eyes of the L rd his G d.


###### II Chronicles 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:6)


עָלָ֣יו עָלָ֔ה נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֖ר מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֑ל וַיַּאַסְרֵ֙הוּ֙ בַּֽנְחֻשְׁתַּ֔יִם לְהֹלִיכ֖וֹ בָּבֶֽלָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Against him came up Nevukhadneżżar king of Bavel, and bound him in fetters, to carry him to Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Against him Nevuchadnezzar king of Bavel went up, and he bound him in copper chains to bring him to Bavel.


###### II Chronicles 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:7)


וּמִכְּלֵי֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה הֵבִ֥יא נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֖ר לְבָבֶ֑ל וַיִּתְּנֵ֥ם בְּהֵיכָל֖וֹ בְּבָבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Nevukhadneżżar carried some of the vessels of the house of the Lord to Bavel, and put them in his temple at Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And (some) of the vessels of the house of the L rd Nevuchadnezzar brought to Bavel, and he placed them in his sanctuary in Bavel.


###### II Chronicles 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:8)


וְיֶ֩תֶר֩ דִּבְרֵ֨י יְהוֹיָקִ֜ים וְתֹעֲבֹתָ֤יו אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂה֙ וְהַנִּמְצָ֣א עָלָ֔יו הִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר מַלְכֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וִיהוּדָ֑ה וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ יְהוֹיָכִ֥ין בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the rest of the acts of Yehoyaqim, and his abominations which he did, and that which was found in him, behold, they are written in the book of the kings of Yisra᾽el and Yehuda: and Yehoyakhin his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the account of the reign of Yehoyakim, and the abominations that he committed, and what was found [out] concerning him, behold, they are recorded in the book of [the chronicles of] the kings of Judah and Israel. And Yehoyachin his son reigned in his place.


###### II Chronicles 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:9)


בֶּן־שְׁמוֹנֶ֤ה שָׁנִים֙ יְהוֹיָכִ֣ין בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֤ה חֳדָשִׁים֙ וַעֲשֶׂ֣רֶת יָמִ֔ים מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yehoyakhin was eight years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for three months and ten days in Yerushalayim: and he did that which was evil in the sight of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yehoyachin was eight years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for three months and ten days in Jerusalem, and he did what was evil in the eyes of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:10)


וְלִתְשׁוּבַ֣ת הַשָּׁנָ֗ה שָׁלַח֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ נְבֽוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֔ר וַיְבִאֵ֣הוּ בָבֶ֔לָה עִם־כְּלֵ֖י חֶמְדַּ֣ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיַּמְלֵךְ֙ אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ אָחִ֔יו עַל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And at the return of the year, king Nevukhadneżżar sent, and brought him to Bavel, with the goodly vessels of the house of the Lord, and made Żidqiyyahu his brother king over Yehuda and Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And at the return of the year, King Nevuchadnezzar sent and brought him to Bavel with the precious vessels of the house of the L rd, and he made Tzidkiyahu his brother king over Judah and Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:11)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֧ים וְאַחַ֛ת שָׁנָ֖ה צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֑וֹ וְאַחַ֤ת עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Żidqiyyahu was twenty one years old when he began to reign, and he reigned for eleven years in Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Tzidkiyahu was twenty-one years old when he [began] to reign, and he reigned for eleven years in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:12)


וַיַּ֣עַשׂ הָרַ֔ע בְּעֵינֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֑יו לֹ֣א נִכְנַ֗ע מִלִּפְנֵ֛י יִרְמְיָ֥הוּ הַנָּבִ֖יא מִפִּ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he did that which was evil in the sight of the Lord his God, and did not humble himself before Yirmeyahu the prophet speaking from the mouth of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did what was evil in the eyes of the L rd. He did not humble himself before the prophet [prophesying] from the mouth of the L rd.


###### II Chronicles 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:13)


וְ֠גַ֠ם בַּמֶּ֤לֶךְ נְבֽוּכַדְנֶאצַּר֙ מָרָ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִשְׁבִּיע֖וֹ בֵּאלֹהִ֑ים וַיֶּ֤קֶשׁ אֶת־עׇרְפּוֹ֙ וַיְאַמֵּ֣ץ אֶת־לְבָב֔וֹ מִשּׁ֕וּב אֶל־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he also rebelled against king Nevukhad­neżżar, who had made him swear by God: and he stiffened his neck, and hardened his heart from turning to the Lord God of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he rebelled, too, against King Nevuchadnezzar, who had besworn him by G d [viz. Ezekiel 17:12, etc.]. And he stiffened his neck and hardened his heart against returning to the L rd, the G d of Israel.


###### II Chronicles 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:14)


גַּ֠ם כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֨י הַכֹּהֲנִ֤ים וְהָעָם֙ הִרְבּ֣וּ (למעול) [לִמְעׇל־]מַ֔עַל כְּכֹ֖ל תֹּעֲב֣וֹת הַגּוֹיִ֑ם וַֽיְטַמְּאוּ֙ אֶת־בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִקְדִּ֖ישׁ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover all the chiefs of the priests, and the people, transgressed very much according to all the abominations of the nations; and polluted the house of the Lord which he had hallowed in Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Also, all the chiefs of the priests and the people were profuse in faithlessness, [doing] according to all of the abominations of the nations. And they defiled the house of the L rd, which He had consecrated in Jerusalem.


###### II Chronicles 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:15)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֡ח יְהֹוָה֩ אֱלֹהֵ֨י אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֧ם עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם בְּיַ֥ד מַלְאָכָ֖יו הַשְׁכֵּ֣ם וְשָׁל֑וֹחַ כִּֽי־חָמַ֥ל עַל־עַמּ֖וֹ וְעַל־מְעוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord God of their fathers sent to them by his messengers, from morning till night; because he had compassion on his people, and on his dwelling place:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd, the G d of their fathers, sent [His words] to them through His messengers [(the prophets)], sending betimes, for He compassionated His people and His abode.


###### II Chronicles 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:16)


וַיִּהְי֤וּ מַלְעִבִים֙ בְּמַלְאֲכֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וּבוֹזִ֣ים דְּבָרָ֔יו וּמִֽתַּעְתְּעִ֖ים בִּנְבִאָ֑יו עַ֠ד עֲל֧וֹת חֲמַת־יְהֹוָ֛ה בְּעַמּ֖וֹ עַד־לְאֵ֥ין מַרְפֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but they mocked the messengers of God, and despised his words, and scoffed at his prophets, until the wrath of the Lord mounted against his people, till there was no remedy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But they mocked the messengers of G d and scorned His words and scoffed at His prophets, until the L rd's wrath rose against His people, beyond healing.


###### II Chronicles 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:17)


וַיַּ֨עַל עֲלֵיהֶ֜ם אֶת־מֶ֣לֶךְ (כשדיים) [כַּשְׂדִּ֗ים] וַיַּהֲרֹ֨ג בַּחוּרֵיהֶ֤ם בַּחֶ֙רֶב֙ בְּבֵ֣ית מִקְדָּשָׁ֔ם וְלֹ֥א חָמַ֛ל עַל־בָּח֥וּר וּבְתוּלָ֖ה זָקֵ֣ן וְיָשֵׁ֑שׁ הַכֹּ֖ל נָתַ֥ן בְּיָדֽוֹ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So he brought upon them the king of the Kasdim, who slew their young men with the sword in the house of their sanctuary, and had no compassion either upon young men, or virgins, old men, or feeble: he gave them all into his hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He brought up against them the king of the Kasdim, and He slew their youths by the sword in their Temple, and He did not compassionate youth or virgin, elder or ancient — He delivered all into his hand.


###### II Chronicles 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:18)


וְ֠כֹ֠ל כְּלֵ֞י בֵּ֤ית הָאֱלֹהִים֙ הַגְּדֹלִ֣ים וְהַקְּטַנִּ֔ים וְאֹֽצְרוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאֹצְר֥וֹת הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וְשָׂרָ֑יו הַכֹּ֖ל הֵבִ֥יא בָבֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the vessels of the house of God, great and small, and the treasures of the house of the Lord, and the treasures of the king, and of his princes; all these he brought to Bavel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the vessels of the house of the L rd, the large and the small, and the treasures of the house of the L rd, and the treasures of the king and his officers — he brought all to Bavel.


###### II Chronicles 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:19)


וַֽיִּשְׂרְפוּ֙ אֶת־בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וַֽיְנַתְּצ֔וּ אֵ֖ת חוֹמַ֣ת יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְכׇל־אַרְמְנוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ שָׂרְפ֣וּ בָאֵ֔שׁ וְכׇל־כְּלֵ֥י מַחֲמַדֶּ֖יהָ לְהַשְׁחִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they burnt the house of God, and broke down the wall of Yerushalayim, and burnt all its palaces with fire, and destroyed all its choice vessels.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they burned the house of G d, and they razed the wall of Jerusalem, and they burned all its palaces in fire, and all its precious vessels they destroyed.


###### II Chronicles 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:20)


וַיֶּ֛גֶל הַשְּׁאֵרִ֥ית מִן־הַחֶ֖רֶב אֶל־בָּבֶ֑ל וַיִּֽהְיוּ־ל֤וֹ וּלְבָנָיו֙ לַעֲבָדִ֔ים עַד־מְלֹ֖ךְ מַלְכ֥וּת פָּרָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And those who had escaped from the sword he carried away into exile to Bavel; where they were servants to him and his sons until the reign of the kingdom of Paras:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he exiled the survivors of the sword to Bavel, and they were slaves to him and to his sons until the reign of the kingdom of Paras.


###### II Chronicles 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:21)


לְמַלֹּ֤אות דְּבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ בְּפִ֣י יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ עַד־רָצְתָ֥ה הָאָ֖רֶץ אֶת־שַׁבְּתוֹתֶ֑יהָ כׇּל־יְמֵ֤י הׇשַּׁמָּה֙ שָׁבָ֔תָה לְמַלֹּ֖אות שִׁבְעִ֥ים שָׁנָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to fulfil the word of the Lord by the mouth of Yirmeyahu, until the land had made good her sabbaths: for as long as she lay desolate she kept sabbath, to fulfil seventy years.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To fulfill the word of the L rd in the mouth of Jeremiah [viz. Jeremiah 29:10], until the land requited its Sabbaths. All the days of its desolation it rested, to complete seventy years [viz. Leviticus 25:18, 26:34 and 35, and Ezekiel 4].


###### II Chronicles 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:22)


וּבִשְׁנַ֣ת אַחַ֗ת לְכ֙וֹרֶשׁ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ פָּרַ֔ס לִכְל֥וֹת דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּפִ֣י יִרְמְיָ֑הוּ הֵעִ֣יר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶת־ר֙וּחַ֙ כּ֣וֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ־פָּרַ֔ס וַיַּֽעֲבֶר־קוֹל֙ בְּכׇל־מַלְכוּת֔וֹ וְגַם־בְּמִכְתָּ֖ב לֵאמֹֽר׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now in the first year of Koresh king of Paras, so that the word of the Lord spoken by the mouth of Yirmeyahu might be accomplished, the Lord stirred up the spirit of Koresh king of Paras, that he made a proclamation throughout all of his kingdom, and put it also in writing, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in the first year of Koresh, king of Paras, at the consummation of the word of Jeremiah, the L rd roused the spirit of Koresh, king of Paras, and he proclaimed throughout his kingdom, and [he issued the proclamation] in writing, too:


###### II Chronicles 36:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Chronicles/r/36:23)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר כּ֣וֹרֶשׁ**׀** מֶ֣לֶךְ פָּרַ֗ס כׇּל־מַמְלְכ֤וֹת הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ נָ֣תַן לִ֗י יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְהֽוּא־פָקַ֤ד עָלַי֙ לִבְנֽוֹת־ל֣וֹ בַ֔יִת בִּירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם אֲשֶׁ֣ר בִּיהוּדָ֑ה מִי־בָכֶ֣ם מִכׇּל־עַמּ֗וֹ יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהָ֛יו עִמּ֖וֹ וְיָֽעַל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus says Koresh king of Paras, The Lord God of heaven has given me all the kingdoms of the earth; and he has charged me to build him a house in Yerushalayim, which is in Yehuda. Whoever is among you of all his people – the Lord his God be with him, and let him go up!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Thus says Koresh, king of Paras: All the kingdoms of the earth have been given me by the L rd, the G d of the heavens. And He has commanded me to build Him a house in Jerusalem, which is in Judah. Whoever of you is [(one)] of all His people, may the L rd his G d be with him, and let him go up!"
